### [1] [Eidola: Modeling Multi-GPU Network Communication Traffic in Distributed AI Workloads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12638)
*Ranganath R. Selagamsetty,Matthew Poremba,Bradford M. Beckmann,Joshua San Miguel,Mikko H. Lipasti*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了Eidola，一个基于gem5的可扩展仿真框架扩展，通过使用精简的GPU模型和真实应用的时序配置文件，实现对大规模多GPU系统中复杂通信流量的周期级精确建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着分布式AI工作负载规模的增长，内核融合与通信计算重叠等技术虽然降低了延迟，但引入了难以用现有工具建模的不规则瞬态流量模式，且对互连带宽和延迟提出了严峻挑战。

Method: 将GPU抽象为精简的“eidolon”模型，仅模拟流量建模所需的最小特征；利用真实应用的标注时序配置文件，以周期级精度模拟点对点GPU写入行为，并支持可配置的每GPU流量模式。

Result: 成功复现了融合内核执行的可变性，实现了SyncMon同步机制并证实了轮询相关内存流量的减少，证明了该工具能灵活、可扩展地模拟大规模多GPU配置下的同步行为。

Conclusion: Eidola为研究GPU间通信提供了一个灵活且可扩展的平台，有效支持现代分布式GPU系统的架构探索与性能分析。

Abstract: As distributed AI workloads grow in scale, multi-GPU systems have become essential for training large models. Although techniques like kernel fusion and overlapping communication with computation help reduce delays, they also introduce irregular and transient traffic patterns that are difficult to model using existing tools. These techniques rely heavily on fine-grained synchronization and peer-to-peer communication, which place significant pressure on interconnect bandwidth and latency.
  In this work, we introduce Eidola, a scalable extension to the gem5 simulation framework that enables detailed modeling of inter-GPU communication traffic. The extension is scalable as our GPU model serves as a succinct eidolon, emulating the minimal characteristics needed for traffic modeling. Eidola uses annotated timing profiles from real applications to emulate peer-to-peer GPU writes with cycle-level precision. This allows researchers to simulate and analyze synchronization behavior across large multi-GPU configurations. The simulator supports configurable per-GPU traffic patterns and enables isolated performance analysis under different communication scenarios.
  We demonstrate Eidola's effectiveness by reproducing variability in fused kernel execution and by implementing a SyncMon-inspired synchronization mechanism, confirming reductions in polling-related memory traffic. Our results show that Eidola provides a flexible and scalable platform for studying inter-GPU communication and supports architectural exploration in modern distributed GPU systems.

</details>


### [2] [ITME: Inference Tiered Memory Expansion with Disaggregated CXL-Hybrid Memories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12556)
*Hakbeom Jang,Younghoon Min,Sunwoong Kim,Taeyoung Ahn,Hanyee Kim,Youngpyo Joo,Hoshik Kim,Jongryool Kim*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出ITME系统，利用CXL混合内存技术为LLM提供TB级可字节寻址的远程内存扩展，有效解决共享上下文基础设施的扩展挑战，实现最高35.7%的吞吐量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型向代理型和长上下文工作负载快速发展，TB级上下文状态已超出单服务器容量，推动行业向分布式共享存储架构演进。虽然基于JBOF架构的DPU卸载方案可加速NVMe-oF目标处理，但仍面临软件级优化复杂、成本效率负担重等问题，共享上下文基础设施的理想扩展架构仍在探索中。

Method: 提出ITME（推理分层内存扩展）系统，利用CXL混合内存技术提供TB级可字节寻址的远程内存扩展。通过直接字节寻址能力简化软件栈，并基于模型权重和前缀缓存的确定性访问模式，主动管理跨内存-存储层次结构的数据移动。系统采用生产级SK海力士CMM模块和PCIe Gen5 NVMe SSD进行性能评估，并通过FPGA硬件原型验证功能可行性。

Result: ITME通过提供额外远程内存扩展有效容纳超出主机内存限制的大型KV缓存占用，在传统CPU卸载基础上实现最高35.7%的吞吐量提升，同时实现成本高效的扩展能力。

Conclusion: ITME成功解决了共享上下文基础设施的扩展挑战，证明了CXL混合内存在LLM推理场景中的有效性，为大规模上下文状态管理提供了可行的架构方案。

Abstract: The rapid shift toward agentic and long-context workloads in Large Language Models (LLMs) is pushing the industry beyond the capacity of individual servers toward disaggregated shared storage to handle TB-scale context states. This movement has led to the emergence of specialized shared context layers designed to externalize and share cumulative inference states across distributed clusters. While offloading to a data processing unit (DPU) within just-a-bunch-of-flash (JBOF) architectures accelerates NVMe-over-fabrics (NVMe-oF) target processing, the need for sophisticated software-level optimization and cost-efficiency burdens remain significant. Consequently, the ideal architecture for scaling this shared context infrastructure is still an active area of exploration. In this paper, we propose ITME (Inference Tiered Memory Expansion), which leverages a CXL-hybrid memory to present a massive, TB-scale byte-addressable remote memory expansion. This approach enables cost-efficient scaling and simplifies the software stack through direct byte-addressability, effectively addressing the challenges of shared context infrastructure. Our key insight is that the deterministic access patterns of voluminous model weights and prefix caches enable the system to proactively manage data movement across the memory-storage hierarchy. We validate ITME by evaluating its performance potential with production-grade SK Hynix CMM and PCIe Gen5 NVMe SSDs, while further demonstrating its functional feasibility through an FPGA-based hardware prototype. Overall, ITME enhances conventional CPU-offloading by providing additional remote memory expansion to accommodate large KV cache footprints beyond host memory limits, achieving up to a 35.7\% throughput improvement.

</details>


### [3] [On the Limits of Performance Portability in Directive-Based GPU Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12753)
*Alessandro Romeo,Nitin Shukla,Stefano Truzzi,Alessio Suriano,Andrea Mignone*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文通过将生产级天体物理磁流体动力学代码gPLUTO从OpenACC移植到OpenMP，评估了基于指令的GPU编程的性能可移植性。结果显示，在NVIDIA A100上两者性能相当，但在AMD MI250X上OpenMP实现的应用级性能慢约3倍，内核级 slowdowns高达一个数量级。研究表明，跨GPU架构的可移植性能需要编译器后端改进和架构感知的优化策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学应用向GPU加速的Exascale系统过渡受到性能、可移植性和生产力之间权衡的制约。本研究旨在评估基于指令的GPU编程在异构GPU平台（NVIDIA和AMD）上的性能可移植性，为科学计算应用的跨平台优化提供参考。

Method: 将生产级磁流体动力学代码gPLUTO从OpenACC移植到OpenMP，在NVIDIA A100（Leonardo Booster）和AMD MI250X（LUMI-G）设备上进行性能对比分析。通过内核级性能剖析，识别运行时间的主要贡献因素，包括内存访问模式、编译器限制和寄存器压力等因素的影响。

Result: 在NVIDIA平台上，OpenACC和OpenMP因共享编译器后端而达到可比性能。在AMD MI250X上，相同OpenMP实现的应用级性能比NVIDIA A100 OpenACC基准慢约3倍，内核级 slowdowns最高达一个数量级。性能瓶颈主要由内存延迟约束而非峰值带宽限制引起。低并行度内核中，C++抽象层增加寄存器压力和溢出，导致特定情况下高达47倍的极端 slowdowns。

Conclusion: 跨GPU架构的可移植性能不仅需要应用层面的修改，还需要编译器后端的持续改进和架构感知的优化策略。基于指令的GPU编程在异构平台间的性能可移植性仍面临重大挑战，特别是内存访问模式和编译器优化能力的差异。

Abstract: The transition of scientific applications to GPU-accelerated exascale systems is constrained by trade-offs between performance, portability, and productivity. This work evaluates the performance portability of directive-based GPU programming by porting gPLUTO, a production-grade magnetohydrodynamics code for astrophysical simulations, from OpenACC to OpenMP, and analyzing its performance on NVIDIA A100 (Leonardo Booster) and AMD MI250X (LUMI-G) devices. On NVIDIA platforms, OpenACC and OpenMP achieve comparable performance due to a shared compiler backend, providing a consistent baseline for assessing algorithmic efficiency. In contrast, the same OpenMP implementation is approximately three times slower at the application level on AMD MI250X with respect to the NVIDIA A100 OpenACC baseline, with kernel-level slowdowns reaching up to an order of magnitude, driven by sensitivity to strided memory-access patterns and compiler limitations. Kernel-level profiling shows that the dominant contributors to run-time are memory-latency-bound rather than limited by peak band-width. In low-parallelism kernels, C++ abstraction layers increase register pressure and spilling, leading to extreme slowdowns of up to 47x in specific cases. These results indicate that portable performance across GPU architectures requires not only application-level changes but also continued advances in compiler backends and architecture-aware optimization strategies

</details>


### [4] [SemanticXR: Low Power and Real-time Queryable Semantic Mapping with an Object-Level Device-Cloud Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12849)
*Rahul Singh,Devdeep Ray,Connor Smith,Sarita Adve*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: SemanticXR是首个满足XR设备实时、开放词汇语义映射需求的端云系统，通过对象级优化实现了低延迟、低带宽和低功耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语义映射方法计算密集，假设服务器级资源，无法满足移动XR设备对开放词汇、实时和低功耗的需求。虽有云卸载路径，但现有系统未能在设备-云端边界划分语义映射或管理其通信、执行和内存占用。

Method: 将语义可识别对象提升为端云间通信、执行和内存的一等单元。服务器端采用对象级并行和几何下采样改善映射延迟，对象级深度映射协同设计减少上行带宽。设备端采用对象级稀疏局部地图，通过增量更新和更新优先级实现网络鲁棒查询，并有界内存和下行带宽。对象级可配置资源与质量权衡支持应用和系统自适应映射。

Result: 与同感知模型的端云基线相比，对象级组织在相同语义质量下将服务器端映射延迟提高2.2倍；深度映射协同设计使上行带宽保持在2.5 Mbps以下；设备端在最多1万个对象下保持低于100毫秒的查询延迟，即使在网络丢包时也能支持数万个对象在500 MB内存内，下行带宽随地图变化而非场景总规模扩展；系统正常工作时仅增加2%的设备功耗。

Conclusion: SemanticXR成功实现了在XR设备上部署开放词汇、实时、低功耗语义映射的目标，通过对象级系统设计有效平衡了性能与资源约束，为XR应用提供了实用的语义映射解决方案。

Abstract: Semantic mapping is a core service that enables grounded interactions in emerging Extended Reality (XR) applications such as AI assistants and spatial object search. Deploying this capability on mobile XR devices requires a system that is open-vocabulary, real-time, and low-power. Existing approaches are compute-intensive and assume server-class resources. Cloud offloading offers a practical path, but no existing system splits semantic mapping across the device-cloud boundary or manages its communication, execution, and memory footprint.
  We present SemanticXR, the first device-cloud system for real-time, open-vocabulary semantic mapping and querying under XR power, bandwidth, and memory constraints. Our key insight is to elevate semantically identifiable objects to first-class units of communication, execution, and memory across the device and server. On the server, object-level parallelism and geometry downsampling improve mapping latency, while object-level depth-mapping co-design reduces upstream bandwidth. On the device, an object-level sparse local map with incremental updates and update prioritization enables network-robust querying with bounded memory and downstream bandwidth. Object-level configurable resource usage vs. quality trade-offs let applications and the system adapt mapping to application requirements and operating conditions, respectively.
  Against a device-cloud baseline with the same perception models, object-level organization improves server-side mapping latency by 2.2X at equal semantic quality. Depth-mapping co-design maintains upstream bandwidth under 2.5 Mbps. On the device, SemanticXR sustains sub-100 ms query latency for up to 10,000 objects even under network drops, supports tens of thousands of objects within 500 MB, and scales downstream bandwidth with map changes, not total scene size. The system adds only 2% device power during normal operation.

</details>


### [5] [High-Order Spectral Element Methods for Wave Propagation on ARM Multicore CPU with SME: Optimizations and Implications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12850)
*Yinuo Wang,Lin Gan,Tianqi Mao,Wubing Wan,Zekun Yin,Wenqiang Wang,Wei Xue,Guangwen Yang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文针对ARM处理器的新型SME扩展，优化了谱元法波传播模拟器SPECFEM3D，实现了4-6倍性能提升，并发现SME改变了最佳离散化参数选择策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于谱元法(SEM)的波传播模拟是典型的高性能计算负载，但现有SEM实现无法充分利用新兴ARM多核CPU上的可扩展矩阵扩展(SME)特性，需要针对SME架构特点进行专门优化。

Method: 提出了三方面的优化方案：1) 针对SEM张量积算子开发了SME感知的分批小矩阵核函数；2) 针对有限HBM内存系统设计了内存感知的MPI+OpenMP混合执行方案；3) 基于色散分析的等精度研究探索(h,p)离散化参数权衡。

Result: 在固定多项式阶数条件下，优化实现相比原始代码获得4-6倍的全应用性能提升，并显著优于优化的非SME CPU基线。更重要的是，SME将性能优势操作点推向更高多项式阶数，进一步降低求解时间和工作集大小。

Conclusion: SME扩展不仅影响核函数效率，还改变了现代ARM多核平台上SEM方法的实用离散化权衡策略，为SEM在新兴架构上的优化提供了新的设计指导。

Abstract: Wave propagation based on the spectral element method (SEM) is a representative HPC workload, but existing SEM implementations are not well matched to emerging ARM multicore CPUs with Scalable Matrix Extension (SME). We present an SME-enabled optimization of \textsc{SPECFEM3D} on the emerging LX2 processor that combines an SME-aware batched small-matrix kernel for SEM tensor-product operators, a memory-aware hybrid MPI+OpenMP execution scheme for limited-HBM systems, and a dispersion-based iso-accuracy study of the $(h,p)$ tradeoff. At fixed polynomial order, the optimized implementation improves full-application performance by 4--6$\times$ over the original code and delivers clear gains over optimized non-SME CPU baselines. Beyond these implementation-level gains, our results suggest that SME shifts the performance-favorable operating point toward higher polynomial orders along the dispersion-based iso-accuracy frontier, further reducing time-to-solution and working-set size. These results indicate that SME affects not only kernel efficiency, but also the practical discretization tradeoff for SEM on modern ARM multicore platforms.

</details>


### [6] [Maestro: Workload-Aware Cross-Cluster Scheduling for LLM-Based Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12950)
*Jinghao Wang,Xiao Zhou,Xiaoyang Sun,Yihui Zhang,Yilong Li,Tianyu Wo,Xu Wang,Chunming Hu,Renyu Yang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了Maestro，一个面向LLM多智能体系统的负载感知调度系统，通过利用智能体语义和角色预测，在节点、集群和全局三个层次进行优化，显著降低了GPU资源消耗并提升了服务质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于大语言模型的多智能体系统（LLM-MAS）通过将复杂任务分解为协作工作流展现出强大能力，但在规模化部署时面临严峻的系统挑战：每次用户查询会引发迭代式的LLM调用流水线，相比单轮查询大幅放大资源消耗；在资源受限的云环境中，这些工作流面临解码阶段的非确定性和输入相关成本、具有内存碎片化和过度配置问题的重尾多模型需求，以及跨集群调度权衡等问题。

Method: Maestro是一个针对GPU预算约束下LLM-MAS服务的负载感知调度系统。其核心方法是显式利用智能体语义和角色：预测每个阶段的输出长度和内存使用，并基于此预测驱动分层调度器。在节点层面，通过分层权重缓存和弹性内存供给实现动态多模型共置；在集群层面，执行延迟感知路由以避免冷启动延迟和内存过载；在全局层面，实施工作流感知优先级排序以最小化交互任务的头-of-line阻塞。

Result: 通过原型实验和跟踪驱动模拟，Maestro将KV保留的HBM使用减少了67.2%，并在高竞争场景下相比EDF（最早截止时间优先）算法将SLO（服务级别目标）达成率提升了23.6个百分点。

Conclusion: Maestro成功解决了LLM多智能体系统在资源受限环境下的规模化部署挑战，通过智能体语义驱动的分层调度策略，在显著降低GPU内存占用的同时提升了服务质量，为LLM-MAS的高效服务提供了有效的系统解决方案。

Abstract: Large Language Model based Multi-Agent Systems (LLM-MAS) have emerged as a powerful paradigm for tackling complex tasks by breaking them into collaborative workflows of specialized LLM-powered agents. However, deploying such multi-agent workloads at scale poses significant system challenges. Each user query spawns an iterative pipeline of LLM calls, greatly amplifying resource consumption compared to single-turn queries. In resource-constrained cloud settings, these workflows face non-deterministic and input-dependent costs at decode stage, heavy-tailed multi-model requirements with memory fragmentation and over-provisioning, and cross-cluster scheduling trade-offs. We present Maestro, a workload-aware scheduling system designed for LLM-MAS serving under strict GPU budgets. Maestro explicitly leverages agent semantics and roles: it predicts the output length and memory usage of each stage and uses this prediction to drive a hierarchical scheduler. At the node level, Maestro enables dynamic multi-model co-location via hierarchical weight caching and elastic memory provisioning. At the cluster level, it performs latency-aware routing to avoid cold-start delays and memory overloads. At the global level, it enforces workflow-aware prioritization to minimize head-of-line blocking for interactive tasks. Across prototype experiments and trace-driven simulations, Maestro reduces KV-reservation HBM by 67.2% and improves high-contention SLO attainment over EDF by 23.6 percentage points.

</details>


### [7] [JiRAIYA: A Reputation-Based Hierarchical Federated Learning Framework on Web3](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13180)
*Venkata Raghava Kurada,Pallav Kumar Baruah*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于Web3技术的联邦学习框架，通过分层架构、共识机制和声誉系统来提升透明度、可信度和可审计性，有效抵御对抗攻击和投毒攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的联邦学习系统主要部署在企业环境中，存在透明度有限和可审计性受限的问题。聚合过程不透明，导致不清楚哪些模型更新被接受或丢弃。当前的缓解策略通常依赖外部验证者，引入了额外的计算和通信开销。

Method: 提出了一种新颖的联邦学习框架，利用现有Web3技术来增强训练过程的透明度和可审计性。该框架采用分层架构，由委托管理者在其各自的联盟内协调FL训练过程。为了缓解对抗性和投毒攻击，采用了新颖性检测和共识机制的组合。模型更新被编码并广播给所有管理者，管理者独立评估其有效性，通过共识批准的模型更新被纳入全局模型。此外，还采用了基于声誉分数的备份机制来确保模型生成。

Result: 在真实场景下进行的广泛实验证明了所提框架的有效性和韧性，突出了其在传统企业环境之外实现透明联邦学习的潜力。

Conclusion: 该框架成功利用Web3技术解决了联邦学习中的透明度和信任问题，通过分层架构和共识机制实现了可审计的联邦学习过程，为联邦学习在更广泛场景中的应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Federated Learning(FL) is predominantly deployed in enterprise environments, where limited transparency and restricted auditability hinder broader adoption. Existing FL systems often suffer from opaque aggregation processes, making it unclear which model updates are accepted or discarded. Current mitigation strategies typically rely on external validators introducing additional computational and communication overhead. In this paper, we propose a novel FL framework that leverages existing Web3 technologies to enhance transparency, trust and auditability throughout the training process. The framework adopts a hierarchical architecture in which delegated managers orchestrate the FL training process within their respective federations. To mitigate adversarial and poisoning attacks, a combination of novelty detection and consensus mechanisms were employed. Model updates are encoded and broad casted to all managers, who independently evaluate their validity and those model updates that are approved by the consensus are incorporated into the global model. Additionally, a reputation score based backup mechanism is employed to ensure model generation. Extensive experiments conducted under real world scenarios demonstrate the effectiveness, resilience of the proposed framework, highlighting its potential to enable transparent FL beyond traditional enterprise setting.

</details>


### [8] [Dynamic Resource Management in Production HPC Clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13266)
*Petter Sandås,Sergio Iserte,Guillaume Houzeaux,Antonio J. Peña*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种实用的MPI可塑性方法论，在无需侵入式代码修改的情况下，在生产环境中实现了动态资源管理，显著降低了节点小时消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模科学应用程序通常表现出时变行为，但生产HPC集群仍依赖固定的资源分配，大多数动态技术仅限于实验室原型，缺乏在生产环境中的实际应用方案。

Method: 提出了一种与现有HPC软件栈和操作实践集成的MPI可塑性方法论，在DMR（Dynamic Management of Resources）框架中实现。该方案设计为非侵入式，无需应用程序进行侵入式代码修改或调度器修改，通过DMR API集成到现有应用中。

Result: 在三个TOP500超级计算机上部署并评估了两个大规模科学应用程序，在受控环境中性能与静态基线相当，同时在相同工作负载下显著减少了节点小时消耗。

Conclusion: 可塑性可以在使用普通资源管理器的生产系统上有效利用，降低了HPC中动态资源管理采用的门槛，证明了动态资源管理在生产环境中的可行性。

Abstract: Many large-scale scientific applications exhibit time-varying behavior, yet production HPC clusters still rely on rigid, fixed-size allocations, and most dynamic techniques remain confined to laboratory prototypes. This work presents a practical MPI malleability methodology that integrates with state-of-the-art high-performance computing (HPC) software stacks and operational practices. The methodology is implemented in the Dynamic Management of Resources (DMR) framework and is designed to ease adoption by existing applications without requiring intrusive code changes or scheduler modifications. We evaluate our approach by integrating the DMR API into two large-scale scientific applications and deploying them on three TOP500 supercomputers under realistic production configurations. Our non-invasive malleability solution achieves performance comparable to static baselines in controlled environments while substantially reducing node-hour consumption for identical workloads. These results show that malleability can be effectively exploited on production systems using vanilla resource managers, lowering the barrier to adoption of dynamic resource management in HPC.

</details>

Abstract: A skiplist is a fundamental data structure widely used in systems and applications for indexing data stores. In this work, we introduce Foresight, a cache-friendly skiplist optimization. Extending Foresight to concurrent settings introduces significant synchronization challenges that we identify and address. Foresight is a surgical optimization, easy to integrate into a wide variety of skiplist designs. We apply it to one sequential and three concurrent skiplist designs and observe throughput improvements of up to 45% in microbenchmarks. When applied to a skiplist-based index in the DBx1000 in-memory database, Foresight yields end-to-end performance gains of up to 15%.

</details>


### [10] [Work Stealing for the 2D-Mesh Topology of Satellite Constellations in Low Earth Orbit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13329)
*Mia Reitz,Dorian Chenet,Jonas Posner*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出邻域窃取策略，在稀疏网状拓扑（如低轨卫星集群）中避免多跳通信，理论模型显示其在规模化时具有延迟优势，实验表明负载均衡性能与全局窃取相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有异步多任务（AMT）运行时假设全连接低延迟网络，采用全局工作窃取策略。但空间边缘计算（SEC）中的低轨卫星通过星间链路形成稀疏网状拓扑，远距离通信需多跳传输增加延迟，传统全局窃取策略不适用。

Method: 提出邻域窃取策略，限制工作窃取仅在直接连接的邻居间进行，避免多跳通信。建立分析模型评估该策略的延迟优势，并在HPC集群上通过模拟网状拓扑进行实验验证。

Result: 分析模型表明邻域窃取策略的单次尝试延迟优势随星座规模增长。实验结果显示，在均衡和非均衡工作负载下，邻域窃取策略性能仅比全局窃取策略低约2.2%，表明限制窃取目标集不会损害负载均衡。

Conclusion: 邻域窃取策略在小规模场景下性能与全局窃取相当，在大规模稀疏网状拓扑中由于延迟优势将更具优越性，为AMT在空间边缘计算中的应用奠定了基础。

Abstract: Asynchronous Many-Task (AMT) is a parallel programming model used in High Performance Computing (HPC). An AMT runtime can distribute fine-grained tasks across processing units called workers, through work stealing: when a worker has no tasks left to process, it tries to steal tasks from other workers. Workers are not restricted to a single compute node but can also be distributed across multiple nodes of an HPC cluster. Existing AMT runtimes assume a fully connected network with low, uniform latency and perform global work stealing, selecting another worker at random from all workers in the system.
  Space Edge Computing (SEC) uses constellations of satellites in Low Earth Orbit (LEO) as distributed compute clusters. Unlike HPC clusters, LEO satellites communicate through inter-satellite links that form a sparse mesh topology. Reaching a distant satellite requires multiple hops, each adding latency.
  As a step toward adapting AMT to SEC, this paper proposes a neighbor-only work stealing strategy in which workers steal exclusively from directly connected neighbors, avoiding multi-hop communication. An analytical model shows that restricting stealing this way yields a per-attempt latency advantage that grows with constellation size. Preliminary experiments on an HPC cluster with an emulated mesh over uniform low-latency links isolate the effect of victim selection: the neighbor-only strategy performs within ~2.2% of global stealing on both balanced and irregular workloads, indicating that restricting the victim set does not harm load balancing in this setting. Taken together, the experiments suggest that neighbor-only stealing can be on a par with global stealing, and the model suggests that neighbor-only stealing becomes preferable at scale.

</details>


### [11] [Temporal Conductance and Bounds on the Voter Model for Dynamic Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13374)
*Tatiana Rocha Avila,Holger Dell,John Lapinskas*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: The voter model is a classical stochastic process that models how opinions might spread through a network: at each step, every node lazily adopts the opinion of a random neighbour; eventually all nodes share the same opinion (consensus). Stronger connectivity should yield faster consensus. Berenbrink, Giakkoupis, Kermarrec, and Mallmann-Trenn (ICALP 2016) make this precise via the network's conductance: if the network has $m$ edges, minimum degree $d_{\min}$, and conductance at least $φ$, then the voter model reaches consensus in expected $O(m/(d_{\min}φ))$ steps. Their results extend to dynamic networks with fixed vertex degrees by considering the network's conductance at each time step.
  We introduce temporal conductance $Φ$, a more general connectivity measure for dynamic networks. Unlike static conductance, which collapses to $0$ whenever some snapshot is disconnected, $Φ$ captures connectivity through edges that appear at different times. We generalise the results of Berenbrink et al. from static conductance to temporal conductance, showing that the expected consensus time of the standard voter model is at most $O(m/(d_{\min}Φ))$. Moreover, we prove that this bound is tight up to constant factors. We expect temporal conductance to be a useful primitive for analysing other dynamics on temporal networks, and potentially time-inhomogeneous Markov chains more generally.

</details>


### [12] [GF-DiT: Scheduling Parallelism for Diffusion Transformer Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13501)
*Xinwei Qiang,Yifan Hu,Shixuan Sun,Jing Yang,Han Zhao,Chen Chen,Yu Feng,Jingwen Leng,Minyi Guo*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出GF-DiT，一种支持弹性并行度的DiT服务运行时，通过动态调整GPU并行配置，相比静态并行方案显著提升吞吐量和延迟性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DiT服务系统采用静态并行配置，无法适应请求间、执行阶段和系统条件的异构性，导致GPU利用率低、服务质量下降。作者认为应将GPU并行度视为一等可调度资源。

Method: 提出GF-DiT运行时系统：1) 异步执行抽象，将请求分解为独立可调度的轨迹任务，支持在线GPU重分配；2) 组无关集合通信（group-free collectives），轻量级通信抽象，支持任意执行组的低开销在线组建和重配置。在vLLM-Omni中实现。

Result: 在图像和视频扩散工作负载上评估：相比静态并行方案，吞吐量提升最高6.01倍，平均延迟降低最高95%，SLO违规率降低最高90%，通信组建立开销从778ms降至约60μs。

Conclusion: 通过将GPU并行度作为动态可调度资源，GF-DiT实现了高效的弹性DiT服务，证明了动态并行调整对异构DiT工作负载的重要性。

Abstract: Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) have become the dominant architecture for image and video generation, creating growing demand for efficient DiT serving. Existing systems assign each request a fixed parallel configuration throughout its lifetime. However, DiT workloads exhibit substantial heterogeneity across requests, execution stages, and system conditions, making static parallelism inefficient and often leading to poor GPU utilization and degraded service quality.
  This paper argues that DiT serving should treat GPU parallelism as a first-class schedulable resource. We present GF-DiT, a policy-programmable runtime for elastic DiT serving that dynamically adapts the parallelism of running requests according to workload demands and service objectives. GF-DiT introduces an asynchronous execution abstraction that decomposes requests into independently schedulable trajectory tasks and enables online GPU reallocation. To make elastic parallelism practical, GF-DiT further proposes group-free collectives, a lightweight communication abstraction that supports low-overhead online formation and reconfiguration of arbitrary execution groups.
  We implement GF-DiT in vLLM-Omni and evaluate it on representative image and video diffusion workloads. Compared with fixed-pipeline execution with static parallelism, GF-DiT improves throughput by up to 6.01$\times$, reduces mean latency by up to 95%, lowers SLO violation rates by up to 90%, and reduces communication-group setup overhead from 778 ms to approximately 60 $μ$s.

</details>


### [13] [Finding Conservation Laws of Large Dynamical Systems with Tasks and Futures: A Case Study in Utilizing Dynamic Data Dependencies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13623)
*Rüdiger Nather*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 论文引入await_delete结构扩展future语义以支持安全值重用，并提出了基于future的分块对称矩阵求逆算法，实验证明在大规模问题上future能实现近线性扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着并行工作负载复杂度增加，细粒度数据依赖管理成为关键挑战。Futures虽然能有效处理不规则算法中的依赖关系，但其值不可变特性限制了内存原地更新，而这对于需要内存回收的高性能线性代数至关重要。

Method: 引入新的await_delete结构，扩展现有future语义以允许消费者完成后安全重用值；基于此扩展，提出了一种新颖的基于future的密集对称矩阵分块求逆算法；在Taskflow扩展版本中实现该方案并通过强扩展实验进行评估。

Result: 实验结果表明，futures在较小问题规模上会产生显著开销，但在大规模矩阵上能够实现近线性扩展。论文分析了摊销阈值，证明futures是大规模线性代数的可行高性能工具。

Conclusion: 通过扩展future语义支持安全值重用，futures可以成为大规模线性代数计算的有效高性能工具，关键在于达到问题规模阈值后开销能够被有效摊销。

Abstract: As parallel workloads grow in complexity, managing fine-grained data dependencies becomes a critical challenge. Futures offer a promising model for handling these dependencies, particularly in irregular algorithms, but they also come with the restriction of value-immutability. This immutability limits the ability to perform in-place memory updates, a necessity for high-performance linear algebra where memory recycling is paramount.
  In this paper, we address these limitations by introducing a new construct, await_delete, which extends traditional future semantics to allow safe value reuse once consumers are finished. Building on this extension, we present a novel future-based algorithm for the block-wise inversion of dense, symmetric matrices, motivated by a recent algorithm for finding conservation laws of dynamical systems.
  We implement our approach in an extended version of Taskflow and evaluate it through strong-scaling experiments. Our results demonstrate that while futures incur significant overhead on smaller problem sizes, they achieve nearly linear scaling on large matrices. We analyze the amortization threshold and show that futures are a viable high-performance tool for large-scale linear algebra.

</details>

### [14] [Quickest Detection of Hallucination Onset: Delay Bounds and Learned CUSUM Statistics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12476)
*Igor Itkin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将大语言模型的幻觉检测问题重新定义为“最快变点检测”问题，发现基于分类AUC的评估会掩盖延迟结构。通过理论分析得出Lorden下界约为1.3个token，实验显示因果循环标注器的检测延迟为11-13个token，远优于基线的31个token，但与理论最优仍有约10倍的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有token级幻觉检测器通常使用AUC等分类指标进行评估，但对于流式监控场景，评估的核心应是“检测延迟”——即从幻觉出现到发出警报之间经过的token数量。传统分类指标掩盖了检测延迟的结构特征，无法真实反映监控器的实际性能。作者希望引入序贯分析理论来更好地理解和优化幻觉检测的延迟性能。

Method: 1. 将幻觉起始检测形式化为经典的最快变点检测问题；
2. 在RAGTruth数据集上验证了一阶马尔可夫模型对潜在忠实/幻觉状态的建模；
3. 基于Lorden理论推导检测延迟的理论下界；
4. 证明因果循环标注器等价于具有学习增量的CUSUM算法；
5. 设计对照实验分解检测优势的来源（per-token评分质量 vs 时序累积）；
6. 利用Donsker-Varadhan型信息率最优性定理分析理论最优与实际性能的差距。

Result: 1. 理论下界：在虚警率0.01条件下，Lorden下界约为1.3个token；
2. 实验表现：因果循环标注器在相同虚警率下检测延迟为11-13个token，而线性per-token基线为31个token；
3. 优势分解：检测优势主要来自更好的per-token评分质量，而非时序累积策略；
4. 性能差距：学习到的评分仅实现了特征所携带信息的1/4.5，剩余差距主要来自有限视界效应，且重校准无法消除这一缺陷。

Conclusion: 传统分类评估指标会掩盖幻觉检测器的延迟结构特征。通过序贯分析视角，本文揭示了检测延迟的理论下界，证明了现有方法与最优性能之间约一个数量级的差距，并指出这一差距源于学习评分的信息利用不足和有限视界效应。这为未来研究提供了清晰的理论框架和改进方向。

Abstract: Token-level hallucination detectors are evaluated as classifiers, by AUC over all tokens, yet a streaming monitor is judged by its reaction time: the number of tokens that pass between the onset of a hallucination and the alarm. We formulate hallucination onset detection as a quickest change detection problem. A first-order Markov model of the latent faithful/hallucinated state, validated on RAGTruth, places the task inside classical change-point theory and yields Lorden's lower bound on detection delay: about 1.3 tokens at a false-alarm rate of 0.01. We then show that a causal recurrent labeler acts as a CUSUM with a learned increment; at a matched false-alarm rate it detects in 11-13 tokens, against 31 for a linear per-token baseline, and a controlled decomposition attributes most of this advantage to a better per-token score rather than to temporal accumulation. An information-rate optimality theorem of Donsker-Varadhan type explains the remaining order-of-magnitude gap: the learned score realizes only 1/4.5 of the divergence the features carry, a deficit that recalibration cannot remove, with the remainder a finite-horizon effect. Classification metrics conceal this delay structure; sequential analysis makes it measurable

</details>


### [15] [Boltzmann Attention: Learnable Ising Couplings for Cooperative Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12478)
*Gilhan Kim,Daniel K. Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出玻尔兹曼注意力机制，通过引入可学习的成对耦合项（基于Ising模型）来显式建模注意力位置间的交互，在字符级语言建模和合成任务中优于标准softmax注意力，尤其适用于长序列，并展示了量子退火训练的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准注意力机制主要通过独立的查询-键相似度计算相关性，虽然softmax归一化引入了位置间的竞争，但缺乏显式可学习的注意力决策间交互参数，限制了其对注意力机制内部的协作或对抗性共注意力结构的建模能力。

Method: 提出玻尔兹曼注意力，一种基于能量的注意力泛化方法，将注意力模式建模为由交互Ising模型控制。在常规数据依赖的局域场上增加可学习的成对耦合项，使模型能表示超越softmax或sigmoid注意力的位置间相关性。同时探索了量子退火采样作为训练方法。

Result: 在字符级语言建模和合成括号匹配任务上，玻尔兹曼注意力在标准Transformer架构中一致优于标准softmax注意力，且随着序列长度增加优势更加明显。四路消融实验证实改进源于可学习的成对耦合项。绝热量子退火训练方法能达到与精确玻尔兹曼计算相当的性能。

Conclusion: 显式的位置间交互为基于注意力的序列建模提供了原则性增强。Ising形式不仅提升了性能，还为基于量子计算的采样策略开辟了自然路径，展示了量子机器学习在实际应用中的潜力。

Abstract: Attention mechanisms are central to modern sequence models, yet standard attention computes relevance primarily through individual query--key similarities. Although softmax normalization introduces competition among positions, a standard attention layer does not explicitly parameterize learnable interactions between attention decisions. This limits its ability to directly model cooperative or antagonistic co-attention structure within the attention mechanism itself. We propose Boltzmann attention, an energy-based generalization in which attention patterns are governed by an interacting Ising model. The method augments the usual data-dependent local fields with learnable pairwise couplings, allowing the model to represent inter-position correlations beyond those captured by softmax or sigmoid attention. Experiments on character-level language modeling and synthetic bracket matching show that Boltzmann attention consistently improves over standard softmax attention within a standard Transformer architecture, with the advantage becoming more pronounced as sequence length increases. A four-way ablation confirms that the improvement arises from the learnable pairwise couplings. These results suggest that explicit inter-position interactions provide a principled enhancement for attention-based sequence modeling. Moreover, the Ising formulation opens a natural path toward quantum-computing-based sampling strategies: we demonstrate that diabatic quantum annealing provides a practical training method while maintaining competitive performance with exact Boltzmann computation.

</details>


### [16] [Positional Encoding in the Context of Memristor-Based Analog Computation for Automatic Speech Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13379)
*Benedikt Hilmes,Nick Rossenbach,Ralf Schlüter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对忆阻器神经网络中位置编码导致ADC转换失真问题，通过调整权重比例和ADC精度或移除编码相关线性变换，分别实现约50%和30%的相对性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 忆阻器虽然能够通过模拟方式执行向量矩阵乘法来提高自然语言处理神经模型的资源效率，但在权重编程和执行过程中存在较大的失真问题。作者发现变换后的位置编码产生的大输出值是导致模数转换器(ADC)作为忆阻器计算组成部分出现严重性能退化的主要原因。

Method: 针对ADC失真问题提出两种解决方案：(1) 调整特定忆阻器层的权重比例和ADC精度位数；(2) 在ADC无法修改的情况下，通过移除与编码相关的线性变换来缓解问题。

Result: 通过调整权重比例和ADC精度，在保持预估能耗稳定的前提下，实现了约50%的相对执行退化减少；在ADC无法修改的场景下，通过移除编码相关线性变换实现了约30%的相对退化减少。

Conclusion: 位置编码的大输出值是忆阻器计算中ADC性能退化的关键因素，通过针对性的硬件参数调整或架构修改可以有效缓解这一问题，为忆阻器在自然语言处理中的应用提供了重要的优化思路。

Abstract: Memristors provide a new chance for resource-efficient computation of neural models for natural language processing by enabling analog execution of vector-matrix-multiplication. Yet, computations on these devices are currently subject to larger distortion, both in weight programming and execution. In this work, we identify large output values of transformed positional encodings to cause major degradation within analog-to-digital conversion (ADC) as part of memristor-based computation. By adjusting the proportion of weight and precision bits of the ADC of specific memristor layers, we reduce the degradation of the execution by ~50% relative, while keeping the estimated energy consumption stable. Additionally, we investigate scenarios where the ADC cannot be modified. In that case the degradation can be reduced by ~30% relative after removing encoding-related linear transformations.

</details>


### [17] [ReCal: Reward Calibration for RL-based LLM Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12479)
*Qihang Yu,Hanwen Tong,Zhengqi Zhang,Bo Zheng,Feng Wei,Shengyu Zhang,Zemin Liu,Fei Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了ReCal框架，通过分层奖励分解和分布感知优化策略，解决了强化学习LLM路由中因奖励信号聚合和样本差异导致的优化问题，在七个数据集上均取得了性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的强化学习LLM路由方法面临两个主要问题：(1)多个目标（正确性、格式行为等）被聚合为单一标量奖励，导致信用分配模糊和优化信号冲突；(2)奖励信号在不同实例间存在显著差异性，部分实例产生更高或更具变异性的奖励，引入了偏向简单样本而非信息丰富样本的优化偏差。

Method: 提出ReCal（奖励校准）框架，包含两个核心组件：(1)分层奖励分解机制，结合组件级优势估计；(2)分布感知优化策略，通过方差感知重加权和每数据集归一化来校准优化变异性。

Result: 在七个数据集上的实验表明，ReCal相比基线方法持续改善了路由性能和训练稳定性。

Conclusion: ReCal框架有效解决了强化学习LLM路由中的奖励信号问题，通过分层分解和分布感知校准实现了更稳定的训练和更好的路由性能，为异构任务下的LLM路由提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) routing has emerged as an effective paradigm for leveraging the complementary strengths of multiple LLMs through dynamic model and reasoning-strategy selection. Recent reinforcement learning (RL)-based routing methods further improve routing quality by optimizing routing policies from interaction feedback. However, they still struggle to provide informative and comparable learning signals under heterogeneous tasks with varying difficulty. In practice, multiple objectives (e.g., correctness, format behavior) are aggregated into a single scalar reward, leading to ambiguous credit assignment and conflicting optimization signals. Moreover, reward signals exhibit significant variability across instances, where some instances produce higher or more variable rewards, introducing optimization bias that favors trivial samples over informative ones. To address these issues, we propose \textbf{ReCal}, a \textbf{\underline{Re}}ward \textbf{\underline{Cal}}ibration framework for RL-based LLM routing. We first introduce a hierarchical reward decomposition mechanism with component-wise advantage estimation. We further propose a distribution-aware optimization strategy that calibrates optimization variability through variance-aware reweighting and per-dataset normalization. Experiments on seven datasets demonstrate that ReCal consistently improves routing performance, and training stability over baselines. Code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/ReCal.

</details>


### [18] [Representing Time Series as Structured Programs for LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12481)
*Jaeho Kim,Changhun Oh,Seokhyun Lee,Irina Rish,Changhee Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了T2SP方法，将时间序列转换为结构化符号程序表示，使现成LLM无需训练即可有效推理时间序列，在三项任务上显著提升性能并降低计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将时间序列序列化为原始数值字符串或对LLM进行微调，但这将时序结构提取的重担直接交给模型，导致模态不匹配，在长序列上性能下降且计算开销大。时间序列超出LLM原生文本模态，亟需一种适合LLM推理的表示方法。

Method: 提出Time-Series-to-Structured-Program (T2SP)表示方法：一种确定性、免训练的方法，将时间序列分解为趋势、周期和显著事件，并以结构化符号程序形式表示。该方法将时序结构提取从模型转移到表示本身，使表示与LLM原生训练的文本和代码模态对齐，让现成LLM能直接利用其现有推理能力理解时间序列。

Result: 在编辑、字幕生成和问答三项推理任务上评估，T2SP相比原始字符串表示一致性地提升性能、减少推理时间并降低失败率。验证了T2SP为时间序列与LLM之间提供了有效接口。

Conclusion: T2SP通过将时间序列转换为与LLM原生模态对齐的结构化程序表示，成功解决了模态不匹配问题，为利用现成LLM进行时间序列理解提供了有效且高效的解决方案，避免了昂贵的模型微调。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated strong reasoning and instruction-following capabilities, making them potentially powerful tools for time-series analysis. However, time series lie outside their native textual modality, raising a fundamental question: how should time series be represented so that LLMs can reason about them effectively? Existing work typically serializes raw numerical sequences or fine-tunes pre-trained LLMs on time-series data. These approaches place the burden of extracting temporal structure directly on the LLM, creating a modality mismatch that often degrades performance on long sequences and introduces substantial computational overhead. In this work, we introduce Time-Series-to-Structured-Program representation (T2SP), a deterministic, training-free method that represents a time series as a structured symbolic program. T2SP decomposes time series into trends, periods, and salient events, expressing them in a program-friendly format aligned with the textual and code-like modalities on which LLMs are natively trained. By shifting temporal-structure extraction from the model to the representation itself, T2SP enables off-the-shelf LLMs to leverage their existing reasoning capabilities for time-series understanding. We evaluate T2SP on three reasoning tasks -- editing, captioning, and question answering -- where it consistently improves performance, reduces reasoning time, and lowers failure rates compared with raw-string representations. Our results demonstrate that T2SP provides an effective interface between time series and LLMs.

</details>


### [19] [Scalable anomaly detection via a univariate Christoffel function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12483)
*Florian Grivet,Didier Henrion,Jean-Bernard Lasserre,Louise Travé-Massuyès*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了UCF（单变量Christoffel函数）方法，通过使用查询点与支持点之间的平方距离，解决了传统Christoffel函数方法在异常检测中的维度扩展性问题，在ADBench基准测试中平均精度超越14个最先进基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于Christoffel函数的异常检测方法虽然具有坚实的数学基础和计算简洁性，但其实际应用受到严重限制：需要逆转变量大小随数据维度呈指数增长的矩阵，导致该方法在中等维度数据集上就变得难以处理。因此需要解决这一维度局限性，同时保持其关键理论特性（支持集二分行为和准确的形状捕获）。

Method: 提出UCF（单变量Christoffel函数）方法，该方法基于查询点与支持点之间的平方距离构建，避免了传统方法中需要求逆大规模矩阵的问题，从而克服了维度扩展性瓶颈，同时保持了Christoffel函数的关键理论性质。

Result: 在ADBench基准数据集上进行大量实验，UCF方法在平均精度指标上持续优于14个最先进基线方法，成功验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过解决Christoffel函数的可扩展性瓶颈，本研究为异常检测方法工具箱增添了稳健、理论基础扎实且普遍适用的方法，扩展了异常检测方法的工具集。

Abstract: Anomaly detection plays a critical role in identifying unusual patterns across domains such as fraud detection, network intrusion, and system fault diagnosis. Recently, Christoffel function-based methods, rooted in polynomial optimization, have emerged as promising alternatives to deep learning due to their strong mathematical foundations and computational frugality. However, their practical applicability is hindered by the need to invert a matrix whose size grows exponentially with the data dimension, rendering the method intractable even for moderate-dimensional datasets. This paper addresses the dimensionality limitations of Christoffel function-based anomaly detection while preserving its key theoretical properties, i.e., the on-off support dichotomy behavior and the accurate support shape capture. We introduce UCF, a univariate Christoffel function which is based on the squared distance between the query point and the support points. Extensive experiments on the ADBench benchmark demonstrate that UCF consistently outperforms 14 state-of-the-art baselines in terms of Average Precision. By resolving the scalability bottleneck of the Christoffel Function, this work expands the toolkit of anomaly detection methods with a robust, theoretically grounded, and universally applicable approach.

</details>


### [20] [Speculative Rollback Correction for Quality-Diverse Web Agent Imitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12485)
*Longkun Hao,Hongyu Lin,Hao Li,Zhichao Yang,Haojie Hao,Dongshuo Huang,Haitao Yang,Hongyu Ge,Ming jie Xie,Yanjun Wu,Zi Hao Yin,Yan Bai,Yihang Lou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SRC框架，通过固定视界的分支审查机制平衡专家干预时机，解决交互式网络智能体模仿学习中早期错误累积和过度依赖专家的问题，在WebArena-Infinity上有效收集高质量轨迹数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在交互式网络智能体的模仿学习中，专家干预时机的选择面临两难：延迟干预导致早期错误累积使页面状态不可恢复；过早或过度干预则使智能体过度依赖专家策略，陷入单一刚性轨迹的局部最优。

Method: 提出推测回滚校正(SRC)框架：学生先执行短推测片段，教师仅在局部进展中断时审查并定位首个有害偏差，回滚保留有用前缀；通过硬验证器过滤成功轨迹并保留在轻量级质量-多样性档案中，支持对局部修正和验证通过轨迹的下一动作监督微调。

Result: 在WebArena-Infinity上收集977条验证器通过轨迹和9,183个下一动作样本；固定视界审查相比步骤级审查改善了恢复与查询的权衡，同时保留验证通过的解决方案变体。

Conclusion: SRC框架通过固定视界分支审查机制有效平衡了专家干预时机，既避免早期错误累积又防止过度依赖，为可重置智能体环境提供了高效的模仿学习范式。

Abstract: Training interactive web agents through imitation learning from expert trajectories has emerged as a highly effective approach. However, determining the optimal timing for expert intervention presents a critical challenge in this context. Delayed intervention often leads to the accumulation of early-stage errors, pushing the page state into an irrecoverable regime. Conversely, premature or excessive intervention causes the agent to become overly reliant on expert policies, trapping the model in local optima characterized by a single, rigid trajectory. We propose Speculative Rollback Correction (SRC), a branch-level imitation framework for resettable agent environments. Instead of requesting teacher labels at every visited state or correcting only after a completed trajectory, SRC uses fixed-horizon branch review: the student executes a short speculative segment before teacher review, and the teacher localizes the first harmful deviation only when local progress breaks. Rollback preserves useful prefixes, while successful rollouts are filtered by a hard verifier and retained in a lightweight quality-diversity archive. The resulting data supports next-action supervised fine-tuning on both localized corrections and verifier-passing trajectories. On WebArena-Infinity, SRC collects 977 verifier-passing trajectories and 9,183 next-action examples; fixed-horizon review improves the recovery-versus-query tradeoff over step-level review while retaining verifier-passing solution variants. Code is available at https://github.com/LongkunHao/SRC_gui_agent.

</details>


### [21] [An Empirical Study on Predictive Maintenance for Component X in Heavy-Duty Scania Trucks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12486)
*Valeriu Dimidov,Sasan Jafarnejad,Raphaël Frank*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对卡车车队基于状态的预测性维护方法，通过将单调非递减时间序列转换为表格数据并使用AutoML进行分类，在Scania Component X数据集上降低了成本并简化了建模过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卡车车队基于状态的预测性维护（PdM）系统的实施面临巨大挑战，包括卡车产生的大量数据、通过传感器数据检测故障的固有复杂性，以及在解决方案实施中难以找到具有成本效益的权衡方案。

Method: 该方法基于一个假设：被监控组件的磨损状态可以表示为单调非递减的时间序列。具体步骤包括：仅选择时间序列中最近的观测数据，将其转换为表格格式，然后使用专为表格数据设计的机器学习模型进行分类，并通过AutoML简化建模过程。

Result: 在Scania Component X数据集上的实验结果表明，与当前最先进（SOTA）方法相比，所提出的方法降低了成本，同时通过AutoML简化了建模过程。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于单调非递减时间序列假设的PdM方法论在降低维护成本方面具有优势，且通过AutoML技术显著简化了模型构建流程，为卡车车队预测性维护提供了一种实用且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Condition-based Predictive Maintenance (PdM) for truck fleets has gained momentum in recent years. This maintenance strategy aims to minimize unplanned downtimes and reduce costs by monitoring the health status of vehicles and taking proactive action based on their condition. However, the implementation of condition-based PdM systems is challenging due to the large volume of data generated by the trucks, the inherent complexity of detecting failures through sensor data and the difficulties in finding cost-effective trade-offs in the solution's implementation. In this paper, we define and validate a condition-based PdM methodology built on the assumption that the wear-and-tear state of the monitored component can be represented as a monotonically non-decreasing time series. It involves selecting only the most recent observations from the time series and transforming them into a tabular format for classification using machine learning (ML) models designed for tabular data. Our results indicate that the proposed methodology reduces costs on the Scania Component X dataset compared to current state-of-the-art (SOTA) approaches, while also simplifying the modeling process through AutoML.

</details>


### [22] [DynamicPTQ: Mitigating Activation Quantization Collapse via Residual-Stream Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12487)
*Zimo Zhao,Maolin Wang,Bowen Yu,Bowen Liu,Xiao Han,Xiangyu Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了DynamicPTQ，一种动态后训练量化策略，通过识别残差流动态中的量化敏感层并仅对这些层使用8位激活量化（其余保持4位），解决了LLM全4位量化中的激活量化难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大语言模型的全4位量化（W4A4KV4）中，激活量化面临巨大挑战。现有最先进的方法主要通过基于变换的平滑技术（如正交旋转和仿射缩放）来缓解巨大激活值问题，但忽视了残差流的跨层动态特性。研究发现，巨大激活值在网络深度上呈现相位式出现和消失模式，引发大的残差变化，导致新注入的逐层更新主导4位量化尺度并削弱历史残差信息，而静态的变换平滑无法完全解决这种由跨层残差变化引起的动态量化不稳定性。

Method: 首先引入Jump Ratio和Historical Feature SNR两个指标来刻画残差流动态行为，揭示静态变换平滑的局限性。基于此分析，提出DynamicPTQ——一种面向相位感知的混合精度激活量化策略。该方法从残差流动态中识别量化敏感层，仅对这些敏感层分配8位激活精度，同时将权重、KV缓存和其他激活保持在4位精度。DynamicPTQ可直接集成到QuaRot、SpinQuant和FlatQuant等强PTQ基线中。

Result: 在LLaMA-2和LLaMA-3上的实验表明，DynamicPTQ在W4A4KV4量化设置下持续改善困惑度（perplexity）和零样本问答性能。在适度的内存开销下，实现了1.05到1.07倍的吞吐量提升，证明了该方法在实际低比特LLM推理中的有效性。

Conclusion: 本研究揭示了残差流跨层动态对低比特量化的关键影响，证明了静态变换平滑方法的局限性。提出的DynamicPTQ通过相位感知的混合精度策略，在保持计算效率的同时有效解决了动态量化不稳定性问题，为稳健的低比特大语言模型推理提供了一条实用路径，成功平衡了量化精度与推理效率。

Abstract: Post-training quantization (PTQ) is essential for efficient large language model inference, but reliably quantizing activations remains challenging when weights, activations, and KV caches are all quantized to 4-bit precision. A key difficulty lies in massive activations, whose extreme values dominate the activation range and amplify quantization errors. State-of-the-art methods mainly mitigate massive activations through transformation-based smoothing, such as orthogonal rotations and affine scaling, but overlook the cross-layer dynamics of the residual stream. In this paper, we show that massive activations emerge and disappear in a phase-wise pattern across network depth, triggering large residual changes. These changes cause newly injected layer-wise updates to dominate the 4-bit quantization scale and weaken historical residual information. To characterize this behavior, we introduce Jump Ratio and Historical Feature SNR. This suggests that static transformation-based smoothing cannot fully resolve dynamic quantization instability caused by cross-layer residual changes. Based on this analysis, we propose DynamicPTQ, a Dynamic Post-Training Quantization policy for phase-aware mixed-precision activation quantization. DynamicPTQ identifies quantization-sensitive layers from residual-stream dynamics and assigns 8-bit activation precision only to these layers, while keeping weights, KV caches, and other activations in 4-bit precision. It can be directly integrated with strong PTQ baselines such as QuaRot, SpinQuant, and FlatQuant. Experiments on LLaMA-2 and LLaMA-3 show that DynamicPTQ consistently improves perplexity and zero-shot QA performance under W4A4KV4 quantization, while achieving 1.05 to 1.07 times throughput improvement with modest memory overhead. These results demonstrate a practical path toward robust low-bit LLM inference.

</details>


### [23] [A Stationary (and Therefore Compatible) Representation is All You Need](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12488)
*Niccolò Biondi,Federico Pernici,Simone Ricci,Alberto Del Bimbo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了d-Simplex固定分类器学习的平稳表示具有兼容性，提出结合交叉熵损失和对比损失的方法来捕获高阶依赖关系，在模型更新和替换场景中实现了最先进的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习兼容表示旨在使特征表示在模型更新时能够互换使用。现有方法在模型顺序微调时面临挑战：仅使用交叉熵损失训练的d-Simplex固定分类器只能对齐一阶统计量，无法完全捕获模型更新间表示的高阶依赖关系。

Method: 作者提出使用d-Simplex固定分类器，通过交叉熵损失和对比损失的凸组合来训练模型。这种方法不仅能够捕获高阶依赖关系，而且在兼容性约束下等价于使用交叉熵损失学习。实验验证包括预训练模型顺序微调和偶尔替换为新模型的场景。

Result: 实验证明平稳表示能够在模型更新和替换期间实现不间断的检索服务（无需重新处理图库图像），同时提升性能，达到了最先进水平。代码已在GitHub上开源。

Conclusion: 本文建立了d-Simplex固定分类器学习的平稳表示与兼容性之间的理论基础，为未来工作奠定了基础，并在实际学习场景中可直接应用。该方法在模型持续更新的实际应用中具有重要价值。

Abstract: Learning compatible representations aims to learn feature representations that can be used interchangeably over time whenever a model undergoes updates. In this paper, we demonstrate that stationary representations learned by d-Simplex fixed classifiers imply compatibility as in its formal definition. This result establishes a foundation for future works and can be directly exploited in practical learning scenarios. We address the challenge of learning compatibility using $d$-Simplex fixed classifiers when the model is sequentially fine-tuned. Learning according to a d-Simplex fixed classifier with the cross-entropy loss aligns feature distributions at the first-order statistics. Consequently, it may not fully capture higher-order dependencies in the representation between model updates. To address this issue, we demonstrate that training the model using a $d$-Simplex fixed classifier through a convex combination of the cross-entropy loss and a contrastive loss not only captures higher-order dependencies, but is also equivalent to learning with the cross-entropy under the compatibility constraints. We confirm our findings with extensive experiments also considering a new scenario where a pre-trained model is sequentially fine-tuned and occasionally replaced with an improved model. We show that stationary representations enable uninterrupted retrieval services (without reprocessing gallery images) while improving performance during model updates and replacements, achieving state-of-the-art. Code at https://github.com/miccunifi/iamcl2r.

</details>


### [24] [Robustness Verification of Recurrent Neural Networks with Abstraction Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12490)
*Li-Jen Lin,Chih-Duo Hong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于循环神经网络（RNN）认证鲁棒性验证的抽象-精化框架。通过分割跨越零点的预激活区间以消除松弛误差，并引入基于SHAP的引导策略来选择关键时间步，从而在控制计算成本的同时显著提升了验证成功率和鲁棒性边界的紧致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对循环神经网络（RNN）进行认证局部鲁棒性验证具有挑战性。现有可扩展的线性边界传播方法由于非线性松弛引入的近似误差会通过循环连接传播并随时间累积，导致验证结果过于保守，难以认证实际上具有鲁棒性的输入，特别是当许多预激活区间跨越零点时。

Method: 本文提出了一个用于RNN验证的抽象-精化框架。其核心方法包括：1）区间分割：对跨越零点的预激活区间进行划分，以消除主要的松弛误差；这使得ReLU激活变得精确，而tanh和sigmoid等平滑激活函数能获得更紧致的线性包络。2）SHAP引导的时间步选择策略：为了控制长序列中分割的组合成本，该策略根据隐藏状态对验证目标的贡献对其进行排名，并仅按时间顺序精化最关键的时间步。

Result: 在CIFAR10和MNIST stroke基准测试上的实验表明，与仅使用抽象的基线方法相比，该方法在验证成功率和鲁棒性边界紧致性方面均有持续提升。实验还揭示了ReLU模型和tanh模型之间存在的运行时间与验证效果之间的明确权衡。

Conclusion: 所提出的抽象-精化框架通过有针对性地消除松弛误差，有效解决了RNN验证中因误差累积导致的保守性问题，在保证可扩展性的同时提高了验证精度。

Abstract: Certified local robustness verification for recurrent neural networks (RNNs) is challenging because approximation errors introduced by nonlinear relaxations can propagate through recurrent connections and accumulate over time. As a result, scalable linear bound propagation methods often become overly conservative and fail to certify inputs that are in fact robust, especially when many pre-activation intervals cross zero. We propose an abstraction-refinement framework for RNN verification that partitions such intervals to remove the dominant relaxation error: on each refined branch, ReLU becomes exact, and smooth activations such as tanh and sigmoid admit substantially tighter linear envelopes. To control the combinatorial cost of splitting in long sequences, we introduce a SHAP-guided timestep selection strategy that ranks hidden states by their contribution to the verification objective and refines only the most critical timesteps in temporal order. Experiments on CIFAR10 and MNIST stroke benchmarks demonstrate consistent improvements in verification success and robustness-margin tightness over abstraction-only baselines, while exposing clear runtime trade-offs between ReLU and tanh models.

</details>


### [25] [Net-Ev$^2$: A Generative Simulator for Network Event Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12494)
*Guangyu Wang,Zhaonan Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Net-Ev²，一种结合事件语义与网络拓扑的生成式模拟器，用于预测扰动事件在网络中的传播演化，并构建了包含650万样本的多模态基准数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法无法同时建模事件的结构化属性与非结构化语义，且在模拟网络事件演化时难以捕捉拓扑结构，因此需要一种能够联合利用事件线索并保持网络拓扑的新型生成式模拟器。

Method: 提出Net-Ev²框架，包含两个阶段：结构引导的掩码预训练和拓扑感知扩散过程，通过类U-Net的图下采样与上采样实现去噪；推理阶段仅需自然语言事件描述即可生成模拟。同时构建了Net-Ev²-6.5M多模态基准数据集及拓扑感知评价指标JL-MMD。

Result: 实验表明，Net-Ev²在生成网络动态模拟方面达到最先进性能，并展现出强大的泛化能力，能够有效保持生成结果中的拓扑结构特征。

Conclusion: Net-Ev²成功实现了事件语义与网络拓扑的联合建模，为现实世界网络中的扰动事件传播模拟提供了灵活且高效的解决方案，在四个大规模道路网络上验证了其有效性和实用价值。

Abstract: Reducing real-world trial and error has long been a central goal of decision making, and generative simulators advance this goal by modeling the evolution of future states. An even more challenging yet meaningful task is simulating how disturbance events (e.g., accidents) propagate their impacts across real-world networks. The existing approaches fall short of modeling both structured attributes and unstructured semantics of events, and capturing topological structures in simulating network event evolution. Therefore, we are motivated to propose Net-Ev$^2$ ($\underline{\textbf{Net}}$work $\underline{\textbf{Ev}}$ent $\underline{\textbf{Ev}}$olution), a novel generative simulator that jointly leverages event cues while preserving network topology in simulations. Specifically, the framework consists of two stages, namely structure-guided masked pre-training and topology-aware diffusion process, which is achieved by U-Net-like graph downsampling and upsampling during denoising. At inference time, Net-Ev$^2$ can generate simulations using natural-language event input only, with greater flexibility for practical usage. Furthermore, we introduce Net-Ev$^2$-6.5M, a multimodal benchmark of aligned event and network traffic data across four large-scale road networks, as well as a new topology-aware metric, namely JL-MMD, to evaluate topological fidelity in generated network dynamics. Extensive experiments demonstrate the state-of-the-art performance and strong generalization ability of Net-Ev$^2$. Code is made available at https://github.com/Guangyu4/Net-Ev-2.

</details>


### [26] [$μ$VLA: On Recurrent Memory for Partially Observable Manipulation in VLA Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12497)
*Egor Cherepanov,Nikita Kachaev,Daniil Zelezetsky,Aydar Bulatov,Artem Pshenitsyn,Yuri Kuratov,Alexey Skrynnik,Aleksandr I. Panov,Alexey K. Kovalev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了μVLA，通过在OpenVLA-OFT中添加可学习的记忆token来实现最小化的循环机制，在部分可观测性任务上将成功率从0.42提升至0.84，同时保持全可观测任务性能不退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言-动作(VLA)模型基于当前观测预测未来动作，在部分可观测场景下无法获取历史关键信息。虽有记忆增强方法存在，但它们同时引入了循环、检索、压缩等多种机制，使得循环机制的独立贡献难以厘清。

Method: 在预训练VLA骨干网络中添加少量可学习的记忆token，跨时间步传递并通过自注意力更新；使用截断时间反向传播端到端训练；无辅助损失、无架构变更；通过记忆宽度m、TBPTT长度K、记忆更新规则(跨步梯度或分离EMA)参数化。

Result: 在MIKASA-Robo基准上，五个训练任务平均成功率从0.42提升至0.84；在相同记忆结构的测试任务上达到0.23(基线0.07)；在LIBERO全可观测任务上实现96.2%成功率，无性能退化。在需要不同记忆结构的任务上性能接近基线。

Conclusion: 研究标定了最小骨干内循环机制的能力边界：在历史信息可由小规模记忆token编码的场景下充分有效，在需要复杂记忆结构(如检索、层次化记忆)的场景下仍需额外机制支持。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models predict chunks of future actions from the current observation, an assumption that fails under partial observability, where decisions depend on information no longer visible. Existing memory-augmented VLAs simultaneously introduce recurrence, retrieval, compression modules, auxiliary objectives, hierarchical memory, or task-specific architectural changes, so the contribution of recurrence itself remains entangled with surrounding machinery. We present a controlled isolation study of recurrence in a strong pretrained VLA backbone. Our formulation augments the transformer with a small set of learnable memory tokens carried across timesteps and updated through self-attention, trained end to end with truncated backpropagation through time, with no auxiliary losses and no architectural changes. We instantiate this as $μ$VLA, a family of OpenVLA-OFT variants parameterized by memory width m, TBPTT length K, and the memory update rule (cross-step gradients or a detached EMA), so that recurrence is the only varying factor. On MIKASA-Robo, $μ$VLA improves average success rate on five training tasks from 0.42 to 0.84 at the strongest setting and reaches 0.23 on held-out tasks with the same memory structure versus 0.07 for the memoryless baseline. On tasks requiring different memory structure, performance remains near baseline. On LIBERO, the strongest recurrent variant achieves 96.2% average success, indicating no regression under full observability. We interpret these results as a calibration of the capability envelope of minimal in-backbone recurrence, identifying the regime in which it is sufficient and the regime where additional memory structure is required. Demos and videos can be found in https://avanturist322.github.io/mu-vla/.

</details>


### [27] [Improving Crash Frequency Prediction from Simulated Traffic Conflicts Using Machine Learning Based Microsimulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12500)
*Xian Liu,Carlo G. Prato,Gustav Markkula*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究比较了机器学习（ML）行为模型与传统基于规则模型在交通微观仿真中的安全预测表现。结果显示，ML模型生成的冲突能够准确预测真实碰撞频率，而传统模型无法进行有意义的预测，表明ML行为模型在提高微观仿真安全预测精度方面具有显著优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于微观仿真的安全研究采用简化的基于规则的行为模型，虽然能较好复现交通流，但往往无法生成现实的冲突动态，限制了碰撞预测精度。机器学习行为模型的出现为通过大规模轨迹数据学习人类驾驶行为提供了机遇，有望提高微观仿真真实性和碰撞预测准确性。

Method: 选取英国利兹五个真实信号控制交叉口，分别使用标准基于规则模型和先进机器学习模型进行交通微观仿真。采用二维碰撞时间（Time-to-Collision）指标分析仿真车辆轨迹以识别模拟冲突，然后运用极值理论建模预测碰撞频率。

Result: 机器学习模型生成的冲突能够产生与真实碰撞数据一致的碰撞预测，而基于规则模型无法进行有意义的预测（推测原因是缺乏针对特定仿真交叉口的模型校准）。直接使用ML生成的模拟碰撞预测现实碰撞频率效果不佳，表明当前ML模型能够真实复现冲突但尚不能生成现实的碰撞。

Conclusion: 研究表明ML行为模型在无需针对特定位置进行模型校准的情况下，能够有效提高基于模拟冲突的碰撞预测能力。研究为基于ML的交通微观仿真指明了明确的未来发展方向，即当前ML模型在冲突模拟方面表现良好，但在碰撞生成方面仍需改进。

Abstract: Traffic microsimulation combined with surrogate safety measures has increasingly been used as a proactive alternative to historical crash data for predicting crash frequency for current or planned road infrastructure designs. However, existing microsimulation-based safety studies have adopted simplified rule-based behaviour models, which reproduce traffic flow reasonably well but often fail to generate realistic conflict dynamics, limiting crash prediction accuracy. Recent advances in machine learning (ML)-based behaviour models offer a promising opportunity to potentially improve microsimulation realism and crash frequency predictions by learning human driving behaviour directly from large-scale trajectory datasets. To investigate this possibility, traffic microsimulation was conducted for five real-world signalised intersections in Leeds, UK, using both a standard rule-based model and a state-of-the-art ML model. Simulated vehicle trajectories were analysed using a two-dimensional Time-to-Collision metric to identify simulated conflicts, which were then modelled using Extreme Value Theory to predict crash frequency. Results show that conflicts from the ML model yielded crash predictions in line with the real-world crash data, whereas the rule-based model did not permit meaningful predictions, presumably due to a lack of model calibration to the specific simulated intersections. Directly using ML-generated simulated crashes to predict real-world crash frequency also yielded poor results, suggesting that while current ML models can realistically reproduce conflicts, they are not yet able to generate realistic crashes. Overall, the findings demonstrate that ML-based behaviour models are promising for improving crash prediction from simulated conflicts, without a need for location-specific model calibration, and suggest clear future directions for ML-based traffic microsimulation.

</details>


### [28] [M*: A Modular, Extensible, Serving System for Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12688)
*Atindra Jha,Naomi Sagan,Keisuke Kamahori,Irmak Sivgin,Rohan Sanda,Steven Gao,Mark Horowitz,Luke Zettlemoyer,Olivia Hsu,Jure Leskovec,Baris Kasikci,Stephanie Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了M*，一个面向复合AI模型的通用服务系统。通过将模型表示为数据流图并引入Walk Graph抽象，实现了跨模态任务的高效处理，在多项基准测试中性能显著优于现有框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前正处于复合模型架构的新时代，模型集成了视觉编码器、语言骨干、扩散头、音频编解码器、动作生成器等多种组件，支撑着统一多模态模型、全模态模型、语音-语言模型等多种应用。然而现有模型服务框架基于狭隘的模型结构假设，无法适应这种新的架构多样性。

Method: 提出M*系统，将模型表示为数据流图，将跨模态和任务的请求处理为图的遍历。核心贡献是Walk Graph抽象，支持模型组件的任意组合、到物理集群的灵活部署，以及在分布式运行时内的模型无关优化。该抽象能简洁地捕获来自广泛家族的复合模型。

Result: 在代表性模型上验证：对于BAGEL的文本到图像任务，端到端延迟比vLLM-Omni平均降低20%；对于Qwen3-Omni的文本到语音任务，实时因子降低达2.9倍，吞吐量提升2.7倍；在机器人规划任务上，性能比V-JEPA 2-AC基线提升达12.5倍。

Conclusion: M*为复杂模型的高效服务铺平了道路，能够在最小化开发者工作量的同时实现性能优化，为复合AI模型的服务提供了统一而高效的解决方案。

Abstract: We are entering a new era of composite model architectures that integrate diverse components such as vision encoders, language backbones, diffusion and flow heads, audio codecs, action generators, and world-model predictors. Such architectures underpin a broad class of multimodal models, including unified multimodal models, omni models, speech-language models, vision-language-action policies, and world models. However, existing model serving frameworks were built on narrow assumptions about model structure, making them ill-suited to accommodate this new architectural diversity. Here we present M*, a universal serving system for efficient serving of composite AI models. M* represents models as dataflow graphs, processing requests spanning diverse modalities and tasks as traversals over these graphs. The core insight is a modular abstraction that supports arbitrary composition of model components, flexible placement onto a physical cluster, and model-agnostic optimizations within a distributed runtime. We call this abstraction the Walk Graph and show how it can concisely capture composite models from a broad range of families. We instantiate M* on representative models and find that it achieves, on average, 20% lower end-to-end latency than vLLM-Omni for text-to-image workloads on BAGEL, while delivering up to 2.9x lower real-time factor and 2.7x higher throughput for text-to-speech workloads on Qwen3-Omni. M* also outperforms the V-JEPA 2-AC rollout baseline for robotic planning by up to 12.5x. Thus, our work paves the road towards more efficient serving of complex models with minimal developer effort.

</details>


### [29] [Policy-driven Conformal Prediction for Trustworthy QoT Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12501)
*Kiarash Rezaei,Omran Ayoub,Paolo Monti,Carlos Natalino*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了Conformal QoT框架，通过融合统计保证的质量传输(QoT)估计与运营决策策略，解决了域偏移下的光路可行性预测问题，将预测准确率从92%提升至99.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在光网络中，质量传输(QoT)估计面临域偏移(domain shift)问题，即训练数据与实际部署环境存在分布差异，导致传统机器学习模型的预测可靠性下降。现有方法缺乏统计保证，难以在实际运营中做出可靠的决策，因此需要开发能够在域偏移下提供可验证预测保证的框架。

Method: 提出Conformal QoT框架，采用共形预测(Conformal Prediction)方法提供统计保证的QoT估计，并将此估计与运营决策策略相结合。该框架是策略驱动的(policy-driven)，能够量化预测不确定性，在域偏移场景下仍能保持预测的可靠性，并据此进行光路可行性决策。

Result: 在开放数据集上的实验表明，Conformal QoT框架将光路可行性预测准确率从基准方法的92%显著提升至99.6%，成功实现了域偏移下的可靠预测，验证了统计保证与决策策略结合的有效性。

Conclusion: Conformal QoT框架成功解决了光网络中域偏移导致的QoT预测可靠性问题，通过共形预测提供的统计保证与运营决策的有机结合，实现了高准确度的光路可行性预测，为实际光网络部署提供了可靠的决策支持工具。

Abstract: We propose Conformal QoT, a policy-driven framework that combines statistically guaranteed QoT estimation with operational decision policies, enabling reliable lightpath-feasibility predictions under domain shift and improving accuracy from 92\% to 99.6\% on open datasets.

</details>


### [30] [Dolph2Vec: Self-Supervised Representations of Dolphin Vocalizations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12503)
*Chiara Semenzin,Faadil Mustun,Roberto Dessi,Pierre Orhan,Alexis Emanuelli,Yair Lakretz,Gonzalo de Polavieja,German Sumbre*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发布了首个海豚专用大规模自监督学习模型Dolph2Vec，在签名哨声分类和检测任务上显著优于通用基线模型，并能学习到可解释的声学单元。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的生物声学自监督学习模型优先考虑跨物种的广泛泛化能力，而非针对单个通信系统的细粒度结构进行优化。为了深入研究海豚通信的精细结构，需要开发针对特定物种优化的模型。

Method: 收集并发布了超过五年的纵向录音数据集（来自半自然海洋环境中的五只已知海豚），基于Wav2Vec2.0架构开发了首个大规模、物种专用的自监督学习模型Dolph2Vec，并在签名哨声分类和哨声检测两个生物学相关任务上进行基准测试。

Result: Dolph2Vec在签名哨声分类和哨声检测两个任务上均显著优于通用基线模型。此外，学习到的嵌入和码本结构能够捕捉与海豚哨声类别及可能的次哨声结构相一致的可解释声学单元。

Conclusion: 研究表明自监督学习不仅可以作为性能优异的模型，还可以作为科学工具来探索动物通信研究中的假设，实现对通信模式的细粒度分析。

Abstract: Self-supervised learning (SSL) has opened new opportunities in bioacoustics by enabling scalable modeling of animal vocalizations without the need for expensive manual annotation. However, current SSL models in this domain prioritize broad generalization across species and are not optimized for uncovering the fine-grained structure of individual communication systems. In this work, we collect and release a novel dataset of over five years of longitudinal recordings, from five known dolphins in a semi-naturalistic marine environment, an unprecedented resource for studying dolphin communication. We adapt the Wav2Vec2.0 Baevski et al. (2020) architecture to this domain and introduce Dolph2Vec, the first large-scale, species-specific SSL model trained exclusively on this data. We benchmark our model on two biologically relevant tasks: signature whistle classification and whistle detection. Dolph2Vec significantly outperforms general-purpose baselines in both tasks. Beyond performance, we show that learned embeddings and codebook structure capture interpretable acoustic units aligned with dolphin whistle categories and possibly sub-whistle structure, enabling fine-grained analysis of communication patterns. Our findings demonstrate how SSL can serve as both a model and a scientific tool to explore hypotheses in animal communication research.

</details>


### [31] [Clipping Makes Distributed and Federated Asynchronous SGD Robust to Stragglers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13287)
*Samuel Erickson,Mikael Johansson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从理论上证明了梯度裁剪能消除异步随机梯度下降（ASGD）中最大延迟对收敛性的负面影响，从而解释了深度学习异步训练中梯度裁剪的稳定作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异步随机梯度下降（ASGD）在现代机器学习中用于并行化训练，但慢节点造成的大延迟会损害收敛性。经验观察表明梯度裁剪能稳定深度学习的异步训练，但缺乏理论解释。本文旨在为这一现象提供理论依据。

Method: 采用sub-Weibull梯度噪声模型（涵盖从sub-Gaussian到更重尾分布），对ASGD算法进行理论分析，证明梯度裁剪能消除最大延迟对oracle复杂度的依赖。

Result: 证明了梯度裁剪后ASGD的期望收敛性，并首次在异步优化中证明了高概率收敛性，同时展示了梯度裁剪消除了最大延迟对收敛率的影响。

Conclusion: 梯度裁剪通过处理重尾噪声，有效消除了慢节点带来的延迟影响，为深度学习异步训练中梯度裁剪的稳定作用提供了坚实的理论基础。

Abstract: In modern machine learning, parallelization of training is an important strategy for increasing scale. Asynchronous stochastic gradient descent (ASGD), which maximizes the utilization of available hardware by avoiding waiting for slow workers. However, with constant step sizes, the convergence of ASGD is nonetheless affected negatively by slow workers due to large delays in updates. At the same time, it has been empirically observed in asynchronous training of deep learning models that gradient clipping "stabilizes" training. In this work, we provide a theoretical justification for this behavior, as we show that clipping removes the dependence of the maximum delay in the oracle complexity. We employ a sub-Weibull model of gradient noise which generalizes sub-Gaussian and sub-exponential distributions to more heavy-tailed distributions, motivated by empirical observations in deep learning. We show convergence in expectation, and the first time in asynchronous optimization, convergence with high probability.

</details>


### [32] [Boosting Direct Preference Optimization with Penalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12505)
*Pengwei Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DPOP通过在DPO基础上增加对参考模型自生成响应的门控惩罚，利用了传统成对偏好优化中未使用的信号，在AlpacaEval 2.0上相对基线提升5.3%和4.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的离线偏好优化方法（如DPO及其变体）仅使用静态数据集中存储的chosen和rejected响应对进行训练，但忽略了一个有价值的信号：参考模型自身针对相同提示词会生成的响应。这导致有用的训练信号被浪费。

Method: 提出DPOP（Direct Preference Optimization with Penalization），在基础偏好损失上增加对reference-greedy响应的门控惩罚项。该惩罚仅在当前策略对preferred响应的似然度低于rejected响应时激活，从而动态地惩罚不期望的行为。同时探索了SimNPO风格的长度归一化惩罚方法。

Result: 在AlpacaEval 2.0基准测试中，DPOP在Llama-3-8b-it和Gemma-2-9b-it两个模型上均取得优于DPO、SimPO和AlphaDPO的性能，长度控制胜率相对基线分别提升5.3%和4.4%。消融实验表明SimNPO风格的长度归一化惩罚优于NPO和token级非似然方法。

Conclusion: DPOP证明了利用参考模型自生成响应作为额外训练信号的有效性，通过门控惩罚机制实现了性能的显著提升，为离线偏好优化提供了一个简单而有效的改进方向。

Abstract: Offline preference optimization has become a practical substitute for reinforcement learning from human feedback, but pairwise objectives such as Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) and its variants use only the chosen and rejected responses stored in a static dataset. This leaves a useful signal unused: the response that the reference model itself would generate for the same prompt. We propose Direct Preference Optimization with Penalization (DPOP), a simple extension of DPO that augments the base preference loss with a gated penalty on reference-greedy responses. DPOP activates this penalty only when the current policy still assigns a lower likelihood to the preferred response than to the rejected response. On AlpacaEval 2.0, DPOP improves length-controlled win rate over DPO, SimPO, and AlphaDPO on both Llama-3-8b-it and Gemma-2-9b-it, achieving relative gains of 5.3\% and 4.4\% over baselines on the two models, respectively. Ablations further show that a SimNPO-style length-normalized penalty is stronger than NPO and token-level unlikelihood in this setting.

</details>


### [33] [Rubric-Guided Self-Distillation: Post-Training Without Rubric Verifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12507)
*MohammadHossein Rezaei,Anas Mahmoud,Zihao Wang,Utkarsh Tyagi,Advait Gosai,Razvan-Gabriel Dumitru,Aakash Sabharwal,Bing Liu,Yunzhong He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了RGSD方法，通过自蒸馏将基于评分准则的条件策略作为教师模型，实现无需验证器的训练，在医疗和科学领域取得了与基于验证器的GRPO方法相当的评分准则满足度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于评分准则的训练方法依赖LLM验证器对每个轨迹进行评分，这带来了显著的训练开销、引入验证器特定的偏差，并将反馈信号局限为稀疏的轨迹级奖励。

Method: 提出评分准则引导的自蒸馏方法(RGSD)，将基于评分准则条件的策略作为教师模型，无条件策略作为学生模型，通过逐token蒸馏将教师分布传递给学生，用密集的每token学习信号替代稀疏的轨迹级奖励，完全移除了训练过程中的LLM验证器。

Result: 在Qwen-2.5 (3B, 7B)和Qwen3-Thinking (4B, 8B)模型上的医疗和科学领域实验中，RGSD在仅使用每个prompt一个on-policy轨迹且无需训练时验证器调用的情况下，达到了与基于验证器的GRPO相当的评分准则满足度。消融实验显示原始评分准则提供了比自生成参考响应更强的教师增强信号。

Conclusion: RGSD作为一种无需验证器的替代方案，在验证器成本或可靠性成为瓶颈时具有重要价值，虽然更强的GRPO验证器在某些设置下可能表现更好，但RGSD提供了一种互补的解决方案。

Abstract: Rubrics have emerged as an alternative to RLVR in open-ended domains where a single ground-truth final answer is not available. Existing rubric-based training methods rely on an LLM verifier that scores each rollout against rubrics. This introduces substantial training-time overhead, exposes optimization to verifier-specific biases, and reduces rubric feedback to a sparse end-of-trajectory signal. We propose Rubric-Guided Self-Distillation (RGSD), a verifier-free training method in which the base policy, conditioned on the rubric, serves as the teacher for the unconditioned student. RGSD distills the rubric-conditioned teacher distribution into the student token-by-token, replacing sparse trajectory-level rewards with dense per-token learning signals and removing the LLM judge from the training loop entirely. Across Qwen-2.5 (3B, 7B) and Qwen3-Thinking (4B, 8B) models on medical and science domains, RGSD achieves rubric satisfaction comparable to judge-based GRPO while using one on-policy rollout per prompt and no training-time verifier calls. Ablations show that raw rubrics provide a stronger teacher enrichment signal than self-generated reference responses, while a stronger GRPO judge can outperform RGSD in some settings, positioning RGSD as a complementary verifier-free alternative when verifier cost or reliability is the bottleneck.

</details>

*Célestin Eve,Gaël Varoquaux,Thomas Moreau*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出利用多次交叉验证拆分来提高机器学习基准测试评估的可靠性，引入了样本增益概念量化方差降低效果，并提出动态早停程序优化计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代机器学习进展依赖经验性基准测试，但评估中固有的统计变异性（因算法随机性加剧）和有限的测试样本导致性能估计不可靠，造成验证危机，难以辨别真正的算法进展。

Method: 1. 提出样本增益（sample gain）概念，量化使用多次交叉验证拆分所实现的虚拟数据增强效果，以降低基准测试方差；2. 在合成数据集和真实数据集（组织病理学扫描和NLP微调）上进行实验验证；3. 开发动态早停程序，通过前几折估计后续折是否带来显著样本增益，从而决定是否提前终止交叉验证。

Result: 实验表明多次交叉验证拆分能显著提高性能估计的可靠性和稳定性；收益递减效应往往比预期出现得更晚，说明继续增加拆分仍有价值；动态早停程序能有效识别何时停止以节省计算资源。

Conclusion: 充分利用交叉验证对可用样本进行多次拆分评估，能够实现更稳健可靠的机器学习基准测试，为区分算法的真实进展提供了有效方法。

Abstract: Modern machine learning progresses through empirical work, benchmarking new methods to evaluate relative performance. However, the statistical variability inherent to evaluation - exacerbated by the stochastic nature of many algorithms - often makes performance estimation unreliable due to the limited test samples available, leading to a validation crisis in which genuine advances are difficult to discern. In this work, we show that cross-validation improves markedly confidence when evaluating and comparing learning algorithm performances. We introduce the concept of sample gain, which quantifies the virtual data augmentation achieved by using multiple cross-validation splits to reduce benchmarking variance. Experiments on both synthetic and real-world datasets (histopathologic scans and NLP fine-tuning) demonstrate that multiple splits can substantially improve the reliability and stability of performance estimates, with diminishing returns often setting in later than expected. We also introduce a procedure to dynamically early-stop cross-validation by estimating from the first few folds if subsequent folds will bring large sample gains. Our findings highlight the value of pushing cross-validation on available samples to achieve robust and reliable benchmarking.

</details>

Conclusion: 为构建能够进行稳健多模态推理的下一代地球观测基础模型提供了实用的架构设计指导。

Abstract: Foundation models are rapidly transforming Earth observation by enabling scalable pretraining across diverse unlabeled geospatial modalities. However, their architectural diversity ranging from encoder-only to encoder-decoder and masked autoencoding paradigms makes it challenging to assess performance trade offs in a consistent manner. In this work, we present an apples-to-apples comparison of leading FM architectures designed for geospatial multimodal reasoning, with a particular focus on flexibility across varied spectral band configurations. We standardize pretraining using identical self supervised learning objectives and training datasets, and evaluate all models under consistent parameterization on the GEOBench benchmark across classification and segmentation tasks. Our results offer new insights into the design trade-offs between model flexibility, modality alignment, and downstream task performance. By highlighting architectural strengths and limitations under controlled conditions, this study provides practical guidance for building next generation geospatial foundation models capable of robust multimodal reasoning.

</details>

Result: 发现嵌入空间存在一个主导的“原生性”轴，与掩码重构困惑度对齐，将序列从建模良好的细胞蛋白、病毒蛋白到打乱和随机序列排序。模型缩放不均匀地压缩不同病毒家族的轴距离。尽管如此，pLM嵌入仍保留病毒特异性信号，病毒蛋白在零样本困惑度和浅层序列特征之外仍可线性分离。

Abstract: Protein language models are trained on highly imbalanced datasets, raising the question of how they represent underrepresented biological sequences. Using viral proteins as a case study across ESM model families, we identify a dominant nativeness axis in embedding space, aligned with masked reconstruction perplexity, that orders sequences from well-modeled cellular proteins through viral proteins to shuffled and random sequences. Scaling contracts this axis unevenly across viral families. Despite this, protein language model embeddings retain viral-specific signal: viral proteins remain linearly separable beyond zero-shot perplexity and shallow sequence features. Together, these results suggest that pLM representations are structured by a general notion of nativeness while preserving information specific to distinct biological groups.

</details>

*Miquel Noguer i Alonso,David Pacheco Aznar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了AI寒冬的补充解释：除了工程失败和过度炒作外，当时的主流范式还遇到了严格的数学形式障碍（包括表示、优化、计算复杂度、统计可学习性和高维近似等方面的局限）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点将第一和第二次AI寒冬归因于工程失败、商业失望和过度期望。本文旨在发展一个互补性论题，论证这些时期的主导范式还遇到了真正的形式障碍，即数学上精确的瓶颈。

Method: 采用综合分析而非档案研究的方法。通过多个理论视角分析AI发展的数学瓶颈：Minsky和Papert的感知机不可能性结果、Blum和Rivest建立的精确神经网络训练复杂度理论困难、Stone的非参数估计高维极小极大率、Hochreiter及Bengio等人的梯度消失分析，以及Vapnik-Chervonenkis等人的经典统计学习理论。

Result: 证明了早期AI的若干核心失望与数学上精确的瓶颈相一致。这些瓶颈涉及表示能力、优化困难、计算复杂度、统计学习的根本限制以及高维空间中的近似问题。同时分析了后来的突破如何缓解（而非完全消除）这些障碍。

Conclusion: AI寒冬不仅仅是工程和期望管理的问题，更深层地反映了当时主导范式所面临的数学本质障碍。后来的技术突破主要是缓解而非完全消除这些障碍，这为理解AI发展历史提供了新的理论视角。

Abstract: Two major periods of reduced funding and confidence in artificial intelligence research, commonly called the first and second AI winters, are usually explained through engineering failure, commercial disappointment, and inflated expectations. This article develops a complementary thesis: that the dominant paradigms of those periods also met genuine formal barriers, including limitations of representation, optimisation, computational complexity, statistical learnability, and high-dimensional approximation. The contribution is synthetic rather than archival. We do not claim that particular theorems mechanically caused the winters; rather, we show that several central disappointments of early AI were aligned with mathematically precise bottlenecks. We analyse these bottlenecks through the perceptron impossibility results of Minsky and Papert, the complexity-theoretic hardness of exact neural-network training established by Blum and Rivest, minimax rates for nonparametric estimation in high dimension due to Stone, vanishing-gradient analyses by Hochreiter and by Bengio and collaborators, and classical statistical learning theory in the tradition of Vapnik and Chervonenkis, Valiant, and Blumer and collaborators. We then relate these barriers to the later breakthroughs that mitigated, rather than eliminated, them.

</details>

*Wiliane Carolina Silva,Evandro César Vilas Boas,Felipe A. P. de Figueiredo*
