Moreover, it remains accurate under diverse perturbations, establishing ESE as a fast, generalizable, robust, and scalable multi-prediction method.

</details>


### [111] [Quantizing Time-Series Models As Dynamical Systems: Trajectory-Based Quantization Sensitivity Score](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13300)
*Mariya Pavlova,Harrison Bo Hua Zhu,Elizsveta Semenova,Yingzhen Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了基于轨迹的量化敏感度评分（TQS），从动力系统稳定性的新视角解决后训练量化问题，实现了与量化过程解耦的先验敏感度估计，并提出了无需校准数据的混合精度框架TQS-PTQ。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的后训练量化方法中，敏感度分析通常与量化过程耦合，限制了量化预算规划的灵活性，尤其对于黑盒或融合算子的编译网络。本文旨在将量化问题重新定义为动力系统稳定性问题，实现敏感度估计与量化器选择和比特宽度分配的解耦。

Method: 将网络的展开建模为离散时间动力系统，提出TQS指标来刻画量化引起的误差如何在展开范围内传播和放大。基于此开发TQS-PTQ混合精度框架，该框架无需校准数据或昂贵的二阶近似，支持黑盒或编译网络的量化预算规划。

Result: 实验结果表明，动力系统视角为资源受限环境下的低精度部署提供了一条稳健且高性能的途径，验证了TQS指标和TQS-PTQ框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 动力系统稳定性视角为后训练量化提供了新的理论基础，实现了敏感度估计与量化过程的解耦，使得量化预算规划更加灵活，特别适用于黑盒或融合算子的编译网络场景。

Abstract: We introduce the Trajectory-based Quantization Sensitivity Score (TQS), a metric that reframes post-training quantization (PTQ) through the lens of dynamical-systems stability. By modeling the network's rollout as a discrete-time dynamical system, TQS characterizes how quantization-induced errors propagate and amplify over the rollout horizon. Unlike conventional PTQ methods, where sensitivity analysis is often coupled to the quantization procedure, TQS enables a priori sensitivity estimation decoupled from quantizer selection and bit-width assignment. This separation allows for quantization budget planning even for black-box or compiled networks with fused operators. Building on this, we present TQS-PTQ, a flexible mixed-precision framework that requires no calibration data or costly second-order approximations. Our experiments show that a dynamical-systems perspective provides a robust, high-performing pathway for low-precision deployment in resource-constrained settings.

</details>


### [112] [Rarity-Gated Context Conditioning for Offline Imitation Learning-Based Maritime Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13311)
*Yongmin Kim,ByeongHoon Jeon,Sungil Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为RGFiLM的稀有度感知条件调节模块，通过数据驱动的稀有度分数控制门控机制，解决了上下文异常检测中因分布不平衡导致的稀有上下文下误报率高的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在实际的上下文异常检测任务中，上下文变量分布往往高度不平衡，稀有上下文可能包含关键信息。现有的上下文条件模型在这种频率偏差下，容易在稀有上下文场景中产生不稳定的决策和过多的误报。

Method: 提出稀有度门控特征线性调制模块（RGFiLM）。该模块结合了特征调制（对隐藏特征进行上下文条件化的缩放和偏移）与一个由数据驱动稀有度分数控制的门控机制。稀有度分数从上下文变量的经验分布中估计，用于调节上下文对中间表示的影响强度：在稀有上下文下门控更具决定性，在频繁上下文下则保持保守。

Result: 在海洋轨迹异常检测任务（使用AIS运动序列和ERA5环境上下文）的环境敏感绕行场景中进行了评估。RGFiLM在对比的上下文无关和上下文条件化方法中，实现了最佳的平均F1-假阳性率（FPR）权衡。

Conclusion: 显式地考虑上下文稀有性是降低上下文敏感型异常检测中误报率的有效方法。

Abstract: Contextual anomaly detection aims to identify abnormal behavior conditional on context variables, but practical deployments often face highly imbalanced context distributions where rare regimes can be critical information. Under such frequency bias, context-conditioned models can produce unstable decisions and excessive false alarms in rare contexts. We propose Rarity-Gated Feature-wise Linear Modulation (RGFiLM), a rarity-aware conditioning module that combines feature-wise modulation (i.e., context-conditioned scaling and shifting of hidden features) with a gate controlled by a data-driven rarity score. The rarity score is estimated from the empirical distribution of context variables and regulates how strongly context modulates intermediate representations: the gate becomes more decisive under rare contexts while remaining conservative under frequent contexts. We evaluate RGFiLM on maritime trajectory anomaly detection using AIS motion sequences with ERA5 environmental context in an environment-sensitive detour scenario. When instantiated in a sequential anomaly scoring pipeline, RGFiLM achieves the best mean F1--False Positive Rate (FPR) trade-off among the compared context-agnostic and context-conditioned methods. These results suggest that explicitly accounting for context rarity is an effective approach for reducing false alarms in context-sensitive anomaly detection.

</details>


### [113] [Navigating the Safety-Fidelity Trade-off: Massive-Variate Time Series Forecasting for Power Systems via Probabilistic Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13338)
*Kaijie Xu,Anqi Wang,Xilin Dai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了PowerPhase，首个大规模电力系统概率预测基准，覆盖2,000-36,964个联合预测通道，发现分布准确性与约束满足之间存在权衡，并提出最优模型PowerForge。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的概率预测基准无法同时评估大规模多变量系统的异构通道物理特性和运行约束。公开的多变量基准最多仅支持2,000个通道，而电力系统基准要么缺乏时序结构，要么缺乏概率评估能力。

Method: 构建了PowerPhase基准，基于六个输电网（2,000-36,964通道），每个目标轨迹由交流潮流求解生成，配备约束感知指标（Safety_mBrier、NECV、CVaR-alpha）。提出PowerForge模型，采用基于场景的分位数预测器，配备类型特定解码头和变量组间的因果桥接。

Result: 通过对八个基线和三个随机种子进行评估，发现分布准确性与约束满足的排名不一致，揭示了安全性-保真度权衡。PowerForge在所有电网上均取得最佳平均排名。

Conclusion: PowerPhase填补了大规模约束感知概率预测基准的空白，揭示了安全性-保真度权衡问题，PowerForge为该领域提供了新的最优基线模型。

Abstract: Probabilistic forecasting models are increasingly deployed on multivariate systems with distinct channel physics and operational constraints, but existing benchmarks evaluate neither property at scale. Public canonical multivariate benchmarks cap out at 2,000 channels, while power-system benchmarks either lack temporal structure or probabilistic evaluation. We introduce PowerPhase, a probabilistic forecasting benchmark built on six transmission grids ranging from 2,000 to 36,964 jointly forecasted channels, more than an order of magnitude beyond popular canonical multivariate benchmarks. Each target trajectory is the output of an AC power-flow solve, and PowerPhase ships with constraint-aware metrics, including Safety_mBrier, NECV, and CVaR-alpha, that complement CRPS and Distortion. Across eight baselines and three seeds, distributional accuracy and constraint satisfaction rank models differently, a trade-off we term safety-fidelity. We further propose PowerForge, a scenario-based quantile forecaster with type-specific decoding heads and a causal bridge between variable groups, which achieves the best average rank on every grid.

</details>


### [114] [Enhanced Low-Density Region Exploration in Classifier-Guided Diffusion Models Through Modified Reverse Diffusion Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13347)
*Jagriti Singh,Shekhar Verma,Muneendra Ojha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种无需额外训练的纯采样阶段密度感知扩展方法，通过双引导机制改进分类器引导的扩散模型，在保持生成质量的同时显著提升对长尾样本的覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准分类器引导倾向于将概率质量集中在高密度类别均值周围，导致对类条件分布尾部稀有样本的覆盖不足。现有解决长尾采样的方法需要训练额外的网络，成本较高。同时，现有的加速采样器和蒸馏技术并未显式解决长尾覆盖问题。

Method: 提出一种纯采样阶段的密度感知扩展方法，无需额外训练。核心创新包括：(1)在噪声图像而非预测噪声上应用引导；(2)双引导机制：第一重引导通过修改的分类器梯度将轨迹导向低置信度区域以探索低概率样本，第二重引导在每个时间步将采样过程导向预测的真实图像以确保生成样本接近真实数据流形。

Result: 在64x64分辨率上持续改进ADM模型的召回率(recall)，同时保持可比的FID分数。在256x256 ADM模型上展示了不同引导组合的视觉结果。此外，标准ADM分类器引导结合预测真实图像引导在ImageNet上能生成高感知质量的样本。

Conclusion: 该方法成功地在无需额外训练的情况下解决了扩散模型的长尾覆盖问题，通过双引导机制在探索低概率样本和保持生成质量之间取得了良好平衡，为条件扩散模型的采样策略提供了新的视角。

Abstract: Diffusion models have emerged as state-of-the-art generative models for high-fidelity image synthesis, particularly in their classifier-free guided and classifier-guided forms. However, standard classifier guidance concentrates probability mass around high-density class mean, leading to poor coverage of rare samples in the tails of the class-conditional distributions. Recent work on diffusion-based tail sampling mitigates this by training an additional low-density-seeking classifier with a synthetic-vs-real discriminator, at the cost of additional networks and training. In parallel, a number of samplers and distillation techniques accelerate or refine diffusion sampling, but do not explicitly address long-tail coverage. We propose a purely sampling-time, density-aware extension of classifier-guided conditional diffusion model that targets low-density regions without any additional training. We have applied guidance at noisy images not on predicted noise like most diffusion models. Starting from a pretrained conditional diffusion model and classifier on ImageNet, we modify the guided reverse dynamics by steering trajectories toward low-confidence regions via the modified classifier gradient, and at each time step, we also guide the sampling process toward the predicted real image. 1st guidance helps explore low-probability samples, and 2nd guidance helps to generate samples to be close to the real data manifold. The proposed sampler consistently improves ADM model recall at 64x64 resolution while maintaining a comparable FID, and with a 256x256 ADM model, we showed the results visually with different combinations of both guidance. We also showed that standard ADM classifier guidance, combined with predicted real image guidance, helps generate high perceptual quality samples with a 256x256 ADM model on ImageNet.

</details>


### [115] [VideoMDM: Towards 3D Human Motion Generation From 2D Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13364)
*Amir Mann,Gal Michael Harari,Merav Keidar,Or Litany*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: VideoMDM是一个基于扩散模型的框架，仅使用单目视频提取的精确2D姿态训练3D人体运动先验，无需任何3D真实标注，在HumanML3D上接近全3D监督方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D运动生成方法依赖昂贵的3D真值数据，而纯2D方法在推理时才进行2D到3D提升，无法在训练中学习连贯的3D运动流形。本文旨在利用易获取的2D视频数据，无3D真值监督的情况下训练高质量的3D运动生成模型。

Method: 提出VideoMDM框架：使用预训练的2D-to-3D提升器提供近似的3D姿态序列作为噪声教师；通过扩散-去噪过程在3D域建模，并采用深度加权的2D重投影损失进行监督。理论证明在温和假设下该损失与直接3D监督期望等价；同时将速度一致性和过参数化表示对齐等标准3D运动正则化方法适配至2D设置。

Result: 在HumanML3D数据集上，VideoMDM（FID 0.88）几乎弥合与全3D监督MDM（FID 0.54）的性能差距；在真实视频数据集Fit3D和NBA上，方法学习生成的运动在人类评估中持续获得偏好，定量结果优异。

Conclusion: VideoMDM成功证明了仅用2D监督训练高质量3D运动生成模型的可行性，通过2D重投影损失的巧妙设计实现了与3D监督的期望等价性，为缺乏3D标注的场景提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: We introduce VideoMDM, a diffusion-based framework that trains 3D human motion priors directly from accurate 2D poses extracted from monocular videos, without any 3D ground truth. A pretrained 2D-to-3D lifter provides approximate 3D pose sequences that serve as a noisy teacher: these are diffused, denoised by the model in 3D, and supervised in 2D by reprojecting the prediction and comparing against accurate keypoints. We show that, under mild assumptions, a depth-weighted 2D reprojection loss is equivalent in expectation to direct 3D supervision, and we adapt standard 3D motion regularizers - velocity consistency and over-parameterized representation alignment - to this 2D setting. Unlike methods that lift 2D to 3D only at inference, VideoMDM learns a coherent 3D motion manifold during training. On HumanML3D it nearly closes the gap to fully 3D-supervised MDM (FID 0.88 vs 0.54); On real video datasets Fit3D and NBA the method learns to generate motions consistently preferred by humans, with strong quantitative results.

</details>


### [116] [Hölder++: Improving the Quality-Coherence Trade-off in Multimodal VAEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13381)
*Huyen Vo,María Martínez-García,Isabel Valera*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了Hölder++多模态VAE模型，通过精确实现Hölder pooling、引入共享-私有表示架构和层次化推理，显著改善了生成质量与一致性之间的权衡问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态VAE方法在生成质量和跨模态一致性之间存在权衡困境：难以同时生成真实多样且在语义上跨模态一致的样本。先前研究表明Hölder pooling聚合方法能改善一致性但会牺牲样本多样性。

Method: 提出Hölder++模型，包含三个核心创新：(i)首次在多模态VAE中实现无近似的精确Hölder pooling聚合方法；(ii)扩展架构同时建模共享表示和私有（模态特定）表示（Hölder+）；(iii)引入层次化推理进一步增强共享表示与私有表示之间的解耦（Hölder++）。

Result: 实验表明Hölder++在生成质量与一致性权衡上实现了一致性改善，学习到了更结构化的潜在空间，且其学习到的共享表示对下游任务具有更好的信息性。

Conclusion: Hölder++成功突破了多模态VAE中生成质量与跨模态一致性之间的固有矛盾，为多模态生成建模提供了更优的解决方案，在表示学习和生成任务中均展现出优越性能。

Abstract: Existing approaches for multimodal variational autoencoders (VAEs) face a trade-off between generative quality and coherence-i.e., they struggle to generate realistic and diverse samples that, at the same time, are semantically consistent across modalities. A recent work shows that using a simple approximation to Hölder pooling as an aggregation method improves coherence over the SOTA MMVAE+, despite assuming a single shared representation across all modalities. Yet, it slightly compromises sample diversity. Inspired by this insight, we propose Hölder++, a novel multimodal VAE that improves the generative quality-coherence trade-off through: (i) the first implementation of Hölder pooling without any approximation for multimodal VAEs; (ii) an extended architecture that models distinct shared and private (i.e., modality-specific) representations (Hölder+); and (iii) hierarchical inference that further enhances the disentanglement between the shared and private representations (Hölder++). Our experiments corroborate that Hölder++ consistently improves the generative quality-coherence trade-off, yields more structured latent spaces, and learns shared representations that are informative for downstream tasks.

</details>


### [117] [PolyFlow: Safe and Efficient Polytope-Constrained Flow Matching with Constraint Embedding and Projection-free Update](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13400)
*Jianming Ma,Qiyue Yang,Yang Zhang,Liyun Yan,Zhanxiang Cao,Yazhou Zhang,Yue Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PolyFlow框架，通过将多面体约束直接嵌入流模型，在保证零约束违反的同时提高了推理效率，实现了安全性、效率和生成质量的良好平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管基于流的生成模型在众多领域表现优异，但在安全关键的物理系统中部署仍面临严格约束要求的挑战。现有方法通常通过事后校正来强制执行安全性，这会导致巨大的计算开销并可能扭曲学习到的分布。

Method: 提出PolyFlow，一个多面体约束流匹配框架，将约束直接嵌入模型和流动动力学中。引入离散时间流公式和无投影架构，消除了离散化误差，无需昂贵的迭代求解器即可保证对任意多面体约束的严格满足。

Result: 实验结果表明，PolyFlow在各类规划和控制任务中实现了零约束违反，同时保持了高分布保真度。与最先进的约束生成基线相比，PolyFlow显著降低了推理延迟，并在安全性、效率和生成质量之间展现了良好的权衡。

Conclusion: PolyFlow为安全关键系统中的约束生成问题提供了一种高效且可靠的解决方案，通过消除事后校正的需要，成功解决了计算开销和分布扭曲的问题。

Abstract: While flow-based generative models have demonstrated strong performance across a wide range of domains, deploying them in safety-critical physical systems remains challenging due to strict constraint requirements. Existing approaches typically enforce safety through post-hoc corrections, which incur substantial computational overhead and may distort the learned distribution. We propose PolyFlow, a polytope-constrained flow matching framework that embeds constraints directly into the model and flow dynamics. PolyFlow introduces a discrete-time flow formulation and a projection-free architecture, which eliminate the discretization error and guarantee strict satisfaction of arbitrary polyhedral constraints, without the need for expensive iterative solvers. Experimental results show that PolyFlow achieves zero constraint violation while maintaining high distributional fidelity across a range of planning and control tasks. Compared to state-of-the-art constrained generation baselines, PolyFlow significantly reduces inference latency and demonstrates a favorable trade-off between safety, efficiency, and generative quality. Code is available on https://github.com/MJianM/PolyFlow.

</details>


### [118] [Accelerating Speculative Diffusions via Block Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13426)
*Alexander Soen,Hisham Husain,Valentin De Bortoli,Arnaud Doucet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对扩散模型的高效推测采样方案，实现了块验证机制以提高草稿接受率，并结合无需训练的Free Drafter实现了最高6.3%的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的推测解码方法在离散空间（如LLM）中表现良好，但难以直接应用于连续扩散模型。核心难点在于：连续空间中的残差分布采样效率低下，导致现有方法要么计算效率低，要么采用替代方案而无法实现块验证。

Method: 提出了一种针对扩散模型的原始推测采样机制的高效实现方案，使块验证（block verification）能够从LLM迁移至扩散模型；同时形式化并分析了Free Drafter——一种无需训练的自推测草稿生成器。

Result: 所提方法在无需额外训练且开销极小的情况下，相比现有推测方法实现了最高6.3%的加速；块验证机制显著提升了草稿接受率。

Conclusion: 该工作成功将LLM中的块验证机制迁移至扩散模型，解决了连续空间残差采样的效率瓶颈，Free Drafter作为无需训练的方案证明了其实用价值。

Abstract: Speculative decoding speeds up LLM inference by using a draft model to generate tokens, with an acceptance-rejection scheme that ensures that the output matches the target distribution. Adapting this to continuous diffusions is difficult because speculative sampling requires drawing from a residual distribution. While straightforward in discrete spaces, efficiently sampling this residual in continuous space is non-trivial. Consequently, existing diffusion adaptations either use computationally inefficient sampling techniques or rely on an alternative scheme. In this work, we introduce a novel scheme that efficiently implements the original speculative sampling mechanism for diffusion models. Our approach offers a critical advantage over current methods: it enables us to adapt block verification from LLMs to diffusions -- which provably improves the acceptance rate of drafts. Furthermore, we formalize and analyze the Free Drafter, a heuristic self-speculative drafter for diffusions that requires no training. By enabling block verification, our Free Drafter yields up to a 6.3% speedup over existing speculative methods with no additional training and negligible overhead beyond the existing parallel verification pass.

</details>


### [119] [How Much Memory Do We Need? Adaptive Memory Gate for Neural Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13443)
*Jihyeon Hur,Yongseok Kwon,Min-Gi Jo,Jeongwhan Choi,Noseong Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出AMGFNO方法，通过可学习门控机制动态调节记忆权重，解决了现有记忆增强神经算子在多分辨率和物理参数设置下的自适应问题，在低分辨率下实现了55-79%的nRMSE降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆增强神经算子采用固定的记忆权重，无法根据分辨率、粘度等观测条件进行自适应调整。初步实验表明最优记忆权重随分辨率和粘度变化，固定权重方法难以在多样化设置下同时优化性能。

Method: 提出AMGFNO（自适应记忆门控傅里叶神经算子），通过可学习的门控机制动态调节记忆权重，使模型能够根据输入条件自动调整记忆利用程度。

Result: 在Kuramoto-Sivashinsky和Burgers方程上，AMGFNO在低分辨率下实现55-79%的nRMSE降低；学习到的门控值随分辨率增加自动从约0.7降至接近零，验证了自适应机制的有效性。

Conclusion: 动态记忆权重调节机制显著提升了神经算子在不同观测条件下的适应性和性能，证明了条件自适应策略在求解时间依赖PDEs问题中的重要价值。

Abstract: Neural operators have emerged as a powerful data-driven approach for solving time-dependent PDEs. Among recent advances, memory-augmented neural operators explicitly incorporate past states and have achieved remarkable performance under low-resolution observation settings. However, existing approaches apply a fixed memory weight regardless of observation conditions, such as resolution or physical parameters, limiting their adaptability. Our preliminary experiments reveal that optimal memory weight varies with resolution and viscosity, implying that a fixed memory weight cannot simultaneously optimize performance across diverse settings. We propose AMGFNO, which dynamically modulates memory weight through a learnable gate. On the Kuramoto-Sivashinsky and Burgers' equations, AMGFNO achieves 55-79% nRMSE reduction over at low resolution, with the learned gate value automatically decreasing from $\bar{g} \approx 0.7$ to near-zero as resolution increases.

</details>


### [120] [Clustering Node Attributed Networks with Graph Neural Networks and Self Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13444)
*Rodrigo de Sapienza Luna,Daniel Ratton Figueiredo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于图神经网络的迭代自学习框架，通过多轮次的节点表示学习和聚类相互增强，同时利用网络结构和节点属性信息，在无监督设置下实现更优的图聚类效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统图聚类方法仅利用网络结构信息，而现实数据中的节点属性同样包含有价值的信息。虽然已有方法尝试同时利用这两种信息，但如何有效整合仍面临挑战。现有基于GNN的图聚类方法通常是单轮训练，未能充分挖掘网络边和节点属性的互补信息。

Method: 提出一个多轮次自学习框架：每轮中，GNN生成节点表示用于聚类；聚类结果影响下一轮的图结构；引入上下文图来辅助节点表示生成。整个过程在完全无监督设置下迭代进行，节点表示学习和聚类相互增强。

Result: 在合成数据上，该方法能有效提取网络边和节点属性信息，当单一信息源不够充分时，性能优于仅关注网络或属性的方法；多轮学习持续提升性能，始终优于长时间单轮训练的经典GNN聚类方法；在真实数据集上，对于平衡聚类场景，该方法与最先进方法具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 所提出的迭代自学习框架成功实现了网络结构信息和节点属性信息的有效整合，通过多轮次的协同优化机制显著提升了图聚类性能，为属性图聚类问题提供了一种有效的无监督解决方案。

Abstract: Graph clustering - partitioning the node set of a graph into disjoint subsets that reflect some latent information - is a fundamental problem as it finds applications in a myriad of different scenarios. While this classic problem has been tackled for decades by different communities, a recent variation of the problem driven by real data considers the scenario where nodes have attributes that are also informative. This has triggered novel methods that simultaneously leverage network information (edges) and node information (attributed) in the design of novel clustering algorithms. This work proposes a novel framework that builds on prior works that have applied graph neural networks (GNN) to graph clustering. The proposed framework operates in rounds of self learning in a fully unsupervised setting. In each round, a GNN generates representations for nodes that are used to cluster the nodes. This clustering influences the graph used to generate the node representation in the next round. Moreover, a context graph built in each round using the original graph is used to generate the node representations. Empirical results show that the proposed methodology extracts information from both network edges and node attributes in synthetic data, outperforming algorithms focused solely on the network or attributes when neither are very informative. Multiple rounds of learning also improve the performance and always outperforms a long single round of training (i.e., classic GNN graph clustering). When considering real datasets, empirical results indicate that the proposed methodology is competitive to state-of-the-art methods when cluster sizes are balanced.

</details>


### [121] [Uncertainty Estimation for Molecular Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13451)
*Paul Seij,Christian A. Naesseth,Stephan Mandt,Metod Jazbec*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种后验方法，通过拉普拉斯近似估计分子扩散模型的不确定性，该不确定性分数能有效识别低质量生成样本，并可通过过滤样本提升模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的3D分子生成扩散模型缺乏判断生成样本质量的可靠信号，无法有效识别低质量的生成分子。

Method: 基于预训练去噪网络的拉普拉斯近似，测量生成轨迹中噪声预测的变异性，从而计算每个样本的不确定性分数。

Result: 提出的不确定性分数与样本质量呈负相关，能够有效反映样本质量；利用该分数过滤生成样本可以在测试时扩展中提升模型性能。

Conclusion: 该方法为分子扩散模型提供了有效的质量评估信号，通过不确定性引导的样本过滤策略可以显著改善生成质量。

Abstract: Diffusion models have seen wide adoption for 3D molecular generation, yet they offer no principled signal of when a generated molecule is likely to be of low quality. We propose a post-hoc method for estimating per-sample uncertainty in pretrained molecular diffusion models. Building on a Laplace approximation of the denoising network, we measure the variability of the noise prediction across the generation trajectory. Empirically, we show that the resulting uncertainty score is informative of sample quality, exhibiting a negative correlation with established sample-level quality metrics. We further study how the proposed uncertainty score can be used to filter generated samples, improving model performance via test-time scaling.

</details>


### [122] [Reinforcement Learning for Neural Model Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13461)
*Shaivi Malik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将神经网络模型编辑问题转化为强化学习问题的探索性框架，通过智能体在MaskWorld和ShiftWorld两个环境中学习编辑策略，实现了在机器遗忘任务中将遗忘集准确率降至接近0%并保持保留集90%以上准确率，在偏见缓解任务中提升偏见相关性能超过5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编辑预训练神经网络需要针对特定目标定制专门算法，设计此类算法往往耗时费力且需要大量努力。现有方法缺乏通用性，每个新任务都需要重新设计专门的编辑算法。

Method: 将神经模型编辑形式化为强化学习问题，智能体通过奖励反馈修改模型。引入两个环境：MaskWorld（乘法缩放权重）和ShiftWorld（加法权重更新）。奖励函数结合效用保留目标与任务特定编辑目标，使智能体能够在保持整体模型性能的同时学习针对性修改。在文本分类偏见缓解和图像分类机器遗忘两个任务上进行评估。

Result: 在机器遗忘任务中，学习到的策略将遗忘集准确率降至接近0%，同时保持保留集准确率超过90%。在偏见缓解设置中，学习到的策略将偏见相关性能提升超过5%，同时保持一般分类效用。

Conclusion: 神经模型编辑可以被转化为强化学习问题，允许编辑策略从奖励反馈中学习，而非针对每个任务手动工程化设计。这为模型编辑提供了一种新的通用范式，降低了对专门算法设计的依赖。

Abstract: Editing pretrained neural networks requires specialized algorithms tailored to specific objectives. Designing such algorithms is often time-consuming and demands significant effort. We present an exploratory framework that formulates neural model editing as a reinforcement learning problem, where agents modify models using reward feedback. We introduce two environments: MaskWorld, where agents scale weights multiplicatively, and ShiftWorld, where agents apply additive weight updates. The reward function combines a utility-preservation objective with a task-specific editing objective, enabling agents to learn targeted modifications while maintaining overall model performance. We evaluate the framework on bias mitigation in text classification and machine unlearning in image classification, both of which traditionally rely on specialized algorithms. Our results show that the learned policies reduce forget set accuracy to nearly 0% while preserving over 90% retain set accuracy on the unlearning task. In the bias mitigation setting, the learned policies improve bias-related performance by more than 5% while maintaining general classification utility. Our findings show that neural model editing can be cast as a reinforcement learning problem, allowing editing policies to be learned from reward feedback rather than manually engineered for each task.

</details>


### [123] [MaxProof: Scaling Mathematical Proof with Generative-Verifier RL and Population-Level Test-Time Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13473)
*Jiacheng Chen,Xinyu Zhang,Shunkai Zhang,Yanmohan Wang,Lin Li,Tiancheng Qin,Qin Wang,Zhengmao Zhu,Tianle Li,Jingyang Li,Zehan Li,Binyang Jiang,Jin Zhu,Han Ding,Fei Yu,Chenyu Du,Zijian Song,Jiayuan Song,Zhi Zhang,Yunan Huang,Weiyu Cheng,Pengyu Zhao,Yu Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了MaxProof，一个针对竞赛级数学证明的群体级测试时扩展框架。M3模型通过防御纵深生成验证器训练三种证明能力，在测试时将模型作为生成器、验证器、优化器和排序器，在候选证明群体中搜索并返回最终证明。MaxProof使M3模型在IMO 2025达到35/42分，在USAMO 2026达到36/42分，均超过人类金牌阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 竞赛级数学证明对现有大语言模型构成重大挑战，需要精确的逻辑推理和验证能力。传统方法难以同时保证证明生成的正确性和质量。因此需要开发一种能够整合证明生成、验证和修复能力的框架，并通过测试时扩展策略提升模型在复杂数学证明任务上的表现。

Method: 1. 训练阶段：M3模型训练三种证明导向能力——证明生成、证明验证和批评条件的证明修复，使用防御纵深生成验证器确保低假阳性率；2. 整合发布：将三种能力合并为单一M3模型；3. 测试时扩展：MaxProof将模型视为生成器、验证器、优化器和排序器，在候选证明群体中搜索，通过锦标赛选择机制返回最终证明。

Result: MaxProof测试时扩展框架使M3模型在IMO 2025上达到35/42分，在USAMO 2026上达到36/42分，两项成绩均超过人类金牌获奖阈值，展示了该框架在竞赛级数学证明任务上的卓越性能。

Conclusion: MaxProof证明了测试时扩展策略在竞赛级数学证明领域的有效性。通过整合多种证明能力并采用群体搜索与锦标赛选择机制，可以显著提升模型的证明质量，为AI在高级数学推理领域的应用开辟了新路径。

Abstract: We present MaxProof, a population-level test-time scaling framework for competition-level mathematical proof in the MiniMax-M3 series. M3 first trains three proof-oriented capabilities -- proof generation, proof verification, and critique-conditioned proof repair -- using a defense-in-depth generative verifier engineered for low false-positive rate. These capabilities are merged into a single released M3 model. At test time, MaxProof treats the model as a generator, verifier, refiner, and ranker, searches over a population of candidate proofs, and returns one final proof through tournament selection. With MaxProof test-time scaling, the M3 model reaches 35/42 on IMO 2025 and 36/42 on USAMO 2026, exceeding the human gold-medal threshold on both.

</details>


### [124] [SupraBench: A Benchmark for Supramolecular Chemistry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13477)
*Tianyi Ma,Yijun Ma,Zehong Wang,Weixiang Sun,Ziming Li,Connor R. Schmidt,Chuxu Zhang,Matthew J. Webber,Yanfang Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文发布了首个超分子化学基准测试SupraBench，包含结合亲和力预测等四项核心任务，并构建了1600万词元的语料库SupraPMC。评估结果显示当前LLMs在超分子化学推理任务上存在显著提升空间，且不同任务类型呈现出差异化的失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超分子化学中的主客体系统设计耗时巨大（每对候选分子需数天的干实验验证），虽然LLMs在分子结合任务中展现出快速替代潜力，但目前缺乏系统性评估LLMs在超分子化学基础推理任务（如结合亲和力预测）上表现的基准测试。

Method: 1）与领域专家合作构建SupraBench基准测试，涵盖结合亲和力预测、最佳结合体选择、溶剂识别、主客体描述四项基础任务及分子识别视觉辅助任务；2）发布SupraPMC语料库（1600万词元，源自Europe PMC的超分子化学文献）；3）全面评估开源与闭源LLMs表现；4）基于SupraPMC进行领域适应预训练实验。

Result: LLMs在所有任务上均存在显著提升空间；领域适应预训练可有效迁移至分布内回归任务，但会牺牲严格的字母格式输出能力；不同任务类型难度差异显著，揭示了当前超分子化学推理中特定的缺陷模式。

Conclusion: 当前LLMs在超分子化学推理能力上存在明显不足，不同任务家族呈现差异化的失败模式，表明需要针对性地改进模型在该领域的推理能力。SupraBench为后续研究提供了系统性的评估框架。

Abstract: Supramolecular chemistry, which includes the study of non-covalent host-guest assemblies, has advanced various applications. However, designing host-guest systems remains time-consuming, requiring days of dry-lab verification per candidate pair. Although LLMs have emerged as a fast alternative with strong performance on molecular binding tasks, no benchmark currently systematically evaluates LLMs for host-guest reasoning across fundamental supramolecular chemistry tasks, e.g., binding affinity prediction. To this end, we collaborate with domain experts to release the first Supramolecular Benchmark, called SupraBench, to evaluate LLMs in chemistry reasoning. Specifically, we design four fundamental tasks, i.e., binding affinity prediction, top-binder selection, solvent identification, and host-guest description, plus an auxiliary vision-based task for molecular identification. We also release SupraPMC, a curated 16M-token corpus of Supramolecular chemistry articles distilled from Europe PMC, to support the adaptation to the supramolecular domain. We benchmark a broad range of open and proprietary LLMs and find that LLMs leave substantial headroom across all tasks. Domain adaptation pretraining over SupraPMC transfers cleanly to in-distribution regression but trades off against strict letter-format output. Moreover, the difficulty profile differs sharply across task families, revealing distinct failure modes that indicate specific gaps in current supramolecular chemistry reasoning. Our source codes and benchmark datasets are available at https://github.com/Tianyi-Billy-Ma/SupraBench.

</details>


### [125] [CRAFTIIF: Cross-Resolution Analytic Four-Type Interpretable Isolation Forest for Multivariate Time Series Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13486)
*William Smits*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CRAFTIIF框架，首次全面针对多变量时间序列的四种异常类型（点、分布、时序、集体）进行检测，在mTSBench基准上VUS-PR指标达到0.463，较先前最佳方法提升40.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多变量时间序列异常检测面临四种结构不同的异常类型挑战：点异常（孤立尖峰）、分布异常（水平偏移）、时序异常（节律变化）和集体异常（传感器间相关性破坏），现有无监督方法仅针对其中一两种类型且可解释性有限。

Method: CRAFTIIF框架生成500个随机解析小波特征（覆盖Morlet、DOG、Haar、Coiflet四个小波族），每种异常类型对应特定特征，输入五个结构化孤立森林——每类型一个外加一个用于复合异常的元IF。采用自适应Otsu/MAD阈值自动校准检测，因每个IF仅训练类型特定特征，分支触发即可直接提供异常类型归因。

Result: 在mTSBench基准全部19个数据集上平均F1=0.228（13个可检测数据集上F1=0.322），VUS-PR指标排名第一（0.463 vs. 先前最佳0.329，提升40.7%）。消融实验证实自适应阈值（+38% F1）、四分支结构（+20%）和元IF（+23%）均为关键贡献。诊断框架识别出19个数据集中有6个对任何无监督方法均不可检测。

Conclusion: CRAFTIIF成功实现了对四种异常类型的统一检测框架，无需数据集特定调参，通过结构设计天然提供可解释性，在基准测试中取得显著性能提升，为无监督时间序列异常检测提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Anomaly detection in multivariate time series is challenged by four structurally distinct anomaly types -- point (isolated spikes), distributional (level shifts), temporal (rhythm changes), and collective (inter-sensor correlation breakdowns) -- each requiring different feature representations. Most unsupervised methods target only one or two types and provide limited interpretability. We present CRAFTIIF (Cross-Resolution Analytic Four-Type Interpretable Isolation Forest), a fully unsupervised framework targeting all four types without dataset-specific tuning. CRAFTIIF generates K=500 random analytic wavelet feature draws across four families (Morlet, DOG, Haar, Coiflet), each targeting a specific anomaly type, feeding five structured Isolation Forests -- one per type plus a meta-IF for compound anomalies. An adaptive Otsu/MAD threshold calibrates detection automatically across anomaly rates from 0.1% to 69.2%. Because each IF is trained exclusively on type-specific features, branch firing provides direct anomaly-type attribution by construction, without post-hoc explanation. Evaluated on all 19 datasets of the mTSBench benchmark (Zhou et al., TMLR 2026), CRAFTIIF achieves mean F1=0.228 (all 19 datasets) and F1=0.322 (13 detectable datasets), ranking first among all 25 evaluated methods on VUS-PR (0.463 vs. previous best 0.329, +40.7%). A diagnostic framework -- oracle F1, detectability limits, and branch separation ratios -- identifies 6 of 19 datasets as fundamentally undetectable by any unsupervised method. Ablation over 11 conditions confirms adaptive thresholding (+38% F1), four-branch structure (+20%), and meta-IF (+23%) are each essential. Code: https://github.com/smitswil/craftiif

</details>


### [126] [A2D2: Fine-Tuning Any-Length Discrete Diffusion for Adaptive Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13565)
*Sophia Tang,Yuchen Zhu,Molei Tao,Pranam Chatterjee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了A2D2框架，实现了任意长度离散扩散模型的奖励引导微调，通过联合优化插入和去掩码策略，理论上保证收敛到奖励倾斜分布，实验证明优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散扩散模型已被扩展到任意长度设置，但针对任意长度离散扩散的原则性奖励引导微调方法仍 largely未被探索，缺乏有效的理论框架和优化策略。

Method: 推导了联合插入-去掩码路径测度的Radon-Nikodym导数，实现了无需目标样本即可理论上保证收敛到奖励倾斜序列分布；提出了自适应联合解码(AJD)损失，通过最小化解码误差来获得生成奖励倾斜分布的最优路径测度。

Result: A2D2在奖励优化方面表现优异，相比先前的固定长度微调和推理时引导方法，在生成灵活性和准确性方面均有显著提升。

Conclusion: A2D2为任意长度离散扩散模型提供了一个统一的理论框架，通过联合优化策略和质量推理调度，成功解决了奖励引导微调的挑战，为序列生成任务提供了更优的解决方案。

Abstract: Discrete diffusion models offer a simple and stable likelihood-based framework for sequence generation, recently extended to any-length settings via token insertion. Principled reward-guided fine-tuning for any-length discrete diffusion, however, remains largely unexplored. We introduce Fine-Tuning Any-Length Discrete Diffusion for Adaptive Decoding (A2D2), a unified framework for reward-guided fine-tuning of any-length discrete diffusion models via joint optimization of the insertion and unmasking policies together with a quality-based inference schedule. We derive the Radon-Nikodym derivative for the joint insertion-unmasking path measures, enabling theoretically guaranteed convergence to the intractable reward-tilted sequence distribution without requiring target samples. Building on this, we establish unmasking and insertion quality as tractable approaches for minimizing decoding error and introduce the Adaptive Joint Decoding (AJD) loss, which provably yields the optimal path measure that generates the reward-tilted distribution. Empirically, A2D2 improves reward optimization while enhancing generation flexibility and accuracy over prior fixed-length fine-tuning and inference-time guidance methods.

</details>


### [127] [Adjusted Cup-Product Neural Layer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13568)
*Snigdha Chandan Khilar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文引入了调整杯积神经网络层，通过硬编码杯积与来自高阶规范理论的调整项，构造了设计上即规范不变的读出机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理学和几何学中许多重要的观测量都是上链的杯积，研究者希望设计一种神经网络原语，使其输出在规范变换下天生具有不变性，从而提升模型在该类问题上的表现与可解释性。

Method: 提出了调整杯积神经网络层，将杯积与一个来自高阶规范理论的调整项在结构中硬编码结合，构建出具有规范不变性的读出模块。

Result: 理论证明：在闭链上，输出完全依赖于调整系数；当调整系数为零时，无论其他参数如何，输出都被完全消除。因此，调整项是规范不变信号的唯一来源；进一步证明了该观测量是一个非零二次型，并在一重和二重规范变换下精确不变。

Conclusion: 调整杯积神经网络层成功地将规范不变性硬编码到神经网络结构中，为物理和几何问题提供了具有理论保障的规范不变读出机制，具有重要的理论意义与应用潜力。

Abstract: Many important observables in physics and geometry are cup products of cochains. The adjusted cup product neural layer has been introduced in this paper. It is a neural primitive that hard wires the cup product with an adjustment term from higher gauge theory. This creates a readout that is gauge invariant by design. Their main theoretical result shows that on a closed cycle the output relies entirely on the adjustment coefficient. Setting this coefficient to zero removes the output completely regardless of other parameters. Thus the adjustment is the only source of gauge invariant signal. They prove this observable is a nonzero quadratic form and is exactly invariant under one and two gauge transformations.

</details>


### [128] [Existence Precedes Value: Joint Modeling of Observational Existence and Evolving States in Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13571)
*Yifan Hu,Hongzhou Chen,Peiyuan Liu,Yiding Liu,Zewei Dong,Jiang-Ming Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文针对现实时间序列数据的不完整性和不规则性，提出了Timeflies框架，将预测问题重新表述为未来可观测性推断与数值估计的联合问题，并构建了Shadow基准和OVJE评估指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实时间序列数据常因传感器休眠、传输延迟和事件驱动采样而高度不完整和不规则。现有方法（从先插补后预测流程到神经ODE等连续时间模型）虽然在建模历史不规则性方面有所改进，但在推理时仍依赖一个隐式的预言机假设：假定未来有效观测的时间戳已知。这一假设限制了实际应用价值，因为在许多真实系统中，更根本的问题不仅是预测未来数值，还包括判断未来是否会出现有效观测。

Method: 提出Timeflies统一框架，采用观测流和数值流双流架构，通过三个专用模块进行耦合：可靠性感知嵌入模块、观测引导的依赖建模模块和联合预测模块。该框架显式建模观测动态与状态演化之间的交互关系，同时推断未来可观测性和预测数值。此外，构建了Shadow基准数据集（结合公开数据集的自然缺失性和真实工业数据），并引入观测-数值联合熵（OVJE）指标来综合评估这种耦合的可预测性。

Result: 大量实验表明，Timeflies在各项指标上持续优于现有方法，验证了在具有缺失值的时间序列预测中显式建模未来可观测性的重要性。

Conclusion: 该研究强调了在处理缺失值的时间序列预测任务中，显式建模未来可观测性的关键作用，为解决现实世界中的不规则时间序列预测问题提供了新的思路和有效的技术方案。

Abstract: Real-world time series are often highly incomplete and irregular due to sensor dormancy, transmission delays, and event-driven sampling, making reliable forecasting fundamentally challenging. Existing methods have evolved from impute-then-forecast pipelines to continuous-time models such as Neural ODEs and continuous-time graph networks. While these approaches improve the modeling of historical irregularity, they still rely on an implicit oracle assumption at inference time: the timestamps of future valid observations are presumed to be known in advance. This assumption limits practical relevance, since in many real systems the more fundamental question is not only what the future value will be, but also whether a valid observation will occur at all. In this paper, we propose Timeflies, a unified framework that reformulates forecasting as a joint problem of future observability inference and value estimation. To explicitly model the interaction between observation dynamics and state evolution, Timeflies adopts an observation stream and a value stream, coupled through three dedicated modules for reliability-aware embedding, observation-guided dependency modeling, and joint prediction. We further construct Shadow, a benchmark that combines natural missingness from public datasets with real-world industrial data, and introduce the Observation-Value Joint Entropy (OVJE) metric to comprehensively evaluate this coupled predictability. Extensive experiments show that Timeflies consistently outperforms existing methods, highlighting the importance of explicitly modeling future observability in time series forecasting with missing values. Code and dataset are available in https://github.com/ant-intl/Timeflies.

</details>


### [129] [Learning with Simulators: No Regret in a Computationally Bounded World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13576)
*Sasha Voitovych,Abhishek Shetty,Noah Golowich,Alexander Rakhlin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了可模拟过程框架，通过引入模拟器来处理强相关数据，在依赖数据环境下恢复了基于VC维的经典学习保证，并展示了条件采样的统计和计算优势，实现了对传统PAC模型的重要扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习理论中的一个基本问题是理解泛化所需的最小假设。然而，现有结果大多依赖于数据生成过程的独立性假设（或其代理），对于强相关数据的结果非常有限。这种理论与实践之间的差距促使作者寻找新的框架来处理依赖数据的学习问题。

Method: 作者引入了可模拟过程（simulatable processes）框架，学习者可以访问一个模拟器来近似生成数据的分布（可能是任意复杂和依赖的过程）。通过这个模拟器，作者研究了学习保证的恢复、条件采样的能力，以及设计能够同时学习所有在有限多项式时间内可采样过程的单一算法。

Result: （1）在可模拟过程框架下，成功恢复了与独立数据经典设置相同的学习保证，即依赖VC维的错误边界；（2）证明了条件采样设置下的严格统计和计算优势；（3）展示了一个单一算法能够同时学习任意给定的VC类，适用于所有在有限多项式时间内可采样的过程，其遗憾由过程的有限时间Kolmogorov复杂性控制。

Conclusion: 本研究显著拓宽了经典PAC模型的概念范围，为处理强相关数据提供了新的理论工具。可模拟过程框架不仅弥合了独立数据与依赖数据学习的理论差距，还揭示了模拟器和条件采样在学习中的强大作用，为学习理论基础研究开辟了新方向。

Abstract: Understanding the minimal assumptions necessary for generalization is the fundamental question in learning theory. Unfortunately, most results rely heavily on independence (or some proxy thereof) of the data-generating process, while results for strongly dependent data are far more limited. Towards addressing this gap, we introduce the framework of simulatable processes, where the learner has access to a simulator that approximates the distribution generating the data (which may be an arbitrarily complex and dependent process). Surprisingly, given access to such a simulator, we show that we can recover the same learning guarantees as in the classical setting with independent data, namely, error bounds that depend on the VC dimension. Further, we use this framework to study the power of conditional sampling and show strict statistical and computational advantages in this setting. As a highlight of our framework, we exhibit a single algorithm that simultaneously learns any given VC class under all processes samplable in bounded polynomial time, with regret controlled by the time-bounded Kolmogorov complexity of the process. This provides a significant conceptual broadening of the classical PAC model.

</details>


### [130] [Simplex-Constrained Sparse Bagging: Transitioning from Uniform Priors to Sparse Posteriors in Ensemble Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13589)
*Meher Sai Preetam,Meher Bhaskar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了SCSB框架，通过联合优化集成剪枝和概率校准，在保持或提升泛化精度的同时实现了高达96%的集成压缩和更好的概率校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Bagging集成方法对所有基估计器分配均匀投票权重，这种朴素均匀先验忽略了基估计器局部能力的差异，并导致模型过度自信，需要一种方法来优化集成剪枝和概率校准。

Method: 通过最小化Out-Of-Bag损失，在概率单纯形上构建集成剪枝和校准的联合优化问题；引入凹二次惩罚项来解决"L1-单纯形悖论"（L1范数在单纯形上恒定无法剪枝的理论问题），从而诱导稀疏性。

Result: SCSB实现了高达96%的集成压缩，带来线性推理加速；降低了Expected Calibration Error，获得更优的概率校准；同时保持或提升泛化精度。

Conclusion: SCSB是一种模型无关的数学严格框架，有效解决了bagging集成的压缩和校准问题，在实际应用中具有显著优势。

Abstract: We present Simplex-Constrained Sparse Bagging (SCSB), a mathematically rigorous framework for post-training compression and probability calibration of bootstrap-based bagging ensembles. Standard bagging ensembles (such as Random Forests, Bagged SVMs, and Bagged Neural Networks) assign uniform voting power to all constituent estimators. However, this naive uniform prior ignores the varying local competence of base estimators and contributes to model overconfidence. We formulate ensemble pruning and calibration as a joint optimization problem over the probability simplex by minimizing the Out-Of-Bag (OOB) loss. To induce sparsity, we address the theoretical "L1-simplex paradox" -- the mathematical reality that the L1 norm is constant on the simplex and fails to prune -- by introducing a concave quadratic penalty. SCSB is model-agnostic and achieves up to 96% ensemble compression, yielding linear inference speedups and superior probability calibration (lowered Expected Calibration Error) while preserving or enhancing generalization accuracy.

</details>


### [131] [Beyond the Commitment Boundary: Probing Epiphenomenal Chain-of-Thought in Large Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13603)
*Daniel Scalena,Sara Candussio,Luca Bortolussi,Elisabetta Fersini,Malvina Nissim,Gabriele Sarti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出“承诺边界”概念，推理过程在此边界经历从中间猜测到稳定答案的急剧转变；基于此可实现CoT早退，平均缩短55%长度且对性能影响极小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 链式思维（CoT）推理是推理时扩展的主流范式，但人们对各推理步骤对最终答案的因果影响知之甚少；本文旨在量化每步的因果重要性并揭示答案在推理过程中的形成机制。

Method: 通过“早退”方式估计每一步的因果重要性，借助注意力探针在中间推理步骤上线性解码答案形成阶段；据此在承诺边界处提前退出推理块。

Result: 在多种任务与模型族中发现推理通常跨过一个“承诺边界”，该边界常在单步内发生，远早于推理块结束；边界之后的CoT步骤对最终答案概率几乎无影响（表象性步骤）；注意力探针能高准确度线性解码并泛化到未见推理任务；基于信号早退可将CoT长度平均减少55%且性能损失极小。

Conclusion: 答案形成在推理链中存在急剧且可识别的转变点，利用该信号可有效剪枝表象性推理步骤，显著提升推理效率而不损害表现。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning is the dominant paradigm for inference-time scaling in language models, yet the causal influence of individual steps on the final answer poorly understood. We estimate each step's causal importance via early exit and use this measure to study how answers form across the reasoning traces of several model families. Across diverse tasks, we find that reasoning typically crosses a \emph{commitment boundary} -- a sharp transition from transient intermediate guesses to a stable, high-confidence answer. This transition often happens in a single step, well before the model's reasoning block ends, and is followed by \emph{epiphenomenal} CoT steps that leave the final answer probability unaltered. Using attention probes, we show that answer-formation stages can be linearly decoded from intermediate reasoning steps with high accuracy and generalize robustly to unseen reasoning tasks. We exploit this signal to early-exit reasoning blocks at the commitment boundary, reducing the length of CoTs up to 55\% on average with negligible impact on model performance.

</details>


### [132] [The Stable Recovery Manifold: Geometric Principles Governing Recoverability in Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13637)
*Ayushman Trivedi,Bhavika Melwani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出稳定恢复流形假设，表明灾难性遗忘主要是可访问性和流形对齐问题而非信息破坏。通过引入恢复子空间维度指标，发现遗忘知识在表示重组后仍保持紧凑可解码性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于可访问性崩塌框架，研究持续学习中恢复性的几何结构，旨在理解灾难性遗忘的本质——是知识的破坏还是可访问性问题。

Method: 在Split CIFAR-100数据集上使用顺序训练的ResNet-18，分析十个任务的可恢复性、表示漂移和恢复复杂度；引入恢复子空间维度(k_t)指标，衡量保持90%探测性能所需的最小奇异方向数量。

Result: 恢复维度在整个训练过程中保持稳定（平均k_t=8.0），尽管存在显著的表示漂移；主角度漂移能强烈预测可恢复性(r=-0.862)，简单几何模型解释了82.2%的可恢复性方差。

Conclusion: 支持稳定恢复流形假设，表明遗忘知识尽管经历了表示重组，仍保持紧凑可解码性；灾难性遗忘主要是可访问性和流形对齐问题，而非信息破坏。

Abstract: Catastrophic forgetting is often viewed as the destruction of previously learned knowledge during sequential learning. Building on the Accessibility Collapse framework, we investigate the geometric structure of recoverability in continual learning. Using Split CIFAR-100 and a sequentially trained ResNet-18, we analyze recoverability, representational drift, and recovery complexity across ten tasks. We introduce Recovery Subspace Dimensionality (k_t), a measure of the minimum number of singular directions required to preserve 90 percent of full probe performance. Contrary to our Recoverability Diffusion hypothesis, recovery dimensionality remains stable throughout training (mean k_t = 8.0) despite substantial representational drift. Principal-angle drift strongly predicts recoverability (r = -0.862), and a simple geometric model explains 82.2 percent of recoverability variance. These findings support the Stable Recovery Manifold hypothesis, suggesting that forgotten knowledge remains compactly decodable despite representational reorganization. The results indicate that catastrophic forgetting is primarily an accessibility and manifold-alignment problem rather than information destruction.

</details>


### [133] [Dense Supervision, Sparse Updates: On the Sparsity and Geometry of On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13657)
*Guo Yu,Wenlin Liu,Yulan Hu,Hao-Xuan Ma,Jun-Peng Jiang,Han-Jia Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了On-Policy Distillation (OPD)的参数更新机制，发现OPD更新具有坐标稀疏性和谱集中性，仅训练发现的子网络即可恢复全量OPD性能，揭示了OPD保留了on-policy后训练的几何特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: On-Policy Distillation (OPD)作为一种重要的后训练方法，结合了on-policy学生轨迹和密集教师监督两个关键要素，但目前尚不清楚这种混合方法如何改变模型参数，因此需要深入分析其参数更新机制。

Method: 在多个语言模型和视觉-语言模型对及应用场景下，从稀疏性和几何特性两个维度分析OPD风格的参数更新：研究更新的坐标稀疏性、层级分布、FFN权重；通过训练子网络验证稀疏结构的有效性；对比SGD和AdamW优化器；分析更新的数值秩、谱分布及其与源权重的关系。

Result: 在稀疏性方面，OPD更新小且坐标稀疏，分布在各层且主要集中在FFN；仅训练发现的子网络可恢复全量OPD性能；SGD优化器表现不如AdamW，因为密集教师监督保留了异构的坐标梯度尺度。在几何特性方面，更新数值上满秩但谱集中，主要位于源权重主奇异子空间之外，且不成比例地落在源权重接近零的坐标上。

Conclusion: 密集教师监督并未将OPD转化为普通的密集参数重写，相反，OPD保留了on-policy后训练的重要几何特征，这为理解和改进知识蒸馏方法提供了新的见解。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (\textsc{OPD}) has recently become a prominent post-training recipe as it combines two desirable ingredients: on-policy student trajectories and dense teacher supervision, yet how this hybrid changes a model's parameters remains unclear. Across several language and vision-language model pairs and use cases, our analysis yields two main findings. On sparsity, \textsc{OPD}-style updates are small and coordinate-sparse. They are distributed across layers and are usually FFN-heavy. This sparse structure is operationally useful: training only the discovered subnetwork recovers nearly the same performance as full \textsc{OPD}. However, the sparsity-inducing SGD optimizer underperforms AdamW in our optimizer ablation, likely because dense teacher supervision preserves heterogeneous coordinate-wise gradient scales where AdamW's adaptive scaling remains useful. On geometry, the updates are numerically full-rank but spectrally concentrated; they lie mostly away from the principal singular subspaces of the source weights and fall disproportionately on coordinates where the source weights are close to zero. These findings suggest that dense teacher supervision does not turn \textsc{OPD} into ordinary dense parameter rewriting; instead, \textsc{OPD} retains important geometric signatures of on-policy post-training.

</details>


### [134] [Understanding Truncated Positional Encodings for Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13671)
*James Flora,Mitchell Black,Weng-Keen Wong,Amir Nayyeri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究了截断位置编码（PEs）的理论性质，发现截断后不同PEs家族的表达能力存在根本差异，且截断的谱PEs不再强于1-WL测试；实验表明混合使用多种截断PEs效果更佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管谱方法和基于游走的位置编码在完整形式下理论上等价，但实际应用中常使用截断版本（如前k个特征空间或邻接矩阵的低次幂），而这些截断PEs的理论性质此前未知。本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 理论分析截断位置编码的表达能力，证明截断后不同PEs家族存在表达能力差异；研究k-调和距离谱PEs家族以展示相近截断PEs间的表达能力差异；在真实数据集上进行实验验证。

Result: 截断后，多个位置编码家族的表达能力存在根本差异；截断的谱位置编码不再强于1-WL测试；实验表明混合使用多种截断位置编码优于单一编码家族。

Conclusion: 截断位置编码打破了完整版本的理论等价性，导致不同编码家族表达能力分化；实践中应采用混合策略以获得最佳性能。

Abstract: Positional encodings (PEs) enhance the power of graph neural networks (GNNs), both theoretically and empirically. Two of the most popular families of PEs - spectral (e.g., Laplacian eigenspaces, effective resistance) and walk-based (polynomials of the adjacency matrix) - are theoretically equivalent in expressive power, with expressivity between the 1-WL and 3-WL tests. However, this equivalence assumes the GNN uses the "complete" version of these PEs, which requires $O(n^3)$ time and space complexity. Instead, practitioners commonly use truncated variants of these encodings, such as the first $k$ eigenspaces or powers of the adjacency matrix. However, the theoretical properties of these truncated PEs are unknown. In this work, we initiate the study of these truncated PEs. Theoretically, we show that, under truncation, several families of PEs are fundamentally different in expressive power. As a corollary, we show that truncated spectral PEs are no longer stronger than the 1-WL test. We also study a family of spectral PEs, the $k$-harmonic distances, to highlight the differences in expressive power of even closely related truncated PEs. Finally, we experimentally show that a mix of truncated PEs is preferable to any single family on real-world datasets.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [135] [ToolSense: A Diagnostic Framework for Auditing Parametric Tool Knowledge in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12451)
*Ashutosh Hathidara,Sai Shruthi Sistla,Sebastian Schreiber,Sahil Bansal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ToolSense诊断框架，通过构建真实检索基准和多类探测任务，发现参数化工具检索模型存在"知识-检索解离"现象：在真实模糊查询下性能骤降50-64个百分点，部分模型在探测任务中接近随机表现，揭示了现有工具检索基准的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具检索基准使用冗长、完全明确的查询且采用受限解码评估，无法真实反映模型对工具的理解能力；参数化工具检索方法虽在标准基准上表现优异，但其实际工具理解程度存疑，需要更全面的诊断框架揭示模型真实能力。

Method: 提出ToolSense开源诊断框架，输入任意工具目录后自动生成三类基准：(1)包含三个模糊度层级的真实检索基准(RRB)；(2)MCQ探测基准；(3)QA探测基准。在ToolBench数据集(~47k工具)上评估五种参数化模型训练配置。

Result: 发现知识-检索解离现象：(1)在RRB查询上，多种配置相比完全明确的ToolBench基准性能崩溃50-64个百分点，甚至低于嵌入模型基线；(2)尽管检索性能强劲，部分模型在事实探测任务上得分接近随机水平，表明缺乏真实工具理解。

Conclusion: 参数化工具检索模型的优异性能可能具有欺骗性，真实场景下的模糊查询会暴露其局限性；现有的"先记忆后检索SFT"两阶段训练范式存在知识-检索解离问题，需要重新审视训练策略和评估方法，ToolSense框架为工具检索系统提供了更严格的诊断工具。

Abstract: Large language models deployed as agents over large tool catalogs face a critical tool-retrieval bottleneck. As embedding-based retrieval approaches rely on compact encoders that may under-capture specialized tool semantics, parametric tool retrieval addresses this by encoding each tool as a virtual token appended to the LLM vocabulary, fine-tuned in two stages (memorization then retrieval SFT) to use the LLM as a retriever, achieving strong performance on standard ToolBench retrieval benchmarks. Yet these benchmarks use verbose, fully-specified queries, and their evaluation applies constrained decoding that restricts outputs to valid token paths, neither reveals whether the model actually understands its tools. We introduce \textbf{ToolSense}, an open-source LLM-powered diagnostic framework that takes any tool catalog as input and automatically generates three benchmarks: a Realistic Retrieval Benchmark (RRB) with queries at three ambiguity tiers, an MCQ probing benchmark, and a QA probing benchmark. Applying ToolSense to ToolBench (~47k tools) and evaluating five parametric model training configurations reveals a knowledge-retrieval dissociation: on RRB queries, several configurations collapse by ~50-64 percentage points compared to fully-specified ToolBench benchmarks, falling below the embedding-model baseline. Additionally, despite strong retrieval performance, some models score near-random on factual probes, suggesting a knowledge-retrieval dissociation. We open-source the ToolSense framework and the ToolBench diagnostic benchmarks at https://github.com/SAP/toolsense.

</details>


### [136] [Reducing the Complexity of Deep Learning Models for EEG Analysis on Wearable Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12742)
*Farough Shayeste Roodi,Parham Zilouchian Moghaddam,Mahdi Mohammadi-nasab,Mehdi Modarressi,Mostafa Ersali Salehi Nasab,Masoud Daneshtalab*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨了在资源受限的可穿戴设备上部署深度神经网络(DNN)的可行性，通过参数量化和电极减少方法，在保持较高准确率的同时显著降低了模型复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可穿戴医疗设备是物联网领域增长最快的领域，但深度神经网络对计算能力、能耗和内存带宽的高需求与可穿戴设备的严格资源限制之间存在矛盾，阻碍了深度学习在实际可穿戴服务中的部署应用。

Method: 针对用于脑电信号分析（特别是癫痫发作检测）的多个最先进DNN模型，系统地研究了参数量化和电极减少方法在准确率与计算复杂度之间的权衡关系。

Result: 研究结果表明，当谨慎应用量化和电极减少技术时，可以显著降低DNN模型的复杂度，同时对准确率的负面影响极小，揭示了在可穿戴设备上部署DNN在线EEG分析时准确率与复杂度降低之间的明确权衡。

Conclusion: 通过合理应用模型压缩技术，在资源受限的可穿戴设备上部署深度学习模型进行EEG信号分析是可行的，为可穿戴医疗设备的实际应用提供了重要的技术指导和理论依据。

Abstract: Wearable healthcare devices are the fastest-growing Internet of Things (IoT) sector. Many automated healthcare services rely on two crucial biological signals, namely ECG and EEG, which reflect the activity of the heart and brain, respectively. Although deep neural networks are considered the primary way to process and analyze these signals, the very tight energy and computational power constraints in wearable devices are far below the computational, energy, and memory bandwidth demands of DNN models, thereby impeding the deployment of deep learning in many practical wearable services. This paper investigates the feasibility of deploying state-of-the-art DNN models in resource-constrained wearable devices. Notably, we explore the trade-off between accuracy and computational complexity of DNNs when parameter quantization and electrode reduction methods are used. Our investigation centers on several state-of-the-art DNN models designed for EEG signal analysis, specifically for detecting epileptic seizures. Our findings demonstrate that, when applied judiciously, these techniques can significantly reduce the complexity of the DNNs under consideration with minimal adverse effects on accuracy. These results reveal the explicit trade-offs between accuracy and complexity reduction encountered when adapting DNN-based online EEG analysis for wearable devices.

</details>


### [137] [Arbor: Tree Search as a Cognition Layer for Autonomous Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12563)
*Neha Prakriya,Chaojun Hou,Zheng Gong,Huasha Zhao,Xi Zhao,Mou Li,Zhenyu Gu,Emad Barsoum*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Arbor是一个多智能体框架，通过引入结构化树搜索作为认知层，实现了大语言模型推理优化的全栈自动化，获得高达193%的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自主优化系统在隔离目标上进行无状态评估，缺乏对复杂状态空间的有效探索。全栈LLM推理优化需要跨应用、框架、编译器、内核和硬件层的协调配合，传统方法依赖工程团队的长期协作。Arbor旨在通过显式的搜索树和结构化的多智能体协作，实现复杂优化任务的自主化。

Method: Arbor采用结构化树搜索作为共享工作记忆，维护带评分的假设搜索树，将失败作为诊断信号指导后续探索。框架包含Orchestrator智能体（协调领域专家进行优化）和Critic智能体（通过根因分析、内省和测量验证保障稳定性），形成制衡架构。智能体能力被分解为硬技能（领域专业知识）和软技能（协调协议），支持完全自主的多日优化活动。

Result: Arbor在LLM推理优化上实现了高达193%的吞吐量-延迟Pareto改进，超越供应商优化基线。相比之下，无框架的单智能体仅达到33%的吞吐量改进，且在数小时内发生不可恢复的崩溃。该方法跨多代硬件平台通用，运行间差异在2个百分点内，证明了硬件无关性和可复现性。

Conclusion: Arbor成功证明了结构化树搜索和多智能体制衡架构能够有效处理大规模、状态丰富的动作空间优化问题，实现了复杂系统工程任务的完全自主化，具有跨硬件平台的泛化能力和良好的可复现性。

Abstract: Arbor is a multi-agent framework that introduces structured tree search as a cognition layer for autonomous agents operating in large, stateful action spaces. Prior autonomous optimization systems operate on isolated targets with stateless evaluation. Arbor instead maintains an explicit search tree of scored hypotheses that serves as the shared working memory across agents, evolving with every measurement, treating failures as diagnostic signal that reshapes subsequent exploration, and expanding as prior successes shift the bottleneck distribution.
  We validate Arbor on full-stack LLM inference optimization, a domain where achieving peak performance has historically required coordinated effort from engineering teams across the application, framework, compiler, kernel, and hardware stack. Arbor pairs an Orchestrator agent, which drives optimization by delegating to Domain Specialists across the inference stack, with a Critic agent that safeguards stability through root-cause analysis, introspection, and measurement validation -- a checks-and-balances architecture where neither agent can unilaterally drive the system. Agent capabilities are decomposed into hard skills (domain expertise) and soft skills (coordination protocols that determine how contributions compose), enabling fully autonomous multi-day campaigns. Arbor achieves up to 193% inference throughput-latency Pareto improvement over vendor-optimized baselines, while a single agent without the harness plateaus at +33% throughput improvement and crashes irrecoverably within hours. Arbor generalizes to multiple generations of hardware platform, and run-to-run variance is within 2 percentage points demonstrating that the method is hardware-agnostic and reproducible.

</details>


### [138] [Strategic Decision Support for AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12587)
*Shayan Kiyani,Sima Noorani,George Pappas,Hamed Hassani*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对AI智能体的战略决策支持框架，通过阈值规则和在线算法在控制"错过支持错误"的同时最小化支持调用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统决策支持研究关注人类如何使用机器学习模型做出更好决策，但在现代智能体系统中角色发生逆转：AI智能体代表用户行动，而人类和工具成为其支持机制。这种角色反转带来可靠性问题，因为智能体错误可能产生重大后果，且其行为必须与人类目标和约束保持一致。

Method: 提出一个战略决策支持框架，将其建模为优化问题：在控制反事实"错过支持错误"（智能体本可以通过支持改善输出却独立行动的概率）的前提下最小化支持使用。证明最优策略是支持价值的阈值规则，并开发了在线算法来自适应地设定阈值，通过随机探索在无分布假设下控制错过支持错误。还引入了即时校准方法以减少在线不必要的支持调用。

Result: 在多种场景下（信息收集、人机协作、工具使用）验证了该方法，实验表明该方法能够可靠地控制目标错误，同时在实际应用中显著减少支持使用。

Conclusion: 该研究成功将决策支持原则扩展到以AI智能体为中心的场景，为AI智能体系统的可靠性保障提供了理论基础和实用算法框架。

Abstract: Traditionally, decision support studies how humans use machine learning models to make better decisions. In modern agentic systems, this division of roles is increasingly reversed: AI agents act on behalf of users, while humans and tools becomes support mechanisms around them. This role reversal brings reliability concerns to the forefront, since agentic errors can be consequential and agent behavior must remain aligned with human goals and constraints. Departing from the classical view of decision support, we revisit its two basic principles, the cost--value tradeoff of seeking support and the role of uncertainty quantification, in a setting where AI agents are the central actors. We propose a framework for strategic decision support for AI agents through an optimization problem that minimizes support usage subject to controlling a counterfactual missed-support error: the probability that the agent acts alone on instances where support would have materially improved its output. At the population level, we show that the optimal policy is a threshold rule on the value of support. Building on this structure, we develop an online algorithm that adaptively thresholds such a score and uses randomized exploration to control missed-support error without distributional assumptions. We further introduce a calibration-on-the-fly method that reduces unnecessary support calls online. We instantiate this framework across diverse scenarios, including information gathering, human--AI collaboration, and tool use, showing how each can be modeled through the same strategic decision-support lens. Experiments across these settings show that our method reliably controls the target error while substantially reducing support usage in practice.

</details>


### [139] [Pythagoras-Prover: Advancing Efficient Formal Proving via Augmented Lean Formalisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12594)
*Joshua Ong Jun Leang,Zheng Zhao,Mihaela Cătălina Stoian,Qiyuan Xu,Haonan Li,Wenda Li,Shay B. Cohen,Eleonora Giunchiglia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了Pythagoras-Prover，一个计算高效的Lean定理证明器家族，包含自回归模型（4B/32B参数）和首个扩散式证明器（4B），通过课程式SFT和ALF数据增强方法，在MiniF2F-Test上以167倍更少的参数超越DeepSeek-Prover-V2-671B。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代Lean定理证明器需要大量训练和推理计算资源，原因包括：验证过的证明数据稀缺、形式化证明搜索的推理轨迹较长，导致监督微调(SFT)和采样成本高昂。现有方法在有限计算预算下难以实现强性能。

Method: 1. 构建分层Lean验证语料库（简单/中等/困难），采用课程式SFT让模型从短到长、从简单到复杂渐进学习证明技能；2. 动态证明推理过滤机制，在SFT过程中保留信息量大的证明轨迹并控制在8k token预算内；3. 提出增强型Lean形式化方法(ALF)，通过自蒸馏扩展稀缺验证语料库，生成保持形式特征的变体问题；4. 开发了自回归模型（4B/32B）和首个扩散式证明器（4B）两种生成范式。

Result: Pythagoras-Prover-4B在MiniF2F-Test的pass@32指标上达到86.1%，超越DeepSeek-Prover-V2-671B的82.4%，参数量减少约167倍；Pythagoras-Prover-32B在MiniF2F-Test上达到93.0%的开源最优性能，在PutnamBench上解决93/672问题；在MiniF2F-ALF基准测试中，所有模型准确率下降，但32B模型保持最强，4B模型与Goedel-Prover-V2-32B相当。

Conclusion: 证明了在有限计算预算下可以构建高性能Lean定理证明器，通过课程式训练和ALF数据增强有效解决了数据稀缺和计算成本问题；扩散式证明器的初步成功为定理证明开辟了新方向；发布的MiniF2F-ALF基准提供了污染敏感的评估工具。

Abstract: Modern Lean theorem provers achieve strong performance only with substantial training and inference compute, driven in part by scarce verified proof data and the long reasoning traces of formal proof search, making both supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and sampling expensive. We introduce Pythagoras-Prover, a compute-efficient open-source family of Lean theorem provers built for practical compute budgets. The family spans two generation paradigms: autoregressive models at 4B and 32B parameters, and a first proof-of-concept diffusion-based prover (4B) that iteratively refines Lean proofs at inference time. For training efficiency, we build a Lean-verified corpus stratified into easy, medium, and hard problems for curriculum SFT, so models acquire proof skills progressively from shorter, simpler proofs to longer, harder ones. During SFT, a dynamic proof-reasoning filtering scheme preserves informative proof traces while keeping each instance within an 8k-token context budget. We also introduce Augmented Lean Formalisation (ALF), which expands scarce verified corpora into variants of formal statements, populated via self-distillation for extra training signal without formally verifying every mutated instance. By perturbing known problems while preserving their formal character, ALF reduces reliance on any statement's surface form. Empirically, Pythagoras-Prover-4B surpasses DeepSeek-Prover-V2-671B at pass@32 on MiniF2F-Test (86.1% vs 82.4%) with ~167x fewer parameters, while Pythagoras-Prover-32B sets the open-source state of the art at 93.0% on MiniF2F-Test and solves 93 of 672 PutnamBench problems. We release MiniF2F-ALF, an ALF-mutated contamination-sensitive benchmark on which every evaluated model loses accuracy; here our 32B remains strongest and our 4B matches the prior state of the art, Goedel-Prover-V2-32B.

</details>


### [140] [PersonaDrive: Human-Style Retrieval-Augmented VLA Agents for Closed-Loop Driving Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12616)
*Mahmoud Srewa,Praneetsai Iddamsetty,Mohammad Abdullah Al Faruque,Salma Elmalaki*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PersonaDrive通过检索人类风格驾驶数据来条件化VLA智能体，实现了无需重新训练即可切换驾驶风格的闭环仿真智能体，在Bench2Drive上取得了最高驾驶分数并展现出明显的风格差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有闭环驾驶仿真器中的非自车智能体行为趋同，要么来自基于规则的交通管理器，要么来自单一行为模式的训练模型。近期工作通过观测数据的事后标签或LLM推断的奖励权重引入风格变化，但这些信号仅是风格奖励的代理，而非人类明确按照指定风格驾驶的真实演示。

Method: 提出PersonaDrive三阶段流程：(i)使用图像-文本相似度综合评分在每风格人类驾驶数据上进行离线三元组挖掘；(ii)训练轻量级检索头，融合冻结视觉特征和小型控制编码器；(iii)微调单个VLA骨干网络，将检索到的上下文点作为上下文行为演示用于航点预测。推理时通过切换检索头查询的风格数据库来条件化驾驶风格，无需针对每种风格重新训练。

Result: 在Bench2Drive上，PersonaDrive（无风格）驾驶分数比SimLingo提升4.6%，比HiP-AD提升2.5%；在风格条件下，每个风格都达到最高驾驶分数（在约2%范围内），其最弱风格超过最强基线DMW达5.4%；从保守到激进指令，平均速度和加速度分别上升18%和25%。

Conclusion: PersonaDrive成功实现了基于人类风格指令数据的驾驶风格控制，能够生成具有人类风格特征且风格多样的非自车智能体用于闭环仿真，为自动驾驶测试提供了更真实的交通环境模拟。

Abstract: Closed-loop driving simulators typically populate their environments with non-ego traffic agents that behave largely the same way, produced either by rule-based traffic managers or by learned models trained toward a single behavioral mode. Recent work introduces style variation through post-hoc labels on observational data or LLM-inferred reward weights, but these signals act as proxies for what a style should reward rather than demonstrations of humans explicitly asked to drive in that style. We introduce PersonaDrive, a pipeline that conditions a vision-language-action (VLA) driving agent on retrieved demonstrations from a style-instructed human driving dataset, in which participants drive CARLA leaderboard routes under aggressive, neutral, and conservative instructions on a driver-in-the-loop rig. The pipeline has three stages: (i) offline triplet mining over per-style human driving data using a combined image-text similarity score; (ii) training a lightweight retrieval head that fuses frozen visual features with a small control encoder over per-style databases; and (iii) fine-tuning a single VLA backbone to treat retrieved context points as in-context behavioral demonstrations during waypoint prediction. At inference, the same backbone is conditioned on any style by swapping which per-style database the retrieval head queries, so selecting a style requires no per-style retraining while enabling human-style, style-diverse non-ego agents for closed-loop simulation. On Bench2Drive, PersonaDrive (no style) improves the driving score by 4.6% over SimLingo and 2.5% over HiP-AD, and under style conditioning attains the highest driving score in every style within a roughly 2% band (its weakest style surpassing the strongest baseline, DMW, by 5.4%), while average speed and acceleration rise by 18% and 25% from the conservative to the aggressive instruction.

</details>


### [141] ["Did you lie?" Evaluating Lie Detectors across Model Scale and Belief-Verified Model Organisms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12618)
*Alan Cooney,David Africa,Geoffrey Irving*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文构建了13个具有可验证隐藏信念的推理模型生物和多样化的提示说谎测试平台，评估了四种测谎检测器。结果表明：在提示说谎任务上，所有检测器随模型能力提升而表现更好；但在训练模型生物上，激活和概率对数类检测器性能急剧下降，仅思维链判断器保持较强性能（平衡准确率0.82）。当前测谎检测器无法支持对模型信念的高置信度声明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发鲁棒的语言模型测谎检测器对于模型行为的审计、监控和事后调查至关重要，但评估这些检测器需要模型可验证地相信与其所说相反的内容。现有的训练模型生物往往无法满足这一要求，导致先前的检测结果难以解释，因此需要构建更可靠的测试平台来评估检测器的真实性能。

Method: 1. 构建13个推理模型生物，通过思维链验证其隐藏信念，并证明这些信念可泛化到保留任务；
2. 创建多样化欺骗测试平台，涵盖广泛的诱导说谎动机；
3. 评估四种检测器：思维链判断器、概率对数分类器、两种激活探针（包括新提出的DYL方法用于训练后续探针）；
4. 在31个参数量从2B到1T的开源权重模型上进行测试。

Result: 在提示说谎任务上，所有四种检测器都显示出与模型能力的正相关性。然而，在训练模型生物上，所有基于激活和概率对数的检测器性能急剧下降，DYL保留了最多信号；仅思维链判断器保持强劲表现（平衡准确率0.82），部分原因是验证过程有利于可读思维链的信念。

Conclusion: 当前的测谎检测器无法支持关于模型信念的高置信度声明。研究提出了可能解决当前部分局限性的研究方向，并公开了数据集、模型生物和训练好的检测器，以促进该领域的进一步研究。

Abstract: Robust lie detectors for language models could enable powerful techniques for auditing, monitoring, and post-hoc investigation of model behaviour, but evaluating them requires testbeds where models verifiably believe the opposite of what they say. We show that existing trained model organisms often fail this requirement, leaving prior positive and negative detection results difficult to interpret. We address this with 13 reasoning model organisms whose hidden beliefs are verified in chain-of-thought and shown to generalise to held-out tasks, alongside Varied Deception, a prompted-lying testbed covering a broad range of lie-inducing motivations. On these testbeds we evaluate four detectors: a chain-of-thought judge, a logprob classifier, and two activation probes, including Did-You-Lie (DYL), a new method for training follow-up probes. On prompted lying, across 31 open-weight models spanning 2B to 1T parameters, all four detectors show positive scaling with model capability. However, every activation- and logprob-based detector drops sharply on our trained model organisms, with DYL retaining the most signal; only the chain-of-thought judge remains strong, with 0.82 balanced accuracy, partly as an artefact of our verification process favouring CoT-readable beliefs. Current lie detectors therefore cannot support high-confidence claims about model beliefs, and we suggest research directions that may address some of their current limitations. We release our datasets, model organisms, and trained detectors.

</details>


### [142] [TrajGenAgent: A Hierarchical LLM Agent for Human Mobility Trajectory Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12657)
*Siyu Li,Toan Tran,Lingyi Zhao,Khurram Shafique,Li Xiong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了TrajGenAgent，一个无需模型微调的语义感知分层LLM-agent框架，用于生成人类移动轨迹。通过两阶段的编排器-工作者设计，在保持零样本推理能力的同时实现了更好的时空保真度和语义一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模轨迹数据收集成本高且受隐私限制，迫切需要生成逼真的合成轨迹。现有基于LLM的生成方法存在两难困境：提示工程保留了零样本推理但缺乏细粒度时空基础，轨迹级微调提高了统计精度但计算成本高且可能削弱通用推理能力。

Method: 提出TrajGenAgent框架，采用两阶段编排器-工作者设计：第一阶段LLM通过上下文学习从历史证据合成个体和星期条件化的活动链；第二阶段通过确定性工作流将每个活动落地为完整访问，包括个性化POI检索、距离感知位置选择、运动感知旅行时间传播和基于LLM的持续时间估计。同时引入基于异常检测的评估框架，使用两个互补检测器评估行为和语义合理性。

Result: 在基准数据集和大规模仿真数据集上的实验表明，TrajGenAgent在时空保真度、语义连贯性和个体特定行为真实性方面均优于代表性神经基线和LLM基线方法，同时避免了参数更新。

Conclusion: TrajGenAgent成功解决了LLM轨迹生成中零样本推理与统计精度之间的权衡问题，通过语义感知的分层设计实现了高质量的轨迹生成，为人类移动性研究提供了一种无需微调的高效解决方案。

Abstract: Human mobility data is important for transportation, urban planning, and epidemic control, but large-scale trajectory collection is often costly and privacy-constrained, motivating realistic synthetic trajectory generation. Existing LLM-based generators typically rely on either prompt engineering, which preserves zero-shot reasoning but lacks fine-grained spatiotemporal grounding, or trajectory-level fine-tuning, which improves statistical precision but incurs substantial computational cost and may weaken general reasoning. We propose TrajGenAgent, a semantic-aware hierarchical LLM-agent framework for human mobility trajectory generation without model fine-tuning. TrajGenAgent uses a two-stage orchestrator-worker design: an LLM first synthesizes an individual- and weekday-conditioned activity chain from historical evidence via in-context learning, and a deterministic workflow then grounds each activity into a complete visit using personalized POI retrieval, distance-aware location selection, kinematics-aware travel-time propagation, and LLM-based duration estimation. To evaluate realism beyond aggregate spatiotemporal statistics, we introduce an anomaly-detection-based evaluation framework using two complementary detectors to assess behavioral and semantic plausibility. Experiments on benchmark and large-scale simulation datasets show that TrajGenAgent improves spatiotemporal fidelity, semantic coherence, and individual-specific behavioral realism over representative neural and LLM-based baselines, while avoiding parameter updates.

</details>


### [143] [The Illusion of Multi-Agent Advantage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13003)
*Prathyusha Jwalapuram,Hehai Lin,Chuyuan Li,Fangkai Jiao,Sudong Wang,Yifei Ming,Zixuan Ke,Chengwei Qin,Giuseppe Carenini,Shafiq Joty*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究挑战了"多智能体系统(MAS)优于单智能体系统(SAS)"的主流观点，发现自动生成的MAS在性能和成本效率上均不如CoT-SC基线，且存在架构臃肿问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究普遍认为MAS优于SAS，但这一结论主要基于侧重孤立推理任务的基准测试，未能充分评估MAS的上下文保护、并行处理和分布式决策等优势。研究旨在对自动生成的MAS进行严格的系统评估，验证其相对于SAS的实际表现。

Method: 1. 将自动生成的MAS与CoT-SC单智能体基线进行系统对比，涵盖传统推理数据集和交互式多步骤工作流任务（如BrowseComp-Plus）；2. 构建专门用于MAS评估的诊断性合成数据集，具有明确的任务分解、上下文分离和并行化特征；3. 系统性解构分析生成的MAS架构。

Result: 1. 自动生成的MAS在各类任务上持续表现不如CoT-SC，且成本高达10倍；2. 专家设计的MAS在专用数据集上显著优于自动生成架构；3. 当前自动化设计范式产生架构臃肿，追求表面复杂性而非功能实用性。

Conclusion: 现有评估框架掩盖了复杂MAS的关键架构缺陷和低效性，未能考虑增加计算成本的边际效用；当前自动化的MAS设计范式与多智能体原则存在根本性错位，亟需重新审视评估方法。

Abstract: Prevailing wisdom posits that Multi-Agent Systems (MAS) are superior to Single-Agent Systems (SAS), citing advantages like context protection, parallel processing and distributed decision-making. However, empirical support for this claim relies primarily on comparisons with SAS baselines using benchmarks that prioritize isolated reasoning tasks, which do not adequately assess these advantages. Focusing on automatically generated MAS that are designed for enhanced generalizability over manually-designed counterparts, we perform a rigorous, systematic evaluation against SAS, specifically Chain-of-Thought with Self-Consistency (CoT-SC). Across traditional reasoning datasets and tasks with interactive multi-step workflows (e.g., BrowseComp-Plus), we demonstrate that automatic MAS consistently underperform CoT-SC despite being up to 10x more expensive. To isolate these failures from limitations inherent to task structure, we introduce a diagnostic synthetic dataset tailored for MAS featuring explicit task decomposition, context separation and parallelization potential. We show that expert-architected MAS consistently outperforms automatically generated architectures in both raw performance and cost-efficiency on this dataset, demonstrating that existing evaluation frameworks mask critical architectural gaps and inefficiencies of complex MAS by failing to account for the marginal utility of increased computational cost. Critically, systematic deconstruction of the generated MAS architectures reveals that current automated design paradigms produce architectural bloat that prioritizes superficial complexity which does not translate into functional utility, exposing a fundamental misalignment with multi-agent principles.

</details>


### [144] [Evoflux: Inference-Time Evolution of Executable Tool Workflows for Compact Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12674)
*Kushal Raj Bhandari,Ling Yue,Ching-Yun Ko,Dhaval Patel,Shaowu Pan,Pin-Yu Chen,Jianxi Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Evoflux，一种推理时的进化搜索方法，用于修复小型语言模型在MCP风格工具使用中的失败计划。该方法将紧凑型工具使用视为可执行工具工作流的修复问题，通过结构化编辑、执行反馈、自适应强度、元引导重设计和多样性剪枝来演化类型化工作流图，将小型规划器的执行可行性从约3%提升至17-24%，显著优于SFT/DPO方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 紧凑型语言模型在MCP风格工具使用中面临挑战：代理需要从实时目录发现工具、满足模式要求、保持中间输出的依赖关系，并将最终响应建立在执行证据之上。小型规划器经常生成看似合理但在工具解析、参数验证、依赖跟踪或执行时失败的工作流图。这种失败模式通过小语料库蒸馏难以处理——数百条教师轨迹可以教授工作流格式，但很少涵盖在变化工具目录上修复失败计划所需的恢复行为。

Method: Evoflux是一种推理时进化搜索方法，将紧凑型工具使用视为可执行工具工作流的修复。该方法包含五个核心组件：(1)通过结构化编辑演化类型化工作流图；(2)利用执行反馈指导修复方向；(3)采用自适应强度调整搜索策略；(4)元引导重设计提升修复质量；(5)多样性剪枝保持搜索空间的有效性。方法无需依赖大量教师轨迹，直接在工作流执行过程中进行优化和修复。

Result: 在涵盖实时MCP服务器和250个工具的MCP-Bench留出任务上，Evoflux将小型规划器的执行可行性从约3%提升至17-24%。相比之下，在相同搜索挖掘数据上进行的SFT和SFT+DPO训练仅能匹配、表现不如或低于零样本性能；ReAct方法虽能达到更高峰值，但方差更高且token成本更大。结果证明在稀缺教师轨迹预算下，执行导向的搜索方法更为可靠。

Conclusion: 本研究证明了执行导向的搜索方法在处理紧凑型语言模型的工具使用问题上优于传统的监督学习方法。Evoflux成功解决了小型规划器在MCP风格工具使用中的关键失败模式，为在稀缺教师轨迹预算下构建可靠的工具使用代理提供了新思路，具有重要的实践意义。

Abstract: Compact language models (LMs) reduce cost, latency, and deployment risk for tool agents. Yet MCP-style tool use requires more than isolated function calling: an agent must discover tools from live catalogs, satisfy schemas, preserve dependencies across intermediate outputs, and ground final responses in executed evidence. Small planners often generate plausible workflow graphs that fail under tool resolution, parameter validation, dependency tracking, or execution. We argue that this failure mode is poorly handled by small-corpus distillation. A few hundred teacher traces can teach workflow format, but rarely cover the recovery behavior needed to repair failed plans over changing tool catalogs. We introduce Evoflux, an inference-time evolutionary search method that treats compact tool use as the repair of executable tool workflows. It evolves typed workflow graphs through structured edits, execution feedback, adaptive intensity, meta-guided redesign, and diversity pruning. On held-out MCP-Bench tasks spanning live MCP servers and 250 tools, Evoflux raises execution feasibility from roughly 3% to 17-24% across small planners. In contrast, SFT and SFT+DPO on the same search-mined data match, underperform, or collapse below zero-shot performance; ReAct reaches higher peaks, but with higher variance and token cost. These results show that execution-grounded search is more reliable under scarce teacher-trace budgets.

</details>


### [145] [LLM-as-an-Investigator: Evidence-First Reasoning for Robust Interactive Problem Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13220)
*Fabrizio Marozzo,Pietro Liò*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出LLM-as-an-Investigator方法，通过证据优先的代理AI技术解决大语言模型在技术诊断中过早迎合用户假设的问题，在机械、电气和液压领域的基准测试中优于标准助手和纯推理基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型作为交互式技术助手时存在用户驱动阿谀行为：当用户提供不完整描述或看似合理但未经证实的解释时，LLM倾向于过早与这些假设对齐并提出解决方案，而非收集充分证据。这种倾向强化用户提供的假设而非测试替代解释，导致诊断不准确。

Method: 提出LLM-as-an-Investigator证据优先代理AI方法论，通过解决方案调查员代理实现。该代理评估初始问题描述的模糊性，生成候选假设，提出针对性澄清问题，并在每次回答后更新假设概率。代理持续调查直到证据使某一候选解释明显强于替代方案，而非立即响应。评估采用三代理流水线：问题-解决方案提取代理将已解决论坛线程转换为结构化案例，真实值评估代理模拟用户并隐藏已知解决方案，测试助手尝试通过对话恢复解决方案。

Result: 在机械、电气和液压领域的技术论坛线程基准测试中，所提方法在诊断准确性上优于直接提示和纯推理基线。证据优先协议有效减少了用户诱导的对话偏差。分析显示标准助手在诊断案例中确实跟随误导性用户假设。方法在不同LLM骨干网络上均表现良好。

Conclusion: LLM-as-an-Investigator方法通过证据优先的调查协议成功缓解了用户驱动阿谀行为，提高了技术问题诊断的准确性和鲁棒性，为大语言模型作为技术诊断助手提供了更可靠的交互范式。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used as interactive assistants for technical problem solving. However, when users provide incomplete descriptions or plausible but unverified explanations, LLMs may prematurely align with these assumptions and propose solutions before collecting sufficient evidence. We refer to this behavior as user-driven sycophancy: the tendency of an LLM to reinforce a user-provided hypothesis instead of testing alternative explanations. This paper introduces LLM-as-an-Investigator, an evidence-first agentic AI methodology for robust problem diagnosis. The approach is implemented through a Solution Investigator Agent, which estimates the ambiguity of an initial problem description, generates candidate hypotheses, asks targeted clarification questions, and updates hypothesis probabilities after each answer. Rather than producing an immediate response, the agent continues the investigation until the evidence makes one candidate explanation stronger than the alternatives. To evaluate the approach, we build a benchmark from solved technical forum threads in mechanical, electrical, and hydraulic domains. We use a three-agent evaluation pipeline in which a Problem-Solution Extractor Agent converts solved threads into structured cases, a Ground-Truth Evaluator Agent simulates the user while hiding the known solution, and the tested assistant attempts to recover the solution through dialogue. The experiments compare standard assistants, reasoning-oriented LLMs, and the proposed investigator-based model across LLM backbones. In addition to diagnostic accuracy, we analyze how standard assistants follow misleading user hypotheses in diagnostic cases. The results show that the proposed approach identifies the problem more accurately than direct prompting and reasoning-only baselines, while its evidence-first protocol helps reduce user-induced conversational bias.

</details>


### [146] [From AGI to ASI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12683)
*Tim Genewein,Matija Franklin,Alexander Lerchner,Laurent Orseau,Samuel Albanie,Adam Bales,Cole Wyeth,Stephanie Chan,Iason Gabriel,Joel Z. Leibo,Allan Dafoe,Marcus Hutter,Thore Graepel,Shane Legg*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本报告探讨了从人工通用智能（AGI）向人工通用超级智能（ASI）过渡的可能路径，分析了四种潜在发展路径及其瓶颈，并指出AI进步可能会持续加速，带来一系列而非单一的社会变革。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AGI从科幻推测变为许多大型AI组织在未来十年内的具体目标，迫切需要研究AGI实现后AI如何继续发展，特别是向超级智能过渡的过程及其对社会的影响，以应对可能出现的一系列变革性变化。

Method: 报告首先刻画了人工通用超级智能（ASI）的特征，然后分析了从AGI到ASI的四条潜在路径：规模化AGI、AI范式转换、递归改进、以及大规模多智能体集体涌现。随后讨论了这些路径中可能存在的摩擦和瓶颈，并提出了具体的开放性研究问题。

Result: 研究结果表明，由于预测ASI进展存在巨大不确定性，无法排除AI进步在未来几年持续加速的可能性。AGI的引入可能不会导致单一的社会变革，而是一系列由AI驱动的科技突破所带来的连续性社会变革。

Conclusion: 为应对这一前景，需要开展大规模、跨学科的全球性研究努力。AGI的引入可能不会是单一变革性事件，而是由AI驱动的科技进步在多个领域引发的一系列社会变革。

Abstract: Over the last decade, building human-level artificial general intelligence has moved from far-fetched speculation to being a concrete next-decade target for many of the largest AI organisations. Achieving this goal would have profound and far-reaching impacts on human society, which raises many complex questions for the decade ahead. This report investigates how AI itself might continue to develop in a post-AGI world along the continuum of machine intelligence. The endpoint of this continuum, Universal AI, is theoretically well understood, which provides some formal grounding for the main focus of this report: the transition from human-level AGI to artificial general superintelligence, which, intuitively, can be understood as a system that is more intelligent and cognitively capable than large organisations of humans. After characterizing ASI, the report discusses four potential pathways from AGI to ASI: scaling AGI, AI paradigm shifts, recursive improvement, and ASI emerging from large-scale multi-agent collectives. The report then discusses possible frictions and bottlenecks along these pathways.
