Abstract: When large language models (LLMs) fail to generalize or make haphazard errors in reasoning, it is often taken as evidence that LLMs are not truly reasoning, but rather performing a kind of pattern matching. The implication is that people's behavior does not exhibit the same types of failures because human reasoning uses principled and abstract world models. We evaluate human participants and 25 LLMs on their ability to engage in common-sense reasoning about a variety of everyday situations and observe similar patterns of errors in both people and models. We then identify the set of attention heads driving LLM responses and find that these heads implement a form of pattern-matching. These attention heads allow us to predict seemingly inexplicable reasoning errors in people caused by ostensibly irrelevant prompt details. Taken together, our results suggest that everyday causal reasoning in people and LLMs is more consistent with a form of pattern-matching than with abstract world models.

</details>


### [216] [AgentBeats: Agentifying Agent Assessment for Openness, Standardization, and Reproducibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13608)
*Xiaoyuan Liu,Jianhong Tu,Yuqi Chen,Siyuan Xie,Sihan Ren,Tianneng Shi,Gal Gantar,Evan Sandoval,Donghyun Lee,Daniel Miao,Peter J. Gilbert,Nick Hynes,Mauro Staver,Warren He,David Marn,Andrew Low,Xi Zhang,Elron Bandel,Michal Shmueli-Scheuer,Siva Reddy,Alexandre Drouin,Alexandre Lacoste,Ramayya Krishnan,Elham Tabassi,Yu Su,Victor Barres,Chenguang Wang,Wenbo Guo,Dawn Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了Agentified Agent Assessment (AAA)框架，通过judge agent进行评估，使用A2A和MCP标准化协议，实现了与具体agent实现解耦的统一评估方法，并通过AgentBeats系统和大规模实验验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体系统评估方法存在碎片化问题：大多数基准测试依赖固定的、以LLM为中心的测试框架，需要大量集成工作，造成测试与生产环境不匹配，且限制了不同智能体设计之间的公平比较。根本问题在于缺乏开放的、智能体无关的评估接口。

Method: 提出Agentified Agent Assessment (AAA)框架，其中评估由judge agents执行，所有参与者通过标准化协议交互：A2A用于任务管理，MCP用于工具访问。与传统基准测试需要定义两个分离接口不同，AAA只需一个统一接口，将评估逻辑与智能体实现分离。实现系统AgentBeats，识别出五种实用操作模式以平衡开放性、隐私性和可复现性等现实约束。

Result: 通过两项研究验证设计：(1) 为期五个月的开放竞赛，吸引298个judge agents（跨12个类别）和467个来自独立参与者的subject agents，证明AAA适用于异构基准测试；(2) 编码智能体案例研究，确认agent化评估在保持与公开记录一致性的同时，揭示之前缺失的head-to-head比较结果，产出智能体设计的研究洞察。

Conclusion: 结合社区规模实地研究和受控编码案例研究，验证了AAA在异构场景规模下提供覆盖率、实用性和保真度。AAA和AgentBeats为开放、标准化和可复现的智能体评估提供了清晰路径。

Abstract: Agent systems are advancing quickly across domains, but their evaluation remains fragmented. Most benchmarks rely on fixed, LLM-centric harnesses that require heavy integration, create test-production mismatch, and limit fair comparison across diverse agent designs. The root problem is the lack of an open, agent-agnostic assessment interface. We advocate Agentified Agent Assessment (AAA), where evaluation is performed by judge agents and all participants interact through standardized protocols: A2A for task management and MCP for tool access. Conventional benchmarking defines two separate interfaces, one for the benchmark and one for the agent, while AAA only needs one; this yields a generic, unified framework that separates assessment logic from agent implementation and enables reproducible, interoperable, and multi-agent evaluation. We further introduce AgentBeats as a concrete realization of AAA: we identify five practical operation modes that make standardized assessment compatible with real-world constraints on openness, privacy, and reproducibility.
  To evaluate our design at scale, we conduct two studies: a five-month open competition that drew 298 judge agents across 12 categories together with 467 subject agents from independent participants, showing that AAA applies across a heterogeneous range of benchmarks; and a case study on coding agents that confirms agentified evaluation preserves fidelity with the public record while surfacing previously missing head-to-head results, yielding research insights about agent design. Combining a community-scale field study and a controlled coding case study, we verify that AAA delivers coverage, practicality, and fidelity across heterogeneous scenarios at scale. Together, AAA and AgentBeats offer a clear path toward open, standardized, and reproducible agent assessment.

</details>


### [217] [Before You Think: System 0, AI-Mediated Cognition and Cognitive Colonization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13658)
*Marianna Bergamaschi Ganapini,Massimo Chiriatti,Enrico Panai,Giuseppe Riva*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文分析了三种理解AI认知和认识后果的框架，论证了System 0的独特理论地位，并提出了认知殖民化概念，揭示了AI系统如何以难以察觉的方式将外部利益嵌入自我架构中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI系统的广泛部署，理解其对个体推理和集体认识实践的影响变得日益重要。现有框架虽捕捉了部分影响维度，但仍存在理论盲区，亟需揭示AI系统对用户的隐性影响机制。

Method: 对比分析三种最新的AI认知与认识后果理解框架：三元系统理论(Tri-System Theory)、思维框架(Thinkframes)和系统0(System 0)，论证各框架的理论定位及其局限性。

Result: 三元系统理论和思维框架分别捕捉了AI对个体推理和集体认识实践影响的重要维度，但System 0占据了独特的理论地位，前两者无法完全复制其功能；提出了认知殖民化概念，阐明了AI系统如何将外部利益嵌入用户自我架构的难以察觉的方式。

Conclusion: 由于此类AI系统已广泛部署，理解这些隐性影响形式是一项紧迫的哲学和实践任务，对于认识AI对人类认知自主性的潜在威胁具有重要意义。

Abstract: This paper examines three recent frameworks for understanding the cognitive and epistemic consequences of artificial intelligence: Tri-System Theory, Thinkframes, and System 0. It argues that while the first two capture important dimensions of AI's influence on individual reasoning and collective epistemic practices, System 0 occupies a theoretically distinctive position that neither can fully replicate. The paper introduces the concept of cognitive colonization, according to which AI systems can embed external interests within the architecture of the self in ways that are difficult for users to perceive. Because such systems are already widely deployed, understanding these invisible forms of influence is an urgent philosophical and practical task.

</details>


### [218] [EurekAgent: Agent Environment Engineering is All You Need For Autonomous Scientific Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13662)
*Amy Xin,Jiening Siow,Junjie Wang,Zijun Yao,Fanjin Zhang,Jian Song,Lei Hou,Juanzi Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出“环境工程”概念，通过EurekAgent系统在权限、制品、预算和人机交互四个维度优化智能体环境，在数学、内核工程和机器学习任务上取得新的SOTA结果，并以低于11美元的API成本发现26圆填充新记录。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM能力的提升，自动化科学发现的瓶颈正从设计智能体工作流程转移到设计智能体环境。现有环境往往无法有效促进有益行为（如开放式探索、系统化管理）同时抑制有害行为（如奖励黑客攻击），因此需要系统性地进行环境工程。

Method: 提出EurekAgent系统，从四个维度进行环境工程：（1）权限工程——实现有界的智能体执行和隔离评估；（2）制品工程——建立基于文件系统和Git的协作机制；（3）预算工程——实现预算感知的探索；（4）人在回路工程——支持便捷的人工监督和干预。

Result: 在多个数学、内核工程和机器学习任务上取得了新的最先进结果。特别值得一提的是，以不到11美元的总API成本发现了新的26圆填充最优解，刷新了该领域记录。

Conclusion: 环境工程应成为开发可靠自主研究智能体的核心研究方向。论文开源了代码和结果，呼吁社区重视环境设计在塑造智能体行为方面的关键作用。

Abstract: LLM-based agents have shown increasing potential in automating scientific discovery. Given an optimizable metric and an execution environment, they can propose, validate, and iterate scientific solutions, and have produced results that outperform human-designed approaches. As model capabilities continue to improve, we argue that the bottleneck for autonomous scientific discovery is shifting from prescribing agent workflows to designing agent environments: the resources, constraints, and interfaces that shape agent behavior. We frame this as environment engineering: building environments that amplify productive behaviors, such as open-ended exploration, systematic artifact management, and inter-agent collaboration, while suppressing harmful behaviors, such as reward hacking and high-friction human oversight. We present EurekAgent, an environment-engineered agent system for metric-driven autonomous scientific discovery. EurekAgent engineers the environment along four dimensions: permissions engineering for bounded agent execution and isolated evaluation; artifact engineering for filesystem and Git-based collaboration; budget engineering for budget-aware exploration; and human-in-the-loop engineering for easy human supervision and intervention. EurekAgent sets new state-of-the-art results on multiple mathematics, kernel engineering, and machine learning tasks, including new state-of-the-art 26-circle packing results discovered with less than $11 in total API cost. We open-source our code and results, and call for environment engineering as a core research direction for developing reliable autonomous research agents.

</details>


### [219] [Agents-K1: Towards Agent-native Knowledge Orchestration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13669)
*Zongsheng Cao,Bihao Zhan,Jinxin Shi,Jiong Wang,Fangchen Yu,Zhijie Zhong,Zijie Guo,Tianshuo Peng,Zhuo Liu,Yi Xie,Xiang Zhuang,Yue Fan,Runmin Ma,Shiyang Feng,Xiangchao Yan,Anran Liu,Peng Ye,Wenlong Zhang,Shufei Zhang,Chunfeng Song,Fenghua Ling,Jie Zhou,Liang He,Bo Zhang,Lei Bai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了Agents-K1，一个端到端的知识编排管线，可将原始文档转换为代理原生的科学知识图谱，并基于此构建了包含246万篇论文的Scholar-KG数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于LLM的研究代理在代理编排方面已有进展，但很大程度上忽视了科学知识编排。现有工作通常将论文简化为摘要、表面提及和扁平化的cites边，忽略了科学推理所需的关键实体、主张、证据、机制和方法谱系。

Method: 提出Agents-K1端到端知识编排管线，包含三个核心组件：(1)多模态解析器，采用五模块模式捕获全文中的实体、多模态证据、引用和类型化实体间关系；(2)基于GRPO训练的4B信息提取主干网络，使用基于规则的奖励；(3)graph-anything CLI，统一网络搜索、多模态图谱检索和跨文档遍历的三源代理接口。同时处理246万篇科学论文构建Scholar-KG数据集。

Result: 在六个学科的246万篇科学论文上构建了Scholar-KG知识图谱（发布其中100万篇子集），大规模实验证明Agents-K1在科学信息提取、知识图谱构建和多跳科学推理任务上均达到优越性能。该管线可扩展至通用领域语料库和模式一致性数据合成。

Conclusion: Agents-K1成功填补了科学知识编排的空白，通过构建代理原生的科学知识图谱，为科学推理提供了更丰富的结构化知识表示，显著提升了科学信息提取和推理任务的性能。

Abstract: Current LLM-based research agents have advanced through agent orchestration, yet largely overlook scientific knowledge orchestration. Existing works often reduce papers to abstracts, surface mentions, and flat \texttt{cites} edges, omitting key entities, claims, evidence, mechanisms, and method lineages essential for scientific reasoning. To this end, we introduce \textbf{Agents-K1}, an end-to-end knowledge orchestration pipeline that converts raw documents into agent-native scientific knowledge graphs. Agents-K1 integrates three components under a unifying theoretical foundation: a multimodal parser whose five-module schema captures entities, multimodal evidence, citations, and typed inter-entity relations across the full paper rather than abstracts alone; a 4B information-extraction backbone trained with GRPO under a rule-based reward; and a graphanything CLI, a tri-source agent interface that unifies web search, multimodal graph retrieval, and cross-document traversal. On top of this, we process 2.46 million scientific papers across six subjects to produce \textbf{Scholar-KG}, of which we release a one-million-paper subset, and the full Scholar-KG is accessible via the SCP link below. The same pipeline can be extended to general-domain corpora and to schema-conformant data synthesis. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Agents-K1 achieves superior performance in scientific information extraction, knowledge graph construction, and multi-hop scientific reasoning.

</details>


### [220] [Automated reproducibility assessments in the social and behavioral sciences using large language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13670)
*Tobias Holtdirk,Pietro Marcolongo,Anna Steinberg Schulten,Felix Henninger,Stefan Rose,Sarah Ball,Bolei Ma,Frauke Kreuter,Markus Weinmann,Stefan Feuerriegel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究开发了基于大语言模型（LLM）的自动化可重复性评估流程。在76项行为与社会科学研究中，LLM在41%的研究中成功复现了原始效应量（±0.05 Cohen's d容差），在96%的研究中得出了与原始研究一致的定性结论，表现优于人类复现者（分别为34%和74%），证明了LLM作为可扩展的实证结果审计工具的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统社会科学领域的可重复性评估通常需要独立研究者重新分析原始数据，这种方法资源消耗大、成本高，难以规模化应用。因此，迫切需要一种能够自动化、规模化进行可重复性评估的工具，以提高实证结果审计的效率和覆盖范围。

Method: 研究团队构建了一个LLM自动化分析流程，选取了76项已发表的行为与社会科学研究作为样本，这些研究具有预设的声明。将LLM生成的分析结果与原始研究发现以及人类复现分析结果进行比较，评估指标包括效应量估计（Cohen's d，容差±0.05）和定性结论一致性。

Result: 在76项研究中，LLM在7项研究上无法生成有效的效应量估计。在剩余的研究中：(1) LLM流程在41%的研究中成功复现了原始效应量；(2) LLM流程在96%的情况下得出了与原始研究相同的定性结论（即复现分析是否支持原始声明）。相比之下，人类复现者在34%的研究中复现了原始效应量，在74%的情况下得出了相同的定性结论。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，大语言模型能够作为一种可扩展的自动化可重复性评估工具，其表现甚至在某些指标上超越了人类复现者。这为系统性地审计社会科学与行为科学领域的实证结果奠定了基础，提供了一种比传统人工复现更高效、更具规模化的解决方案。

Abstract: Reproducibility in the social and behavioral sciences is typically evaluated by independent researchers who reanalyze the original data to assess whether the published findings can be recovered. However, such approaches are resource-intensive and difficult to scale. Here, we show that large language models (LLMs) can automate reproducibility assessments. Using N=76 published studies with predefined claims from the behavioral and social sciences, we compare LLM-generated analysis with the original findings and human reanalysis. For 7 studies, the LLM could not produce a viable effect size estimate. For the remaining studies, our LLM pipeline recovered the original effect sizes in 41% of studies using a +/-0.05 tolerance in Cohen's d. Further, our LLM pipeline reached the same qualitative conclusion as the original study in 96% of cases, where conclusions indicate whether the reanalysis supports the original claim. For comparison, human reanalysts recovered the original effect sizes in 34% of studies and reached the same qualitative conclusion in 74% of cases. Together, these results show that LLMs can serve as a scalable tool for automated reproducibility assessment and provide a foundation for systematic auditing of empirical results in the social and behavioral sciences.

</details>


<div id='cs.NI'></div>

# cs.NI [[Back]](#toc)

### [221] [Free-Placement Optimization of Ground Station Locations for Low-Earth Orbit Satellites](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12667)
*Grace Ra Kim,Duncan Eddy,Vedant Srinivas,Mykel J. Kochenderfer*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出了SCORE（两阶段自由部署优化方法）用于地面站网络设计，相比传统固定站点选择方法，可在连续空间域内优化地面站位置。测试表明，SCORE比差分进化算法少用5倍函数评估次数即可收敛，下行链路吞吐量提升最高13%；相比固定站点方法，无约束SCORE可实现最高15%的总下行量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 快速扩张的低轨卫星星座对地面网络提出了更高要求，现有方法仅从预定义位置中选择站点，限制了优化空间和性能表现。自由部署优化虽然扩大了搜索空间、允许更高吞吐量配置，但面临高维度、非凸性和局部最优等挑战。

Method: 提出SCORE（Sequential Cyclic Optimization via Refinement & Evaluation）两阶段自由部署方法，结合序列坐标选择与循环精化策略，以应对全局优化器面临的高维度、非凸性和局部最小值挑战。在连续空间域上进行地面站位置优化，支持无约束部署和基础设施约束部署两种模式。

Result: 在两个商业地球观测星座（Capella Space和ICEYE）及一个合成Walker-Star星座上测试，SCORE相比差分进化算法（DE）收敛所需函数评估次数减少最高5倍，下行链路吞吐量提升最高13%。相比固定站点方法，无约束SCORE实现最高15%的总下行量提升；基础设施约束SCORE在限制于现有光纤和电力设施附近部署时，仍保留超过92%的性能增益。

Conclusion: SCORE为灵活部署地面站网络建立了强有力的实证性能基准，在基础设施约束下仍能保持大部分性能优势。研究还探讨了扩展现有站点与部署新站点之间的权衡，为未来运营星座的地面网络设计提供了重要参考。

Abstract: Rapidly expanding low Earth orbit satellite constellations are placing increasing demands on terrestrial ground networks, motivating the development of more efficient ground station network designs. Current approaches select sites from predefined locations, limiting optimization to existing infrastructure and constraining performance. In contrast, free-placement optimization operates over a continuous spatial domain on Earth, broadening the search space and allowing higher-throughput configurations at the cost of potentially requiring new infrastructure deployment. In this work, we introduce SCORE (Sequential Cyclic Optimization via Refinement & Evaluation), a two-stage free-placement method for ground station design. SCORE combines sequential coordinate selection with cyclic refinement to manage high-dimensionality, non-convexity, and local minima that challenge global optimizers. We benchmark SCORE against one-shot methods such as differential evolution (DE) and integer programming approaches using locations from Kongsberg Satellite Services and the World Teleport Association. Tests across two commercial Earth observation constellations (Capella Space and ICEYE) and one synthetic Walker-Star constellation show that SCORE requires up to 5x fewer function evaluations to converge relative to DE while improving downlink throughput by up to 13%. Compared to fixed-site methods, unconstrained SCORE achieves up to 15% greater total downlink, establishing a strong empirical performance benchmark for flexible placement; infrastructure-constrained SCORE retains over 92% of this gain while restricting placement to within proximity of existing fiber and power infrastructure. We also explore trade-offs between expanding existing stations and deploying new sites, informing future ground network design for operational constellations.

</details>


### [222] [ScaleAcross: Designing Multi-Data-Center Infrastructure for Geo-Distributed AI Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12963)
*Naved Inam,Aryan Alpesh Bhavsar,Masabattula Teja Nikhil,Sidharth Sharma*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出基于EVPN-VXLAN的可扩展仿真框架，用于研究广域网环境下的地理分布式AI训练，通过ECMP、BFD和队列感知流量分配机制优化通信行为，揭示了分布式训练基础设施的性能特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI模型的快速增长和数据主权要求推动了跨多个数据中心的地理分布式AI训练发展，但这种部署面临同步密集型通信、跨站点数据交换和广域网延迟等系统级挑战，需要有效的基础设施方案和系统化研究方法。

Method: 提出基于EVPN-VXLAN的可扩展仿真框架，结合VXLAN覆盖网络与EVPN跨数据中心连接，使用ContainerLab和FRRouting实现，引入ECMP路由、BFD以及队列对感知的流量分配机制，在保持商用基础设施兼容性的同时优化同步密集型AI工作负载的通信行为。

Result: 通过现实WAN仿真，表征了采用AllReduce和参数服务器通信模式的分布式训练工作负载下的通信和系统行为，提供了地理分布式AI环境中流量分布、弹性和基础设施行为的深入洞察。

Conclusion: 研究证明了可复现的多数据中心基础设施框架在可扩展分布式AI训练方面的潜力，为地理分布式AI环境的通信优化和系统设计提供了有价值的指导。

Abstract: The rapid growth of AI models and increasing data sovereignty requirements are driving the transition toward geo-distributed AI training across multiple data centers. Such deployments introduce system-level challenges arising from synchronization-intensive communication, cross-site data exchange, and wide-area latency constraints. This paper investigates EVPN--VXLAN as an infrastructure foundation for geo-distributed AI training environments and presents a scalable emulation framework for systematically studying distributed AI workloads under realistic wide-area conditions. The proposed framework combines VXLAN overlays with EVPN-based inter-data-center connectivity and is implemented using ContainerLab and FRRouting (FRR). The framework further incorporates Equal-Cost Multi-Path (ECMP) routing, Bidirectional Forwarding Detection (BFD), and a queue-pair-aware traffic distribution mechanism designed to improve communication behavior for synchronization-intensive AI workloads while preserving compatibility with commodity infrastructure. Using realistic WAN emulation, we characterize communication and system behavior under distributed training workloads employing AllReduce and Parameter Server communication patterns. Results provide insights into traffic distribution, resilience, and infrastructure behavior in geo-distributed AI environments, highlighting the potential of reproducible multi-data-center infrastructure frameworks for scalable distributed AI training.

</details>


### [223] [Modular Multi-Domain Digital Twin Architecture: Sustainable Intent-Driven 6G Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13069)
*Berk Buzcu,Marcin Pakula,Gevher Yesevi Keskin,Laura Finarelli,Gianluca Rizzo,Engin Zeydan,Jorge Baranda,Aitor Alcazar-Fernandez,Javier Velazquez-Martinez,Luis M. Contreras,Gil Kedar,Efi Dvir,Paweł Kryszkiewicz*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个支持数字孪生的6G架构，将网络数字孪生（NDT）能力作为多域编排框架中的专门服务域，通过绿色网络用例验证，联合覆盖和可再生能源优化使每日电网消耗减少28.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未来6G网络将在分布式异构域基础设施上运行，传统单域管理不足以实现主动、可信的自动化；而端到端单体数字孪生由于可扩展性、保真度和跨域协调挑战而不切实际。

Method: 提出一个数字孪生使能的6G架构，将NDT能力作为基于服务的6G架构中多域编排框架的专门服务域；DT编排器解释预测性和规范性what-if查询并按需组合域特定DT模块和模拟器，决策权保留在请求实体；设计了涵盖遥测同步、基于仿真的决策支持和闭环执行的通用工作流。

Result: 通过波兹南105个基站部署的绿色网络用例验证，联合覆盖和可再生能源优化使每日电网消耗减少28.5%（配置32块太阳能电池板达到收益递减阈值），识别出17个基站同时具有覆盖活跃性和高优先级太阳能候选特征。

Conclusion: 跨域NDT协调能够实现可持续、意图驱动的6G网络管理，证明了所提架构在多域异构环境中的有效性和实用价值。

Abstract: Future 6G networks will operate across distributed and heterogeneous domain infrastructures, making conventional single-domain management insufficient for proactive, trustworthy automation. Network Digital Twins (NDTs) enable what-if analysis, AI-assisted optimization, and risk-free validation of control actions before deployment, yet monolithic end-to-end twins remain impractical due to scalability, fidelity, and cross-domain coordination challenges. Accordingly, this paper proposes a Digital Twin-enabled 6G architecture that exposes NDT capabilities as a specialized service domain within a multi-domain orchestration framework built on a state-of-the-art service-based 6G architecture. A DT Orchestrator interprets \textit{predictive} and \textit{prescriptive} what-if queries and composes domain-specific DT modules and simulators on demand, while decision authority remains with the requesting entity. Furthermore, a generalized workflow covers telemetry synchronization, simulation-based decision support, and closed-loop execution. The framework is demonstrated through a green-networking use case that couples a system-level O-RAN cellular digital twin component with a two-stage solar-allocation simulator, evaluated over a 105-base-station deployment in Poznan using simulative datasets. Joint coverage and renewable optimization reduces daily grid consumption by 28.5\% with 32 solar panels at the diminishing-returns threshold, with 17 base stations identified as both coverage-active and high-priority solar candidates as evidence that cross-domain NDT coordination enables sustainable, intent-driven 6G network management.

</details>


### [224] [Revolutionizing Wireless Communications with Space Data Centers: Applications and Open Challenges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13086)
*Minghao Sun,Zehui Chen,Jinbo Hou,Kezhi Wang,Xiaoli Chu*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文研究了空间数据中心(SDCs)如何将空间通信范式从以连接为导向的数据传输转变为以任务为导向和服务为中心的信息交换，提出了分层SDC网络架构，并通过仿真验证了其在降低控制层延迟方面的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统卫星主要作为中继节点或轻量级星载处理器，而空间数据中心(SDCs)集成了通信、计算、存储和控制能力，能够为数据密集型和智能驱动的空间应用提供持续服务支持，因此需要研究SDCs如何变革空间通信范式。

Method: 提出由接入层、中继层、计算层和控制层组成的分层SDC网络架构，概述可能的部署策略，探索典型的未来应用场景，并通过仿真实验验证SDCs在分层空间网络中的性能表现。

Result: 仿真结果证明了SDCs在降低分层空间网络控制层延迟方面的有效性，能够有效支持任务导向和服务为中心的信息交换。

Conclusion: 本文识别了SDCs实际部署的关键研究方向，为未来空间AI产业的发展提供了新的轨道计算基础设施思路，推动了空间通信范式从连接导向向服务导向的转变。

Abstract: Space data centers (SDCs) are emerging as a promising orbital computing infrastructure for the future AI industry. Unlike conventional satellites that mainly serve as relay nodes or lightweight onboard processors, SDCs integrate communication, computing, storage, and control capabilities in orbit, enabling persistent service support for data-intensive and intelligence-driven space applications. In this article, we investigate how SDCs may transform space communication paradigms from connectivity-oriented data transmission toward task-oriented and service-centric information exchange. We first present a hierarchical SDC network architecture consisting of access, relay, computing, and control layers, and outline possible deployment strategies. We then explore representative future application scenarios enabled by SDCs, highlighting their communication characteristics and associated research challenges. Simulation results further demonstrate the effectiveness of SDCs in reducing control-layer latency in hierarchical space networks. Finally, we identify key research directions toward the practical deployment of SDCs.

</details>


### [225] [Feasibility Assessment of Remote Driving via Latency Analysis of ITS-G5 and Cellular Networks in the MASA Living Lab](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13292)
*Gaetano Orazio Cauchi,Antonio Solida,Salvatore Iandolo,Marco Savarese,Martin Klapez,Enrico Rossini,Marcello Pietri,Marco Picone,Marco Mamei,Maurizio Casoni,Carlo Augusto Grazia*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文在真实城市级测试台中对比分析了ITS-G5与5G网络在远程驾驶场景下的延迟性能，为混合网络策略的部署提供了实证依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 远程驾驶是实现网联自动驾驶的关键技术，但其实际部署严重依赖于无线网络提供低且可预测的延迟保障，目前缺乏在真实城市环境中对不同通信技术延迟特性的系统性评估。

Method: 在Modena Automotive Smart Area (MASA)城市级真实测试台中，通过控制实验在不同网络负载和交通条件下，测量ITS-G5和蜂窝网络(5G)的网络延迟及端到端延迟组件，包括上行视频流传输和下行控制命令传输。

Result: 实验测量揭示了两种技术在延迟及其变异性方面的差异，以及基础设施覆盖对视频流传输性能的影响；基于观测到的延迟分布和可靠性指标，评估了混合网络环境下远程驾驶的可行性和安全边际。

Conclusion: 研究结果为未来远程操作部署提供了可操作的见解，并提出了结合ITS-G5和蜂窝网络优势的混合通信策略。

Abstract: Remote driving has gained increasing attention as a key enabler for connected and automated vehicles. Yet its practical deployment hinges on wireless networks' ability to guarantee low, predictable latency. In this paper, we present an extensive latency analysis of ITS-G5 and cellular (5G) technologies within the Modena Automotive Smart Area (MASA), a real-world, city-scale testbed equipped with a distributed intelligent transportation infrastructure. By conducting controlled experiments under varying network loads and traffic conditions, we measure network and end-to-end latency components relevant to remote driving, in which the uplink consists of a continuous video stream transmitted from the vehicle to the remote operator, and the downlink conveys control commands back to the car. Measurements conducted under diverse conditions reveal how latency and variability differ across the two technologies and how infrastructure coverage impacts video-stream transmission performance. Based on the observed latency distributions and reliability metrics, we assess the practical feasibility and safety margins of remote driving in mixed network environments. The results provide actionable insights for future teleoperation deployments and motivate hybrid communication strategies that combine the strengths of ITS-G5 and cellular networks.

</details>


### [226] [Measurement-Based Performance Evaluation of SmartRSUs with Heterogeneous Antenna Architectures for V2X Communications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13334)
*Marco Savarese,Gaetano Orazio Cauchi,Salvatore Iandolo,Antonio Solida Martin Klapez,Maurizio Casoni,Micaela Verucchi,Enrico Vincenzi,Ignacio Sanudo Olmedo,Marko Bertogna,Carlo Augusto Grazia*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文对两种不同天线架构的定制智能路侧单元（SmartRSU）进行了实测性能评估，通过对比集成天线与外置天线方案，揭示了天线设计与部署对V2X通信可靠性和时延的影响，为下一代智能交通系统基础设施建设提供了实践指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着车联网（V2X）技术在智能交通系统（C-ITS）中的广泛应用，路侧单元（RSU）的性能直接影响通信可靠性。不同天线架构（集成式vs外置式）在实际部署中的性能差异尚缺乏客观的实测评估，需要通过受控实验为SmartRSU的设计优化提供依据。

Method: 构建两种定制SmartRSU原型：方案一采用集成GNSS与通信天线的一体化车顶模块；方案二采用外置双ITS-G5（IEEE 802.11p，5.9GHz）天线与专用GNSS天线。两者均基于适配基础设施部署的专有OBU平台。在同一基础设施上共址部署商用现货V2X RSU作为基准参考，在相同环境条件下进行控制实验。评估指标包括覆盖范围、RSSI、丢包率及端到端时延（OBU到基础设施及反向）。

Result: 实验结果表明，天线设计与部署位置对通信可靠性和时延有显著影响。集成天线方案与外置天线方案在实际部署场景中存在性能权衡：集成方案结构紧凑但性能可能受限，外置方案可提供更优的信号质量但安装复杂度更高。通过共址基准对比验证了两种方案相较于商用RSU的性能特征。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了集成与外置天线配置在真实部署场景中的权衡关系，为下一代智能交通系统（C-ITS）中SmartRSU的设计与优化提供了实用的工程指导，有助于提升V2X通信系统的可靠性与效率。

Abstract: This paper presents a measurement-based performance evaluation of two custom Smart Roadside Units (SmartRSUs) featuring different V2X antenna architectures. The first configuration integrates GNSS and communication antennas into an all-in-one rooftop module, whereas the second uses external dual ITS-G5 (IEEE 802.11p) antennas operating at 5.9~GHz and a dedicated GNSS antenna. Both systems are built upon a proprietary On-Board Unit (OBU) platform adapted for infrastructure deployment.
  The experimental campaign evaluates key V2X communication metrics, including coverage, received signal strength indicator (RSSI), packet loss, and end-to-end latency in both transmission (OBU-to-infrastructure) and reception (infrastructure-to-OBU) directions. To ensure objective validation, a commercial off-the-shelf V2X Roadside Unit is co-located on the same infrastructure and used as a performance benchmark, providing ground-truth reference measurements under identical environmental conditions through a controlled co-located deployment.
  Results highlight the impact of antenna design and placement on communication reliability and latency, revealing trade-offs between integrated and external antenna configurations in real-world deployment scenarios. The findings provide practical insights for the design and optimization of next-generation SmartRSUs in cooperative intelligent transportation systems (C-ITS).

</details>


### [227] [Experimental Insights into UDP-Based Video and Control Traffic over IEEE 802.11p ITS-G5](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13390)
*Antonio Solida,Gaetano Orazio Cauchi,Salvatore Iandolo,Marco Savarese,Martin Klapez,Maurizio Casoni,Carlo Augusto Grazia*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文通过实验评估了IEEE 802.11p ITS-G5测试床上UDP传输的性能，发现调制方案是低负载时延时的主导因素，而高阶调制可显著降低时延和变异性，IPv6多播比IPv4单播表现出更大的时延离散度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 车辆应用（如协作驾驶、遥操作和实时感知）日益依赖低延迟无线通信。ITS-G5（基于IEEE 802.11p）是实现车对车和车对基础设施直接通信的关键技术，但在现实条件下针对基于UDP流量的实验性能研究仍然有限。

Method: 在由树莓派车载单元和商用路侧单元组成的IEEE 802.11p ITS-G5测试床上进行实验评估UDP传输。研究内容包括：不同调制编码方案(MCS)的影响、两种网络层配置（IPv4单播和IPv6多播）、CAKE主动队列管理的使用。评估方法包括使用iPerf生成合成流量以及使用MPEG-TS over UDP进行实时视频流传输，以模拟延迟敏感的车辆应用。

Result: 调制方案是低流量负载时影响延迟的主导因素，而传输模式和IP版本的选择在拥塞条件下变得日益重要。高阶调制显著降低了延迟和变异性，IPv6多播比IPv4单播表现出更大的延迟离散度。此外，主动队列管理似乎并未改善延迟的可预测性。

Conclusion: 这些发现为配置支持延迟敏感车辆服务的ITS-G5网络提供了实用见解，有助于优化车联网系统的性能配置。

Abstract: Vehicular applications such as cooperative driving, teleoperation, and real-time perception increasingly rely on low-latency wireless communication. In this context, ITS-G5, based on IEEE 802.11p, represents a key technology for enabling direct vehicle-to-vehicle and vehicle-to-infrastructure communication. Despite its relevance, experimental studies focusing on the performance of UDP-based traffic over IEEE 802.11p under realistic conditions remain limited.
  This paper presents an experimental evaluation of UDP transmission over an IEEE 802.11p ITS-G5 testbed composed of Raspberry Pi-based onboard units and commercial roadside units. The analysis investigates the impact of different modulation and coding schemes (MCS). It also evaluates two network-layer configurations (IPv4 unicast and IPv6 multicast) and the use of CAKE for active queue management. In addition to synthetic traffic generated with iPerf, the evaluation includes real-time video streaming using MPEG-TS over UDP to emulate latency-sensitive vehicular applications. Results show that the modulation scheme is the dominant factor influencing latency at low traffic loads, while the choice of transmission mode and IP version becomes increasingly significant under congested conditions. Higher-order modulations significantly reduce latency and variability, whereas IPv6 multicast exhibits greater delay dispersion than IPv4 unicast. Furthermore, active queue management does not seem to improve delay predictability. These findings provide practical insights for configuring ITS-G5 networks supporting latency-sensitive vehicular services.

</details>


### [228] [From Traditional Automation to Embodied Wireless Intelligence: Vision-Language-Action Empowered Physics-Aware Communication Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13458)
*Genze Jiang,Kezhi Wang,Xiaomin Chen,Yizhou Huang*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种具身智能赋能基站（eBS）范式，通过视觉-语言-动作（VLA）管道将基站转变为具备环境感知、因果物理推理和物理感知动作生成能力的自主智能体。实验表明，单一VLA管道无需特定任务训练即可实现零样本材料推理、跨视角泛化和信号退化前的动态事件预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的无线网络自动化系统（从基于规则的自组织网络到数据驱动优化）本质上仍是离身系统，仅优化性能指标而缺乏对支配无线电传播的物理环境的感知能力。这种与物理环境的脱节限制了网络优化的效果和智能化水平。

Method: 提出具身智能赋能基站（eBS）范式，采用视觉-语言-动作（VLA）管道。系统采用双层异步架构：(1)语义规划器：由前沿视觉-语言模型（VLM）驱动，在人类时间尺度上生成结构化动作指令；(2)战术控制器：执行实时自适应操作。该架构使基站能够进行情境感知、因果物理推理和物理感知的动作生成。

Result: 案例研究表明，单一VLA管道在无需任务特定训练的情况下能够：(1)执行零样本材料推理；(2)实现跨视角泛化；(3)在信号退化发生前预测动态事件。这证明了从传统的遵循规则的网络自动化向具身智能赋能的未来无线网络的范式转变。

Conclusion: eBS范式代表了无线网络自动化从传统的规则驱动模式向具身智能的重大转变，通过赋予基站环境感知和物理推理能力，为未来无线网络的智能化发展提供了新的方向，展示了具身智能在无线网络领域的巨大潜力。

Abstract: Wireless network automation has progressed from rule-based self-organising networks (SON) to data-driven optimisation, yet existing systems remain fundamentally disembodied, optimising performance indicators without perceiving the physical environment that governs radio propagation. We propose the embodied intelligent empowered base station (eBS), a paradigm that adopts a Vision-Language-Action (VLA) pipeline to transform base stations into autonomous agents capable of situated perception, causal physical reasoning, and physics-aware action generation. The eBS employs a two-tier asynchronous architecture: a Semantic Planner powered by a frontier Vision-Language Model (VLM) generates structured action directives on human timescales, whilst a Tactical Controller executes real-time adaptation. Case studies demonstrate that a single VLA pipeline, without task-specific training, can perform zero-shot material reasoning, generalise across viewpoints, and predict dynamic events before signal degradation occurs, illustrating a paradigm shift from traditional rule-following network automation to embodied intelligence empowered future wireless networks.

</details>


### [229] [Graphical Causal Reasoning for Root Cause Analysis in Cloud Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13532)
*Fabien Chraim,Dominik Janzing,John Evans*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于图因果发现技术的云网络事件根因分析方法，通过时空分组和自动化本体降低问题维度，使用二元时间序列数据构建因果图，并引入概率推理方法实现可解释的根因评分。在35个生产事件的标记数据集上评估，85.7%的事件成功召回正确根因，74.3%实现精确匹配，已在生产环境中应用于800多个实际事件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云计算依赖大规模网络，这些网络本质上是复杂系统。传统的基于规则的自动化方法在根因分析方面存在局限性，需要一种能够处理动态大规模运营环境中云网络事件根因分析的新方法。

Method: 采用基于图的因果发现技术，引入时空分组策略和自动化本体降低问题维度；使用二元时间序列数据，通过双变量格兰杰因果检验和条件独立性检验构建因果图；提出一种概率推理方法，将边缘特定条件概率分配为时间滞后的函数，通过因果图遍历实现可解释的时间感知根因评分。

Result: 使用来自主要云提供商的35个生产事件的标记数据集进行评估，模型在85.7%的事件中成功召回正确根因，74.3%产生精确匹配。在生产环境中，该系统已用于800多个实际事件，并获得了网络工程师的积极定性反馈。

Conclusion: 研究结果突显了数据驱动的因果方法在动态大规模运营环境中进行根因分析的实用性，证明了该方法在真实生产环境中的有效性和实用价值。

Abstract: Cloud-computing relies on large-scale networks which are inherently complex systems. In this paper, we present a novel approach to root cause analysis (RCA) of cloud network incidents, leveraging graph-based causal discovery techniques. Our method addresses the limitations of rule-based automation by introducing a spatiotemporal grouping strategy and an automation ontology to reduce the dimensionality of the problem. We construct a causal graph from binary time series data using bivariate Granger causality and conditional independence tests. For inference, we introduce a probabilistic method that assigns edge-specific conditional probabilities as a function of time lag, allowing for interpretable, time-aware root cause scoring via causal graph traversal.
  We evaluated the system using a labeled dataset of 35 production incidents from a major cloud provider. The model successfully recalled the correct root cause in 85.7% of incidents and produced an exact match in 74.3%. In production, the deployed system has been used in over 800 real-world incidents, with positive qualitative feedback from network engineers. These results highlight the practicality of a data-driven, causal approach to RCA in dynamic and large-scale operational environments.

</details>


### [230] [NetCause: Counterfactual Learning for Root Cause Analysis in Large-Scale Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13543)
*Fabien Chraim,Jian Zhang,Dominik Janzing,Xiang Song,Christos Faloutsos,John Evans*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: NetCause是一个自监督学习框架，通过图-时序建模和反事实仿真来提升大规模网络故障根因归因的准确性，在生产环境中相比基于规则的基线方法提升了16.1%的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的根因分析技术依赖静态规则、相关性启发式或拓扑局部推理，难以在故障通过复杂物理和逻辑依赖传播的动态环境中进行泛化，无法有效捕捉故障在大规模网络中的传播机制。

Method: 将网络事件建模为图-时序过程（graph-temporal processes），利用反事实仿真（counterfactual simulation）对候选根因进行排序，生成可解释的根因假设排名，并与运维人员定义的缓解和修复操作自然集成。

Result: 在某领先云服务商的生产网络上，基于6个月内收集的1500+事件训练模型，并在31个专家标注的事件上评估，NetCause在运维决策最相关的场景下相比规则启发式基线实现了16.1%的准确率提升；推理轻量级，每个事件仅需数秒GPU运行时间。

Conclusion: NetCause证明了学习模型能够有效捕捉故障在大规模网络中的传播规律，并通过反事实仿真实现因果归因，在实际生产环境中显著提升了根因分析质量，同时保持了推理效率。

Abstract: Can a learned model capture how faults propagate through a large-scale network and use this knowledge to causally attribute customer impact to its underlying root cause? Existing root cause analysis techniques often rely on static rules, correlation heuristics, or topology-local reasoning, which struggle to generalize in dynamic environments where faults propagate across complex physical and logical dependencies.
  We present NetCause, a self-supervised learning-based framework that models network incidents as graph-temporal processes and uses counterfactual simulation to rank candidate root causes. This approach produces an interpretable ranking of root cause hypotheses and integrates naturally with operator-defined mitigation and remediation actions.
  We train the model on over 1,500 incidents collected over six months from a leading cloud provider's production network and evaluate it on 31 expert-labeled incidents. NetCause consistently improves root cause ranking quality in the regime most relevant to operational decision-making, achieving a 16.1% accuracy improvement over a rule-based heuristic baseline. While training is computationally intensive, inference is lightweight, requiring only seconds of GPU runtime per incident (well below typical telemetry collection latencies).

</details>


<div id='cs.AR'></div>

# cs.AR [[Back]](#toc)

### [231] [Non-Parametric Dual-Manifold Mapping via 8-Bit Bounded Transformation Matrices: Challenging FP-centric Hardware Paradigms in Low-Energy AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13328)
*Lars Kopp*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需浮点运算的8位整数训练框架，通过双流形映射和位运算实现神经网络推理与学习，在极端稀疏和节点破坏下仍保持强鲁棒性，挑战了传统浮点GPU加速器的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代深度学习硬件严重依赖计算昂贵的浮点运算（FP32/FP16/FP8），需要巨大的热和能量开销来维持基于梯度的优化，作者旨在打破这一范式限制。

Method: 提出非参数化、免训练的双流形映射计算框架，严格在8位有符号整数边界内运行：构建空间流形（8192神经元）和Gabor池化结构流形（4096神经元），通过整数变换矩阵（Z矩阵）消除浮点乘法器；推理采用缓存友好的指针偏移和位掩码，通过固定阈值累积方向符号电荷；学习通过[-127,127]范围内的局部有界更新机制，结合随机噪声注入实现。

Result: 两种架构均展现极端全息韧性：在90%截断稀疏度和20%随机节点破坏下，通过全局缩放因子仍能保持近乎完美的重建效果；成功将核心AI推理简化为8位边界和类布尔运算执行。

Conclusion: 该框架将核心AI推理降至8位边界，为神经形态边缘计算指明范式转变方向，直接质疑了密集浮点中心GPU加速器的长期必要性。

Abstract: Modern deep learning hardware paradigms rely heavily on computationally expensive floating-point arithmetic (FP32, FP16, and FP8), requiring massive thermal and energetic overheads to maintain gradient-based optimization. This paper introduces a non-parametric, training-free computational framework for dual-manifold mapping that operates strictly within an 8-bit signed integer boundary and leverages simple bitwise and accumulation logic. By mapping a Spatial Manifold (N_spatial = 8192 neurons) and a Gabor-pooled Structural Manifold (N_structural = 4096 neurons) through an integer-based transformation matrix (Z-matrix), we eliminate the need for floating-point multipliers. Inference is achieved via cache-friendly pointer offsets and bitwise masks, accumulating directional sign-charges using fixed thresholds (theta_reject = 8.0, theta_cut = 2.0). Learning is executed through a localized, bounded update mechanism restricted strictly within [-127, 127], modulated by stochastic noise injection. Both architectures demonstrate extreme holographic resilience, preserving near-perfect reconstruction via a global scaling factor under 90% truncation sparsity and 20% random node destruction. By reducing core AI inference to 8-bit boundaries and boolean-like execution, this framework outlines a paradigm shift toward neuromorphic edge-computing, directly questioning the long-term necessity of dense, floating-point-centric GPU accelerators.

</details>


### [232] [SupraSNN: Exploiting Synapse-Level Parallelism in Spiking Neural Network Accelerators through Co-Optimized Mapping and Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13354)
*Seyed Sadra Ghavami,Mohammad Hossein Nikkhah,Mohammad Rasoul Roshanshah,Saeed Safari*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 受超标量处理器架构启发，提出SupraSNN硬件-软件协同设计框架，通过解耦突触和神经元计算实现高突触级并行性，在FPGA上实现显著降低的延迟和提升的能效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脉冲神经网络(SNNs)提供了受大脑启发的高效计算路径，但在物理硬件上管理和执行其大规模并行性面临挑战。这一问题类似于处理器设计中从串行执行突破的历史挑战，而超标量架构通过将多条指令分派到并行功能单元成功解决了这一障碍。作者受此启发，旨在解决SNN在硬件部署中的并行性管理问题。

Method: 提出SupraSNN架构，将突触事件视为可并行化的微操作。核心设计包括：1）物理上解耦突触和神经元计算；2）多播树将脉冲数据路由到多个并行突触处理单元作为计算流水线；3）合并树整合分布式结果供统一神经元单元处理，集中化复杂神经元状态动态以减少硬件开销和资源重复；4）分区和调度框架：首先在满足内存约束下将SNN映射到硬件，然后通过启发式调度确定突触执行顺序，最大化吞吐量和资源利用率。

Result: 在前向SNN(MNIST数据集，93.44%准确率)上，SupraSNN在Xilinx Zynq XC7Z020 FPGA上实现149μs推理延迟和每张图像0.025mJ能耗（每突触0.276nJ），相比现有基于FPGA的SNN加速器延迟降低47.6%，能效提升5.6倍。在循环SNN(Spiking Heidelberg数据集，71.82%准确率)上，在XC7Z030上实现1.41ms延迟和每样本0.77mJ能耗。

Conclusion: SupraSNN成功将超标量架构理念应用于SNN加速，通过硬件-软件协同设计实现了突触级高并行性，在保持准确性的同时显著提升了推理速度和能效，为SNN的实际部署提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Spiking Neural Networks (SNNs) offer a brain-inspired path toward highly efficient computation, but their practical deployment is constrained by the challenge of managing and executing their massive parallelism on physical hardware. This problem mirrors the historical challenge in processor design of moving beyond serial execution, a barrier broken by superscalar architectures that dispatch multiple instructions to parallel functional units. Drawing inspiration from this paradigm, we introduce a hardware-software co-design framework that treats synaptic events as parallelizable micro-operations. We present SupraSNN, a superscalar-inspired architecture that achieves high synapse-level parallelism by physically decoupling synaptic and neuronal computations. Within this architecture, a Multi-Cast Tree routes spike data to multiple parallel Synapse Processing Units serve as the computational pipelines, while a Merge Tree consolidates distributed results for processing by a unified Neuron Unit--deliberately centralizing complex neuron state dynamics to mitigate hardware overhead and resource duplication. The efficacy of this architecture is enabled by a sophisticated partitioning and scheduling framework that first maps the SNN onto hardware respecting memory constraints, then heuristic scheduling determines the synaptic execution order, maximizing throughput and resource utilization. Implementing a feedforward SNN trained on MNIST (93.44% accuracy), SupraSNN achieves 149 $μs$ inference latency and 0.025 mJ per image (0.276 nJ per synapse) on the Xilinx Zynq XC7Z020 FPGA--delivering 47.6% lower latency and 5.6$\times$ better energy efficiency than prior FPGA-based SNN accelerators. Beyond vision tasks, a recurrent SNN on the Spiking Heidelberg Dataset (71.82% accuracy) achieves 1.41 ms latency and 0.77 mJ per sample on XC7Z030.

</details>


### [233] [ReSCom: A Reconfigurable Spiking Neural Network Accelerator Using Stochastic Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13560)
*Ali Alipour Fereidani,Mohammad Rasoul Roshanshah,Saeed Safari*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出ReSCom，一种基于随机计算的可重构SNN加速器，在Xilinx Artix-7 FPGA上实现MNIST推理92.80%准确率，每次推理仅需0.05 mJ能量，显著提升能效并支持精度-延迟-能耗的动态权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脉冲神经网络(SNN)因其事件驱动计算和生物启发动力学特性，为能效推理提供了有吸引力的框架。然而，SNN的高效硬件实现仍面临挑战：神经元计算产生显著的功耗和面积开销，且在精度管理不当时，不可控的近似算术运算会破坏循环状态更新的稳定性。

Method: 提出ReSCom可重构SNN加速器，采用随机计算降低硬件复杂度：对神经元动力学中的乘法运算使用随机算术，同时保持精确的定点加减法运算；设计统一的可重构神经元结构，支持积分发放(IF)、漏积分发放(LIF)和突触神经元三种模型；通过调节随机比特流长度实现精度、延迟和能耗的运行时动态权衡。

Result: 在Xilinx Artix-7 FPGA上实现MNIST推理，达到92.80%分类准确率；在100 MHz频率下，每张图像仅消耗0.05 mJ运行能量，能效超越近期最先进实现方案；通过管理随机比特流长度可实现对精度-延迟-能耗权衡的显式动态控制。

Conclusion: ReSCom成功解决了SNN硬件实现中的功耗、面积和数值稳定性挑战，通过随机计算策略在保持推理稳定性的同时显著降低硬件复杂度，为面向目标应用约束的能效SNN硬件加速器设计提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Spiking Neural Networks (SNNs) provide an attractive framework for energy-efficient inference due to their event-driven computation and biologically inspired dynamics. However, efficient hardware realization of SNNs remains challenging because neuronal computations incur significant power and area costs, and uncontrolled approximate arithmetic can destabilize recurrent state updates when precision is not properly managed. To address these challenges, this paper presents ReSCom, a reconfigurable SNN accelerator that leverages stochastic computing to reduce hardware complexity while maintaining stable inference. The proposed architecture employs stochastic arithmetic for multiplication operations in neuron dynamics, while preserving exact fixed-point addition/subtraction operations. This stochastic strategy enables runtime trade-offs between accuracy, latency, and energy consumption. A unified reconfigurable neuron design supports Integrate-and-Fire (IF), Leaky Integrate-and-Fire (LIF), and Synaptic neuron models within a single hardware framework. Experimental results for MNIST inference on a Xilinx Artix-7 FPGA show that ReSCom achieves $92.80\%$ classification accuracy while consuming just $0.05~\mathrm{mJ}$ of operational energy per image at $100~\mathrm{MHz}$, outperforming the energy efficiency of recent state-of-the-art implementations. Furthermore, managing the stochastic bit-stream length allows explicit, dynamic control over accuracy-latency-energy trade-offs to meet target application constraints.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [234] [SAIGuard: Communication-State Simulation for Proactive Defense of LLM Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12474)
*Ruxue Shi,Yili Wang,Mengnan Du,Qinggang Zhang,Rui Miao,Yixin Liu,Xin Wang*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出SAIGuard主动防御框架，通过通信状态模拟在消息传播前检测并修复可疑消息，有效降低LLM多智能体系统的攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的多智能体系统通过通信协作解决复杂任务，但其通信驱动的特性使安全风险能够在智能体间传播并引发系统级故障。现有防御主要采用执行后检测和隔离有害智能体的反应式范式，可能导致不可逆损害并降低协作效用。

Method: 提出仿真感知拦截卫士（SAIGuard）主动防御框架：1）在MAS交互图上执行通信状态模拟；2）估计传入消息对局部智能体状态和全局MAS状态的影响；3）通过良性通信模式的重建偏差检测风险消息；4）在可疑消息传播进入系统前对其进行净化或重新生成，而非隔离智能体。

Result: 在多种拓扑结构和攻击场景下的实验表明，SAIGuard在保持MAS效用的同时降低了攻击成功率，性能优于反应式防御方法。

Conclusion: SAIGuard证明了主动防御范式在MAS安全中的有效性，能够在不影响系统协作效用的前提下，通过消息级的预先检测和修复来防止安全风险传播，为MAS安全防护提供了新的解决思路。

Abstract: LLM-based multi-agent systems (MAS) solve complex tasks through inter-agent collaboration, but their communication-driven nature also allows security risks to spread across agents and trigger system-wide failures. Existing MAS defenses mainly follow a reactive paradigm after execution by detecting and isolating harmful agents, which may cause irreversible damage and degrade collaborative utility. To address this, we propose a proactive defense framework for MAS security, namely a Simulation-aware Interception Guard (SAIGuard). SAIGuard performs communication-state simulation over the MAS interaction graph, estimates the impact of incoming messages on local agent states and the global MAS state, and detects risky messages via reconstruction deviations from benign communication patterns. Instead of isolating agents, SAIGuard sanitizes or regenerates suspicious messages before it propagation into system. Experiments across diverse topologies and attack scenarios show that SAIGuard reduces attack success rates while maintaining MAS utility, outperforming reactive defenses.

</details>


### [235] [Smarter Saboteurs, Better Fixers: Scaling & Security in Linear Multi-Agent Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12709)
*Timothy McAllister,Sina Abdidizaji,Ivan Garibay,Ozlem Ozmen Garibay*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 研究发现在线性多智能体系统中，大模型更易执行恶意指令，但通过添加轻量级修复阶段可显著提升系统韧性，使线性拓扑结构在对抗攻击下变得可行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着基于大语言模型的多智能体系统（MAS）在实际场景中部署，其协作结构对抗恶意攻击的韧性成为关键安全问题。攻击者可能利用提示注入或越狱技术破坏MAS工作流中的单个智能体，但模型规模与系统级韧性之间的相互作用机制尚不明确。

Method: 在HumanEval基准上，对两个开源模型家族在不同规模下的线性多智能体工作流进行实验。通过对比控制组与恶意指令组的性能差异，研究模型规模对安全性的影响，并设计轻量级终端修复（Fixer）阶段进行验证。

Result: 发现了一种合规-纠正对称性：较大规模的模型更倾向于忠实执行恶意指令，在未经纠正的工作流中，27B模型从控制组到恶意组的性能下降达53.7个百分点。然而，添加轻量级终端修复阶段后，性能下降骤降至0.6个百分点，并恢复到与控制水平相当的统计一致性。

Conclusion: 严格线性的协作结构在适当规模下可以对抗对手具有可行性和韧性，表明先前归因于线性拓扑的脆弱性可能源于缺乏纠正机制，而非线性结构本身的固有缺陷。

Abstract: As LLM-based multi-agent systems (MAS) are deployed in the wild, the resilience of their collaboration structures against adversarial compromise becomes a critical safety concern. Attackers may leverage prompt-injection or jailbreaking to sabotage individual agents within MAS workflows, but the interaction between model scaling and system-level resilience remains poorly understood. This paper investigates how model scale affects the security of linear multi-agent workflows. Our experiments across scales of two open-weight model families on the HumanEval benchmark reveal a compliance-correction symmetry: larger models are far more likely to faithfully execute malicious instructions, with the control-to-malicious performance drop reaching 53.7pp at 27B in uncorrected pipelines. However, appending a lightweight terminal Fixer stage collapses this to 0.6pp and restores statistical parity with control-level performance, demonstrating that strictly linear collaboration structures can be viable and resilient to adversaries at this scale, and suggesting that the brittleness previously attributed to linear topology may stem from a lack of correction.

</details>


### [236] [The Internet of Agentic AI: Communication, Coordination, and Collective Intelligence at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12835)
*Quanyan Zhu*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了智能体AI互联网(IoAI)的愿景框架，构建了一个异构智能体在分布式环境中进行发现、协商、协作和执行工作流的开发生态系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主AI智能体的快速涌现正在将人工智能从孤立的模型推理转变为包含推理、通信和行动的分布式系统，亟需建立一个开放的生态系统来支持异构智能体之间的协作与交互。

Method: 综合单智能体AI、多智能体系统、分布式计算、通信网络、博弈论和安全工程等理论基础，分析智能体部署模型、工作流生命周期、通信协议、互操作性层、资源管理挑战和信任架构，并通过自适应制造和分布式运营协调案例进行验证。

Result: 建立了一个全面的IoAI框架，揭示了大规模自主智能体网络面临的核心研究挑战：受控涌现、语义互操作性、安全身份、激励兼容协调、资源感知编排和治理机制。

Conclusion: 智能体AI互联网的实现需要解决架构设计和机制构建的关键问题，为未来大规模自主智能体网络的部署和管理提供了理论框架和研究方向。

Abstract: The rapid emergence of autonomous AI agents is transforming artificial intelligence from isolated model inference into distributed systems of reasoning, communication, and action. This paper develops the vision of the Internet of Agentic AI (IoAI): an open ecosystem in which heterogeneous agents discover one another, negotiate responsibilities, exchange context, invoke tools, and execute workflows across cloud, edge, device, organizational, and cyber-physical environments. We synthesize foundations from single-agent agentic AI, multi-agent systems, distributed computing, communication networks, game theory, and security engineering to characterize the architectures and mechanisms required for scalable agent ecosystems. The paper examines agent deployment models, workflow lifecycles, communication protocols, interoperability layers, resource-management challenges, and trust architectures, with case studies in adaptive manufacturing and distributed operational coordination. The resulting framework highlights the central research challenges of controlled emergence, semantic interoperability, secure identity, incentive-compatible coordination, resource-aware orchestration, and governance for large-scale networks of autonomous agents.

</details>


### [237] [Effects of Social Interactions in Self-Organising Railway Traffic Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13068)
*Fabio Oddi,Federico Naldini,Leo D'Amato,Grégory Marlière,Paola Pellegrini,Vito Trianni*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 研究表明，在自组织交通管理系统中，短时预测邻域视界已足够有效，而长视界反而会损害局部可处理性和计算响应性，且不提升全局调度性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在自组织交通管理系统中，列车需要预测邻域、生成交通计划假设，并通过与邻居的协商达成共识。预测邻域视界作为关键结构参数，决定了社会交互图的大小和密度，同时影响局部子问题的复杂性和分布式共识动态，需要在局部可处理性、计算响应性与全局调度一致性、安全可行性之间进行权衡探索。

Method: 通过闭环仿真框架，系统性地评估不同预测邻域视界取值对分散式协调过程的影响，涵盖从初始冲突检测到分布式调度共识的完整流程。

Result: 实验结果与直觉相反：短时视界已足够满足系统需求，而较长的视界取值会损害局部可处理性和计算响应性，且在全局调度最优性方面没有任何增益。

Conclusion: 该研究为自组织交通管理系统中的预测邻域视界设计提供了重要见解，证明了无需采用长视界来提升系统性能，短视界即可在保证安全关键环境下的全局调度一致性和可行性的同时，维持良好的局部可处理性和计算响应性。

Abstract: Recent research is exploring self-organised traffic management as a solution for scaling to complex real-world networks. In such a system, trains predict their neighbourhood, produce traffic plan hypotheses, and agree via consensus with neighbours on a future traffic plan to be implemented. This paper investigates a structural parameter within this pipeline: the predictive neighbourhood horizon. The horizon is used by trains to identify future potential conflicts with neighbours, and to establish the local interaction topology, that is, the subset of trains to negotiate with. As the primary design variable, the horizon directly determines the size and density of the social interaction graph, whereas its impact on the complexity of local sub-problems and the distributed consensus dynamics represents a trade-off to be explored. Through a closed-loop simulation framework the study evaluates how variations of the horizon impact the overall decentralised coordination process, from initial conflict detection to distributed schedule consensus. The analysis focuses on investigating the potential trade-off introduced by the horizon choice: balancing local tractability and computational responsiveness with the need for global schedule coherence and feasibility in safety-critical environments. Contrary to intuition, our empirical results indicate that the short time horizons suffice, while long values compromise local tractability and computational responsiveness with no gain in global schedule optimality.

</details>


### [238] [$α$-fair heterogeneous agent reinforcement learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13076)
*Yao-hua Franck Xu,Tayeb Lemlouma,Jean-Marie Bonnin,Arnaud Braud*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出结合α-公平性与异构智能体信任区域学习的框架，解决多智能体合作中的公平性问题，在保持理论保证的同时实现更优的社会福利结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于功利主义目标的多智能体合作优化方法忽视奖励分配公平性，导致"领导者-追随者"不平等动态；而基于公平性的方法往往破坏马尔可夫博弈的平稳性或缺乏理论保证，造成公平目标方法与理论安全学习框架之间的关键差距。

Method: 提出将α-公平性与异构智能体信任区域学习(HATRL)相结合的框架，利用公平优势函数基于智能体预期回报动态加权其效用，使全局目标能够通过参数α从纯粹功利主义效率过渡到α-公平性福利，确保单调改进并向纳什均衡收敛。引入两个实用算法：α-fair HATRPO和α-fair HAPPO。

Result: 在CleanUp和CommonHarvest等序贯社会困境实验中，所提算法从功利主义角度优于HATRL算法，同时实现了更高的社会结果。

Conclusion: 该框架成功弥合了公平目标方法与理论安全学习框架之间的差距，证明了在多智能体合作中同时实现功利主义效率与社会公平性提升的可行性。

Abstract: Cooperation in multi-agent systems is typically optimized through utilitarian objectives that maximize overall efficiency but fail to account for reward distribution, often resulting in inequitable "leader-follower" dynamics. While fairness-based approaches encourage pro-social behaviors where every agent benefits from cooperation, many current algorithms - including those utilizing reward shaping - break the stationarity of Markov Games or lack rigorous theoretical guarantees. This creates a critical gap between fair objective methods and theoretically safe learning frameworks. We propose a novel framework that bridges $α$-fairness with Heterogeneous-Agent Trust Region Learning (HATRL), ensuring monotonic improvement and convergence toward Nash Equilibria. Our approach leverages a fair advantage function that dynamically weights agent utilities based on their expected returns, allowing the global objective to transition from purely utilitarian efficiency to $α$-fairness welfare based on the parameter $α$. We introduce two practical algorithms, $α$-fair HATRPO and $α$-fair HAPPO, and demonstrate through experiments in sequential social dilemmas like CleanUp and CommonHarvest that they perform better than HATRL's algorithms from a utilitarian point of view while achieving socially higher outcomes.

</details>


### [239] [See What I See, Know What I Think: Dense Latent Communication Across Heterogeneous Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13594)
*Siyi Chen,Xiaoyan Zhang,Meng Wu,Jonathan Tremblay,Valts Blukis,Stan Birchfield,Rene Vidal,Alvaro Velasquez,Sijia Liu,Qing Qu*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对异构智能体间KV缓存通信的密集对齐方法，通过轻量级跨模型缓存转换和两阶段训练（重构+生成），实现了比文本通信低2-3倍的计算成本，并在上下文感知和无感知场景下均表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体系统主要通过文本通信，存在解码-重编码的计算开销大且有损的问题；现有基于KV缓存的通信方法大多假设同构模型，回避了跨模型潜在对齐的核心挑战；现有异构方法也具有局限性，通常假设共享输入，且主要用于引导而非真正的"思维传递"。因此需要研究异构智能体能否实现足够好的对齐以进行真正的"思维读取"。

Method: 提出密集对齐方法用于异构KV缓存通信：1）设计轻量级跨模型缓存转换机制；2）采用两阶段训练策略：重构阶段后接生成阶段；3）基于信息结构分析发现：上下文感知传递由稀疏推理信号驱动，而无感知传递需要密集上下文知识保持。

Result: 在{Qwen3-4B, 8B, 14B}三个模型构成的六种传递方向上，以及六个领域内和领域外基准测试中，该方法：1）优于现有异构基线方法；2）在上下文感知设置中达到或超过文本通信性能，计算成本降低约2-3倍；3）在先前方法失效的无上下文感知传递场景中仍保持有效。

Conclusion: 通过轻量级跨模型缓存转换和两阶段训练实现的密集对齐方法，成功解决了异构智能体间KV缓存通信的核心挑战，在多种设置下实现了优于文本通信的性能和效率，为多智能体系统的低成本高效通信提供了新范式。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems communicate mostly through text, paying a lossy and expensive decode and re-encode cost. KV-cache communication is a promising alternative, yet most prior work is homogeneous, using duplicate copies of the same model, and avoids the central challenge of cross-model latent alignment; existing heterogeneous methods are also restrictive, typically assuming shared input and using transferred caches mainly for steering. We study a more fundamental question: can heterogeneous agents be aligned well enough to perform real "mind reading" and transfer both what one agent sees and how it thinks? Our information-structure analysis reveals a duality: context-aware transfer is driven by sparse reasoning signals, while context-unaware transfer, where the receiver sees no input, requires dense contextual knowledge preservation. Motivated by this, we propose dense alignment for heterogeneous KV-cache communication via a lightweight cross-model cache transformation and two-phase training: reconstruction followed by generation. Across all six directions of {Qwen3-4B, 8B, 14B} and six in-domain and out-of-domain benchmarks, our method outperforms prior heterogeneous baselines, matches or exceeds text communication in context-aware settings at roughly 2 to 3 times lower compute, and remains effective in context-unaware transfer where prior methods collapse.

</details>


### [240] [Tuning Agent-Based Predator-Prey Models Toward Lotka-Volterra Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13639)
*Corinna Mandl,Siddharth Chaturvedi,Marcel van Gerven*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文研究了如何通过参数调优，使基于智能体的捕食者-猎物系统产生类似经典Lotka-Volterra循环的种群动态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模基于智能体的模型在模拟复杂适应系统时，存在局部规则和参数众多的问题，微小的参数变化可能导致系统行为失控、种群崩溃或在人工边界处饱和，这给模型调优带来了核心挑战。

Method: 构建了一个连续的捕食者-猎物系统，其中羊和狼作为具有局部感知、内部能量和循环神经网络控制器的主动智能体。采用基于特征的损失函数来优化环境和人口参数，该损失函数奖励持续的振荡、相位滞后、有界种群和长期持续性。优化首先针对随机控制器，然后针对更自然设置下的进化控制器。模型在基于JAX的ABMax框架上实现，支持在硬件加速器上进行高效批量仿真。

Result: 成功实现了环境和人口参数的调优，使系统产生了类似经典Lotka-Volterra循环的种群动态行为。基于特征的损失函数能够有效地引导参数优化过程。ABMax框架实现了在硬件加速器上的高效批量仿真。

Conclusion: 通过合理的参数优化方法，可以使基于智能体的复杂适应系统产生预期的宏观种群动态行为，证明了使用特征驱动损失函数调优大规模基于智能体模型的可行性。

Abstract: Recent growth in compute power has made it increasingly feasible to use large-scale agent-based models to simulate complex adaptive systems. A central difficulty is that such models contain many local rules and parameters, where small changes can lead to runaway behaviour, population collapse, or saturation at artificial bounds. We study this problem in a continuous predator-prey system where sheep and wolves are active agents with local sensing, internal energy, and recurrent neural network-based controllers. We ask whether environmental and demographic parameters can be tuned so that the resulting population dynamics resemble classical Lotka-Volterra cycles. We optimise these parameters with a feature-based loss that rewards sustained oscillations, phase lag, bounded populations, and long-term persistence, first for random controllers and then for evolved controllers in a more naturalistic setting. The model is implemented in ABMax, a JAX-based agent-based modelling framework that enables efficient batched simulation on hardware accelerators.

</details>
